# IMPLEMENTATION LOG — V3.9 Flight-Delay Data Collection

> **Integrated operator edition — final repository-aware implementation manual, Phase 0→7 completeness pass, 2026-09-02.** This is the same current safety-re-audited Implementation Log, reorganized and updated rather than scientifically redesigned. The binding scientific/experimental authority for this audit is the uploaded `V3.9_DataCollectPlan_f.8(4).md` §§0–21 (V3.9-f.8). This Log tells the human operator and repository-capable AI how to implement, verify, execute, stop, and record that Plan. It cannot override the Plan.

## How to use this Log

**Read every working session:** §0 current truth → §1 complete Phase 0→7 implementation manual → §2 exact Replit commands → §3 code-owner reference → §4 quick phase/gate lookup only → §5 test matrix → §6 gate board → §7 GO/NO-GO/handoff.

**Engineering reference when implementing/debugging:** §§8–26.

**Foundational/teaching/reference that you normally do not reread every day:** §§27–31.

**Project records:** §§32–35. **Only §36 is archive/superseded history.**

The reorganization changes navigation, not V3.9 methodology or authorization. Any repository path or command is current only after repository evidence verifies it.

### Current section order (no legacy numbering in the active manual)

| Priority | Current sections | Use |
|---|---|---|
| **Daily / operator** | §§0–7 | Current truth, implementation steps, Replit commands, code/scripts, phases/gates, offline verification, gate status, stop/return decisions |

| **Archive only** | §36 | Superseded commands, historical reports/status snapshots, and original old-log material; never current execution authority |

## 0. Authority, current repository truth, readiness, and status vocabulary

1. The uploaded `V3.9_DataCollectPlan_f.8(4).md` §§0–21 (V3.9-f.8) is the sole scientific/experimental/operational authority for this edition.
2. This Log is the implementation/runbook authority derived from that Plan. It tells the human operator and repository-capable AI **what to build, where to inspect/create it, what to test, what command to run, what evidence closes the step, and when to stop**. It **cannot weaken or override** the Plan.
3. `V3.9_RUN_REPORTS_AND_EVIDENCE.md` is the evidence ledger. It records what actually happened. A report may propose a next step only by pointing to the current §1/§2 step; a report cannot create a new requirement, command, gate, PASS criterion, authorization, airport, budget, threshold, or scientific rule.
4. Active §§0–35 are current Log authority. §36 is archive/history only.
5. If Plan and Log disagree, create `DOC_CONFLICT`, stop the affected path, and correct the Log to the Plan. Never invent a third rule.
6. A file existing is not implementation. A component advances only through:

```text
NOT_INSPECTED
  → DOCUMENTED
  → CODED_STANDALONE
  → UNIT_TESTED
  → PRODUCTION_WIRED
  → OFFLINE_INTEGRATION_TESTED
  → LIVE_VERIFIED       (only where live evidence is required)
```

7. `PRODUCTION_WIRED` requires the real production caller, schema/migration, configuration owner, failure/refusal path, and mapped tests. A helper, script, migration, table, or pure-function test alone cannot close a requirement.
8. Any repository statement below is scoped to the GitHub snapshot explicitly named below. **Replit may contain uncommitted changes or a different HEAD**, so every implementation session begins by reconciling Replit to that snapshot before editing or executing.

These are current code findings at the inspected GitHub commit, not historical guesses.

| ID | Current repository finding | Why it is blocking | Required Phase-0 disposition |

| `CRIT-001` | `adbCollectionController_v3.ts` currently uses `autoCollect: process.env.ADB_AUTO_COLLECT !== "0"` and documents auto-collection as default ON | the literal environment value `"false"` still evaluates as ON; missing env is ON; this violates the Plan's fail-closed preparation state | **first code fix:** explicit opt-in only; absent/`false`/`0`/invalid values = OFF; only the separately authorized Phase-6 transition may enable; add tests |
| `CRIT-002` | V3 routes start the collection watchdog as part of route registration | unsafe if `autoCollect` parsing/default is not fail-closed | watchdog may run, but automatic start must be impossible before `PHASE6_READY` + exact authorization; test startup with missing env |
| `CRIT-003` | production `server/routes_v3.ts` webhook does not currently call `rawIngress_v3.ts` before semantic extraction/upsert and its catch path is designed to return 200 | S2/raw-before-2xx is not production-wired merely because `rawIngress_v3.ts` exists | route must durably persist the normalized immutable envelope/items before successful 2xx; processing attempts remain separate; failure semantics must match Plan §6 |
| `CRIT-004` | management subscription route can default omitted `maxDeliveryRetries` to a nonzero value | V3.9 experimental collection requires `maxDeliveryRetries=0` | experimental subscription creation must set/refuse anything other than 0; test every creation path |
| `CRIT-005` | controller accounting/stop path uses legacy `adb_ingest_events`, UTC-day logic, configurable reconciliation tolerance, and an old short/single balance-read pattern | Plan requires immutable `budget_day_id`, authoritative settled balance delta, ≥3 equal stable reads, Gate-3 `tol=0`, separate Alert/REST ledgers | centralize shared settlement/accounting and make controller/probe/canary/gates reuse it |
| `CRIT-006` | controller still contains legacy static tier/fallback/window scheduling assumptions | Plan requires frozen reference-backed tier/region rules, `UNCLASSIFIED`/`UNMAPPED`, solver-generated 26/3/2 shapes, crossover constraints, and exact manifest | production scheduler must consume frozen frame/calendar/manifest rather than recreate old rules |
| `CRIT-007` | `fidsCensus_v3.ts` still contains older fixed-range/cost/truncation/service-date/storage assumptions | Plan §5 has a substantially stronger FIDS contract: role-aware membership, retrieval grid, availability/cutoff, DST, max 3 total transient attempts, category budget, raw query provenance | repair FIDS client/persistence and its tests before S1 is considered wired |
| `CRIT-008` | `flightInstanceCanonical_v3.ts` and current identity tests still treat ≥2h/date-shift retimes as a new flight identity | Plan §7.1 now keeps retimes as append-only schedule versions under one physical flight unless positive distinct-leg evidence exists | replace old retime split rule; update TEST-008/provider tests together |
| `CRIT-009` | `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`/route path still relies on an older location-oriented event-key pattern and nullable provenance fields | semantic observation identity must work for non-location updates and preserve raw-item/source-clock provenance | introduce/repair general semantic-observation identity and production wiring |
| `CRIT-010` | `manifest_v3.ts` still contains old `f.9`/old-path/self-declared verification concepts | final manifest must be generated from current evidence/hashes and refuse missing values | rebuild manifest contract around f.8 Plan + current evidence; no hard-coded `verified:true` |
| `CRIT-011` | many canonical `v39:*` commands in `package.json` are still `echo` placeholders | a printed message cannot close a gate or Phase-0 requirement | replace every required stub with a thin real wrapper over one production owner; prove refusal behavior |
| `CRIT-012` | `v39:preflight` currently maps to the same contradiction scanner as `v39:scanner` | scanner PASS is not aggregate Phase-0 preflight PASS | implement aggregate preflight that fails unless migration/tests/typecheck/lint/build/registry/traceability/scanner/safety all pass |
| `CRIT-013` | full-repository typecheck was previously reported nonzero and no new zero-result is established by this GitHub inspection | Phase-0 quality closure requires exit 0 under current Log | fix or explicitly remain blocked; no “0 errors only in `_v3.ts`” exception |

It is designed to emit both `dist/index.mjs` and an `index.cjs` wrapper, so the output extensions themselves are not the defect | production build/deployment is not currently a valid readiness check and may never exercise the V3.9 server | fix the package path, point the build entry to the real production server (`server/index.ts` unless repository architecture proves another current owner), verify both Replit deploy and `npm start`, then run offline production startup smoke |

### 0.5 Exact current next action

**Remain in Phase 0.** Follow §1.5 in order, beginning with **§1.5.1 fail-closed runtime safety** and **§1.5.2 production raw-before-2xx ingress**. Do not continue Gate 0 or any other provider/account/live action.

### 0.6 Canonical status vocabulary

| Status | Exact meaning |
|---|---|
| `DOC_ALIGNED` | Plan and Log express the same current rule; no code claim |
| `NOT_INSPECTED` | owner/path/runtime has not been examined for the current evidence scope |
| `REPORTED_EXISTING` | file/path was observed, but semantics/wiring are not yet proven |
| `CODED_STANDALONE` | code exists but production caller/wiring is not proven |
| `UNIT_TESTED` | focused deterministic unit test passed on the recorded commit |
| `PRODUCTION_WIRED` | real production caller + schema/config/failure path proven |
| `OFFLINE_INTEGRATION_TESTED` | real production path passed required offline/integration cases |
| `PARTIAL_EVIDENCE` | some mapped evidence exists but closure criteria remain open |
| `STUB` | interface exists but performs no required implementation/evidence work |
| `PASS` | every criterion for the named scope passes on one recorded evidence state |
| `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE` | no criterion failed but pre-frozen evidence minimum is unmet; never PASS |
| `FAIL` | a criterion failed; affected path stops |
| `MISMATCH` | accounting/identity/manifest/protocol state disagrees; affected work pauses |
| `BLOCKED` | prerequisite is missing or unresolved |
| `BLOCKED_NOT_AUTHORIZED` | live/mutating action lacks exact valid human authorization |
| `DOC_CONFLICT` | Plan and Log disagree; affected path stops until corrected |
| `LIVE_VERIFIED` | current external evidence with account/channel/commit/config/evidence ID is recorded |

Status advancement is valid only for the same commit, schema, config, provider/reference pin, and evidence scope. A relevant change invalidates dependent PASS states.

## 1. Master implementation manual — complete Phase 0 → Phase 7 execution tree

**Authority hierarchy:** binding Plan §§0–21 → this §1/§2 implementation manual → Run Reports as evidence. If they disagree, stop and reconcile to the Plan.

**Purpose of §1:** this is the main implementation manual for both you and the coding AI. It is deliberately organized by **PHASE first**. Gates are children of the phase that owns them. Every phase below answers the same questions:

1. **Why does this phase exist?**
2. **What Plan obligations belong here?**
3. **What current repository files must be inspected/edited?**
4. **What file may be created only if no compliant owner already exists?**
5. **What tables/migrations/config are involved?**
6. **What code behavior must exist?**
7. **What tests must prove it?**
8. **What §2 commands are run?**
9. **What evidence is written?**
10. **What constitutes PASS, FAIL, BLOCKED, and the next stop point?**

**Do not implement from §4.** §4 is only a compact lookup/index. **§1 owns implementation sequence; §2 owns shell commands.**

### 1.1 The entire project in one hierarchy — phases own gates

```text
PHASE 0 — CODE / SCHEMA / OFFLINE SAFETY
  ├─ implement and production-wire R1–R7
  ├─ implement and production-wire S1–S5
  ├─ PRE + AIRBORNE snapshots
  ├─ identity / clocks / targets / outcomes
  ├─ FIDS population + history + weather
  ├─ sampling/adaptation/anchor/crossover logic
  ├─ accounting/settlement/watchdog
  ├─ manifest/split/evaluation instrumentation
  ├─ migrations + canonical command surface
  └─ TEST-001–028 + full quality closure
       ↓ MANDATORY REPORT + STOP

PHASE 1 — ACCOUNT / BUDGET IDENTITY
  └─ Gate 0
       ↓

PHASE 2 — COVERAGE / REFERENCES / FRAME / ANCHOR SELECTION
  ├─ prerequisite P = PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION
  ├─ Gate 1 = coverage
  ├─ traffic + region reference freeze
  ├─ final frame rebuild/hash
  ├─ pre-probe reference freeze
  ├─ mandatory safety smoke
  └─ Gate 2 = Stage 1 + Stage 2 anchor probes
       ↓

PHASE 3 — LIVE ACCOUNTING / PROVIDER SEMANTICS / RELIABILITY
  ├─ Gate 3 = official credit canary
  ├─ Gate 0.5 = dedicated provider/content/clock/T/target pilot
  └─ Gate 4 = cap/reliability verification
       ↓

PHASE 4 — POPULATION OBSERVABILITY
  └─ Gate 5
       ↓

PHASE 5 — FINAL READINESS / FREEZE
  ├─ reverify provider/retention/history/weather
  ├─ materialize REST budget from final calendar
  ├─ solve/hash final 31-batch-day calendar
  ├─ freeze split-assignment rule
  └─ write/hash complete final manifest
       ↓ MANDATORY REPORT + STOP

PHASE 6 — AUTHORIZED 31-BATCH-DAY COLLECTION
       ↓

PHASE 7 — PROTECTED DATASET MATERIALIZATION / MONTH-1 EVALUATION
```

**Binding order:** `Phase 0 → Phase 1/Gate 0 → Phase 2/P → Gate 1 → pre-probe freeze → safety smoke → Gate 2 → Phase 3/Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4 → Phase 4/Gate 5 → Phase 5/FREEZE → separate Phase-6 authorization → Phase 6 → Phase 7`.

### 1.2 Current checkpoint — what the latest repository inspection means

The current project remains in **Phase 0**, now with direct GitHub evidence at commit `5bffdafce6ef152a665eb3b22f735d6cf394491f`.

The most important rule is:

> **Do not ask “which gate do we run next?” until every Phase-0 row below is PASS on one current Replit evidence state.**

Current critical work starts with the runtime and ingress safety defects listed in §0.2. The earlier 208-test result is useful regression evidence, but some of those tests encode **older behavior that must now change**. For example, current identity tests explicitly expect ≥2h retimes/date shifts to create a new identity; binding Plan §7.1 requires retimes to remain versions of the same physical flight unless there is positive distinct-leg evidence. A previously green test can therefore become intentionally red while the implementation is corrected.

### 1.3 Human operator and coding-AI responsibilities

#### 1.3.1 You / human operator

1. Work only from the binding Plan + this Log + current Run Reports.
2. Open the authoritative Replit workspace and ensure the Replit branch/HEAD/worktree are recorded before edits.
3. Until Phase-0 Step 0A is fixed, set the current environment to **`ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=0`**, not the string `false`.
4. Never paste API keys, secrets, webhook secrets, or tokens into this Log/Run Reports.
5. Do not manually choose a paid airport/window/credit amount outside a frozen artifact + exact AUTH.
6. Approve a live/mutating action only after the preceding phase/gate is PASS and the exact authorization record is shown.
7. On `FAIL`, `MISMATCH`, `BLOCKED`, unresolved settlement, unexpected provider mutation, or unclear state: stop and preserve evidence.
8. Bring the Run Report back at every mandatory handoff in §1.13.

#### 1.3.2 Repository-capable AI

1. Reconcile Replit to current GitHub/working-tree truth before changing code.
2. Preserve user changes; never reset/discard work merely to make tests green.
3. Reuse/repair the current production owner whenever possible. **Do not create parallel scientific/accounting logic.**
4. For each requirement record: Plan subsection → requirement ID → production owner → schema/config owner → tests → command → evidence.
5. Fix production behavior and its stale tests together.
6. Any new script must be a thin CLI over the production service, not a separate implementation.
7. Any live/mutating CLI must resolve and print its plan before SEND/request, verify exact AUTH, and refuse mismatches.
8. Run targeted tests after each work package, then the Phase-0 regression cycle.
9. Write evidence to Run Reports; do not overwrite historical run entries.
10. Never self-authorize spend, refill, subscription creation, probe continuation, or Phase 6.

### 1.4 Repository placement rules — edit/reuse first, create only if absent

#### 1.4.1 Current verified project zones

| Zone | Current role | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `server/lib/disruption/` | production V3.9 domain logic | primary location for reusable collection/science/accounting services |
| `server/routes_v3.ts` | production V3 API + webhook wiring | route should orchestrate services, not implement separate scientific rules |
| `server/index.ts` | application boot/runtime | must never make V3.9 paid collection auto-start possible before readiness |
| `server/db.ts` | DB/migration boot owner | migration registration + startup behavior |
| `shared/schema.ts` | shared Drizzle/schema declarations | reconcile with SQL migrations; no silent schema drift |
| `migrations/` | database contract | append migrations; prove fresh/upgrade/repeat/constraint/recovery |
| `scripts/` | operator/CLI wrappers | wrappers reuse production services; no duplicate algorithms |
| `tests/` | deterministic offline proof | every TEST-001–028 behavior maps here or to an explicitly documented integration harness |
| `package.json` | canonical command surface | no readiness/gate command may remain an `echo` stub |
| `SEPmd/` | plan/log/reports artifacts | docs/evidence only; not a substitute for code |

#### 1.4.2 File creation decision

Before creating a file:

```text
SEARCH repository for responsibility
  ↓
found compliant owner?
  YES → edit/reuse it
  NO  → found partial/legacy owner?
          YES → repair/refactor it unless separation is scientifically necessary
          NO  → create one clear owner in server/lib/disruption/
  ↓
wire the real production caller
  ↓
add/update tests
  ↓
map CLI wrapper if operator access is needed
```

Suggested names such as `preSnapshotBuilder_v3.ts`, `airborneSnapshotBuilder_v3.ts`, or `outcomeTerminalizer_v3.ts` below are **only suggestions if no compliant current owner exists**.

### 1.5 Phase 0 — complete offline implementation and safety closure **(CURRENT PHASE / DO NOW)**

#### 1.5.0 Phase-0 purpose and exit contract

**Purpose:** before spending another provider unit/credit, prove that the same production path intended for later gates/Phase 6 implements the Plan's population, raw durability, identity, timestamps, snapshots, outcomes, sampling, budgets, settlement, scheduler, manifest, and refusal rules.

**Canonical data-layer target that Phase 0 must make real (Plan §2):**

```text
AeroDataBox webhook
  → raw_delivery
  → raw_delivery_item
  → flight_events (append-only semantic observations)
  → flight_state (mutable latest-state convenience only)
  → raw_airborne_events → clean_airborne_points → flight_trajectory

AeroDataBox airport FIDS
  → immutable/versioned raw FIDS query/response provenance
  → flight_population (append-only provider-observable membership)

flight_population + cutoff-safe as-of features
  → flight_snapshots (PRE_DEPARTURE)

flight_trajectory + cutoff-safe as-of context
  → flight_airborne_snapshots (AIRBORNE)

terminal webhook/recovery evidence
  → flight_outcomes (operational state + target-specific label status)

historical_feature_store + weather_observation/weather_forecast
  → as-known-at-cutoff feature inputs for both snapshot families
```

**Four identities must stay distinct through that graph (Plan §6):**

1. **Raw delivery identity** = provider notification ID + delivery-attempt sequence where present, otherwise deterministic request/body hash + attempt metadata.
2. **Raw item identity** = `(raw_delivery_id, item_index, raw_item_sha256)`.
3. **Semantic observation identity** = canonical flight instance + semantic event type/phase + applicable provider source clock(s) + raw-item hash; must support non-location events.
4. **Canonical flight-instance identity** = Plan §7.1 Identity-v2 physical-leg key; never interchangeable with notification/delivery/item/event identity and never derived from provider `flight.id` alone.

**Phase 0 has no live numbered gate.** It closes only when every work package 0A–0Q below is evidence-backed on one current commit/schema/config.

**Phase-0 exit requires all of these simultaneously:**

```text
runtime fail-closed safety PASS
raw-before-2xx production wiring PASS
FIDS/population contract offline PASS
identity/timestamp/T logic offline PASS
PRE snapshot builder offline PASS
AIRBORNE pipeline/snapshot builder offline PASS
outcome terminalizer/recovery offline PASS
history/weather as-known joins offline PASS
offline security/retention control machinery + expiry/backup/redaction dry-run PASS
frame/adaptation/probe-score logic offline PASS
R1/R2/R3/R4/R5 accounting/settlement/watchdog offline PASS
R6 calendar/crossover offline PASS
R7 manifest/split/evaluation guard offline PASS
migrations fresh/upgrade/repeat/constraint/recovery PASS
TEST-001–028 required offline portions PASS
full project test suite PASS
full repository typecheck exit 0
lint PASS
production build PASS
registry/traceability/dictionary/lineage PASS
scanner PASS
aggregate v39:preflight PASS
ADB_AUTO_COLLECT resolved OFF
```

Then write the Phase-0 report and **STOP before Phase 1 / Gate 0**.

#### 1.5.1 Phase 0A — make the runtime fail closed before any other implementation work

**Plan coverage:** §§0, 3, 8.1, 11.3, 15 R1/R2/R5/R7, 16–17, 20.

**Current files to inspect/edit:**

- `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts`
- `server/routes_v3.ts`
- `server/index.ts`
- `package.json`
- `.replit`
- configuration registry owner (`configRegistry_v3.ts`)
- tests covering controller/gates/runtime startup

**Current defect:** the inspected controller treats any `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT` value other than `"0"` as ON. Therefore `"false"` is unsafe at this snapshot.

**Required code behavior:**

1. Automatic collection is **explicit opt-in**, never default-on:
   - missing env → OFF;
   - `"0"` → OFF;
   - `"false"` → OFF;
   - invalid value → OFF + warning/refusal;
   - only a separately authorized Phase-6 transition may produce ON.
2. `startCollectionWatchdog()` may monitor, but it may not create subscriptions unless `PHASE6_READY`, manifest hash, exact AUTH, budget admission, and all predecessor evidence match.
3. Every non-Phase-6 process start keeps collection OFF.
4. All experimental subscription creation paths force `maxDeliveryRetries=0` and reject nonzero experimental values.
5. Manual/admin routes cannot bypass the canonical authorization/state machine.
6. `--force`, loose “cleanup all,” generic “start now,” or implicit default parameters cannot bypass safety.
7. A process restart in any pre-Phase-6 phase returns to safe OFF and does not resume collection automatically.
8. Reconcile `.replit`, `package.json`, and the actual build owner. At the inspected commit, `package.json` incorrectly calls `script/build.ts`; the real file is `scripts/build.ts`.
9. Repair `scripts/build.ts` to bundle the real current production server. It currently names nonexistent `server2/index.ts`; verify whether `server/index.ts` is the authoritative server and record the decision. The builder intentionally emits `dist/index.mjs` plus a small `dist/index.cjs` wrapper, so `.replit`/`npm start` may legitimately use different extensions only after the build artifact is proven. Run production-start smoke with provider clients stubbed/offline.

**Offline security/retention machinery required before Phase-0 exit (Plan §17 step 4a; real rights are verified later at prerequisite P):**

- implement/configure secret-manager or equivalent non-log secret handling;
- implement the least-privilege database-role path used by webhook/raw/expiry jobs;
- implement TLS/auth configuration checks and an explicit compensating-control state when the provider offers no webhook-auth mechanism;
- use replay-safe raw-delivery identity and duplicate handling;
- implement redaction tests proving API keys, webhook secrets, authorization headers, and raw payloads do not enter normal command/error telemetry;
- implement retention-expiry execution across primary storage plus replica/backup/object/log surfaces, with a dry-run mode and permitted non-content tombstone/hash behavior;
- implement incident-stop behavior so authentication, raw persistence, reconciliation, or deletion failure disables new experimental subscription starts pending human review;
- keep all real Plan-Term/content-class/legal-basis values unset/BLOCKED until Phase-2 prerequisite P verifies them from Gate-0 channel evidence.

**Tests to add/update:**

- env missing → OFF;
- env `"false"` → OFF;
- env `"0"` → OFF;
- env invalid → OFF/refuse;
- `PHASE6_READY=false` + attempted start → no provider call;
- missing/expired/mismatched AUTH → no provider call;
- experimental subscription request with retries omitted → stored/sent value 0;
- experimental subscription request with retries >0 → refuse;
- startup route/watchdog with pre-Phase-6 state → zero subscription creation calls.

**PASS:** a fake provider spy proves zero outbound provider mutations for every pre-Phase-6 startup/refusal case.

**Until PASS:** in Replit use `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=0` and do not leave `npm run dev` running for V3.9 work without verifying the value.

#### 1.5.2 Phase 0B — production-wire S2 raw immutable ingress **before successful 2xx**

**Plan coverage:** §§2, 5.1 provenance, 6 S2/S3/S4, 11, 15 S2–S4, 17 step 2.

**Current files:**

- `server/routes_v3.ts` — current production webhook
- `server/lib/disruption/rawIngress_v3.ts` — standalone raw-ingress service
- `server/lib/disruption/flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts`
- `server/lib/disruption/flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`
- migrations `0025_raw_ingress_immutable_layers.sql` or current successor
- `tests/raw_ingress.test.ts`
- `tests/dedup_events.test.ts`
- `tests/flight_extractor.test.ts`

**Required architecture:**

```text
HTTP webhook received
  → authenticate/minimally validate
  → identify delivery attempt
  → transactionally persist immutable raw_delivery
  → persist raw_delivery_item rows + hashes/provenance
  → commit durable raw transaction
  → only then successful 2xx
  → semantic extraction/normalization
  → append flight_events / airborne observations
  → mutable convenience state
  → processing_attempt outcome
```

**Required repair:**

1. Make `routes_v3.ts` call the raw-ingress production service before `upsertFlightNotifications` or semantic-event writes.
2. Raw envelope and raw-item identities are distinct from semantic observations and flight identities.
3. Persist provider notification ID/attempt sequence when supplied; otherwise deterministic request/body hash + attempt metadata.
4. `raw_delivery_item` identity includes delivery + item index + raw item SHA.
5. `raw_persisted_at_utc` and actual `available_at` must be populated from durable commit timing; do not write `availableAt=null` for facts used by snapshots.
6. Parser/state failure after durable raw commit writes `processing_attempt`; it must not erase raw evidence.
7. Raw DB/persistence failure must follow the Plan's failure response, not a blanket “always return 200 to save credits” rule.
8. The provider retry setting for experimental collection remains 0; do not redesign raw correctness around provider retries.
9. Preserve compliance expiry/deletion separately from scientific mutation; expired raw content may be removed only according to §10.2 with permitted audit tombstone/hash.

**Create only if absent:** a small webhook-ingress orchestration service may be extracted from `routes_v3.ts`, but it must call the one `rawIngress_v3.ts` owner rather than duplicate inserts.

**PASS tests:**

- raw DB failure → no successful acknowledgment;
- successful raw commit → successful acknowledgment can occur even if later semantic parse fails, with processing attempt recorded;
- duplicate delivery attempt handling preserves distinct attempt provenance;
- raw body/item hashes deterministic;
- semantic/current-state failure never deletes raw rows;
- raw-before-2xx ordering proven with injected call sequence.

#### 1.5.3 Phase 0C — implement S1 provider-observable prediction population and complete FIDS protocol

**Plan coverage:** §§4.3–4.4, 5.0–5.4, 7.1–7.2, 15 S1, Gate 1/Gate 5 prerequisites.

**Current owners to inspect/edit:**

- `server/lib/disruption/fidsCensus_v3.ts`
- `server/lib/disruption/aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts` / central REST limiter
- `server/lib/disruption/flightInstanceCanonical_v3.ts`
- `server/lib/disruption/timestampTaxonomy_v3.ts`
- `scripts/measure_coverage.ts`
- migration `0019_collection_v39_population_and_events.sql`
- `tests/provider_fids.test.ts`
- **create `tests/fidsTimezone.test.ts` because the binding Plan names it**

**Required FIDS request contract:**

- endpoint: airport FIDS current pinned form, never legacy `/flights/schedule`;
- direction uses the one provider parameter with allowed values `direction=Arrival`, `direction=Departure`, or `direction=Both`; PRE departure-population uses the requested-airport departure movement as the primary membership role, while `Both` may be contextual only;
- direction task-specific; PRE population uses requested-airport departure primary role where applicable;
- `withLeg=true`;
- explicitly stamp `population_role=requested_airport_primary | opposite_movement_context`;
- `withCancelled=true`;
- `withCodeshared=true`;
- request `withCargo=false` and `withPrivate=false` when supported/verified; `withCodeshared=true` remains enabled for internal canonicalization;
- unresolved charter/scheduled scope remains `unknown`, never silently included/excluded;
- `withLocation=false` for membership calls;
- provider local-time inputs are IANA-timezone/DST aware;
- canonical internal service interval is half-open `[start,end)`;
- provider edge inclusivity and `fids_max_range_for_actual_plan` are later Gate-0.5 MEASURE→FREEZE items;
- maximum 3 total FIDS transport attempts (initial + at most 2 retries);
- 15-second timeout per physical attempt;
- retry only the pre-frozen transient classes: `429`, eligible `5xx`, connect timeout/reset;
- when the provider supplies `Retry-After`, honor it; otherwise use bounded exponential backoff + jitter;
- never retry authentication, validation/schema, or other non-transient `4xx`;
- every physical attempt reserves/debits its REST category budget **before issuing the HTTP request**;
- exhausted category/rate budget returns `DEFER/FAIL`; it may not bypass through another script;
- no independent REST caller may bypass the central limiter.

**Required population persistence:**

`flight_population` stores append-only membership observations keyed by `population_query_id`/retrieval identity + canonical/provisional analytic identity + population role. It must preserve:

- `source_airport_icao`;
- `query_direction`;
- `population_role`;
- `service_window_start_utc` / `service_window_end_utc`;
- original `fromLocal` / `toLocal`;
- `airport_iana_timezone`;
- `scope_classification`;
- `codeshare_resolution_status`;
- `fids_retrieval_utc`;
- `response_hash`;
- provider/API version plus frozen identifiers such as `fids_api_version`, `fids_docs_date`, and `openapi_sha256` when the contract pin is written;
- raw query/response provenance;
- `available_at`.

At horizon selection also record `population_observed_at_utc` and `population_staleness_seconds`. Repeated horizon/retrieval observations never overwrite.

**T−24/T−6/T−90 acquisition implementation:**

1. do not reconstruct earlier cutoffs from a later final schedule;
2. implement the pre-frozen retrieval grid concept now;
3. later Gate 0.5 freezes acquisition guard, refresh cadence, and staleness ceiling;
4. each snapshot selects the latest population observation with durable `available_at ≤ prediction_cutoff_utc`;
5. later FIDS responses never backfill earlier membership;
6. `assignment_frozen_at` must be early enough to schedule the complete guarded T−24 acquisition grid; otherwise that horizon is unavailable rather than reconstructed later.

**DST tests required:**

- northern spring-forward gap;
- northern fall-back repeat hour;
- southern hemisphere transition;
- UTC experimental window crossing local midnight.

**Population scope and denominator behavior the code must preserve:**

- confirmed scheduled commercial passenger operating flights are the core population;
- cargo/private are excluded from the core population when the verified provider filters support that exclusion;
- charter/non-scheduled records are excluded only when positively classifiable; otherwise `scope_classification='unknown'` and they stay outside the confirmed-core denominator rather than being guessed;
- known canceled and diverted flights remain population members;
- if cargo records are retained for diagnostics they stay in separately named auxiliary `population_cargo`, never the core denominator;
- POST-only auxiliary airports/rows are explicitly `post_only_auxiliary=true` / `POST_auxiliary=true`, separately authorized/budgeted, and never fill a core slot/crossover or support primary denominator claims;
- marketing codeshares are never separate primary physical-leg rows;
- snapshot existence is population-defined, not webhook-capture-defined;
- population membership at a cutoff uses the latest qualifying observation with `available_at ≤ prediction_cutoff_utc`;
- unresolved population absence/staleness is not silently converted to “flight did not exist.”

**Required PRE population/capture counters (persist numerator + denominator, not only rates):**

- `population_count`;
- `horizon_eligible_count`;
- `snapshot_expected_count` (= horizon eligible);
- `snapshot_created_count`;
- `webhook_captured_count`;
- `required_features_complete_count`;
- `optional_feature_missing_count`;
- `target_observed_count`;
- `target_applicable_snapshot_count`;
- `confirmed_operating_leg_count`;
- `ambiguous_codeshare_record_count`.

A zero denominator yields `rate=NULL` + reason, never numeric zero. Coverage dimensions remain parallel fields (`in_frame`, PRE/POST eligibility, subscription started, delivery received, provider failure, zero-yield state, captured in/outside population, snapshot created, outcome observed).

**Current old assumptions to remove/repair:**

- fixed 12-hour protocol if not the actual account/frozen contract;
- fixed REST cost estimates instead of category ledger/current account pin;
- `>=500` as an assumed provider truncation contract;
- FIDS raw storage only in legacy `adb_ingest_events`;
- UTC-date fallback as canonical service date;
- unversioned “one final population row” behavior.

#### 1.5.4 Phase 0D — canonical flight identity, codeshares, retimes, and source clocks

**Plan coverage:** §§5.1 timestamp/canonicalization, 6.0, 6.4, 7.1–7.2, 7.5.

**Current owners:**

- `flightInstanceCanonical_v3.ts`
- `timestampTaxonomy_v3.ts`
- `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts`
- `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`
- `tests/flight_identity.test.ts`
- `tests/provider_fids.test.ts`
- `tests/timestamps_raw.test.ts`

**Critical current mismatch:** current identity code/tests treat ≥2h and date-shift retimes as new IDs. The Plan now requires:

> later schedule revisions—including ≥2h/local-date shift—remain append-only schedule versions under the same physical `flight_instance_id` unless independent provider/operational evidence positively proves a distinct leg.

**Required identity-v2 behavior:**

1. **Do not use provider `flight.id`/`flightId` as canonical key material.** The Plan does not assume it exists or is stable. If a future provider identifier is independently verified, store it only as an optional attribute/evidence field.
2. assign every raw/FIDS appearance a deterministic `provider_flight_record_id` / `provider_record_key` so unresolved provider records remain traceable without pretending they are confirmed physical legs;
3. canonical physical-leg key v2 is derived from **operating carrier + operating flight number + origin ICAO + original destination ICAO + immutable `initial_service_date` + first verified provider-native schedule identity + collision discriminator**;
4. `initial_service_date` is the origin-local date of the first verified provider-native schedule identity; later schedule/date shifts do not rewrite it;
5. retimes update append-only `schedule_version_id`/monotonic `retime_version`; they do **not** create new physical flight IDs by time threshold or date shift alone;
6. a distinct physical leg requires positive independent provider/operational evidence;
7. unresolved distinct-leg case → `identity_resolution_status='ambiguous_distinct_leg'`, preserve/bound rather than double-count;
8. codeshare `Unknown` remains unresolved (`ambiguous_unknown` / analytic `ambiguous_codeshare`); never coerce it to operator/marketing after the fact;
9. marketing identifiers become attributes (`marketing_flight_numbers[]`) on a resolved operating leg, never separate physical-leg rows;
10. collision fallback may use the deterministic provider-record-key hash suffix only to disambiguate distinct retained records; it must not be a mutable schedule value;
11. cross-airport appearances of one physical leg share one `flight_instance_id` while retaining each query/source-airport provenance row;
12. report `confirmed_operating_leg_count` and `ambiguous_codeshare_record_count` separately; an unresolved provisional identity cannot silently support an exact physical-leg denominator.
13. retain `original_scheduled_destination`, `current_operational_destination`, `actual_destination`, and `diversion_flag` separately; directed route/OD is `(origin ICAO, original_scheduled_destination ICAO)` and is never silently made undirected.
14. tail identity fallback is `aircraft_reg > aircraft_mode_s > icao24 > verified provider aircraft ID`, but `tail_known=true` only for a verified non-null registration; conflicts or aircraft swaps break the chain rather than forcing a fallback join.
15. record `midnight_crossing=true/false`; cross-midnight retimes/rotations do not change the immutable origin-local `initial_service_date` merely because UTC/local date changes.

**Clock taxonomy to preserve separately:**

- provider notification generated;
- delivery attempt sequence/time;
- provider state update when actually supplied;
- location-reported time;
- FIDS retrieval;
- HTTP receive;
- raw persist;
- normalized `available_at`;
- semantic event source/occurrence time (nullable when provider supplies no real occurrence time).

Never replace unknown occurrence time with receipt time without `timestamp_source`.

Also preserve record identity/cost/QC fields required by the Plan:

- `notification_id` (webhook envelope identity; not a flight ID);
- `delivery_attempt_seq_no`;
- `delivery_attempt_utc`;
- `delivery_attempt_cost_credits` where the pinned webhook contract supplies it;
- `provider_notification_generated_utc`;
- `provider_state_updated_utc` only on applicable records;
- `location_reported_utc` only when a real location exists;
- `fids_retrieval_utc` only on FIDS/query records;
- `http_received_at_utc` and `raw_persisted_at_utc` only on webhook/raw ingress paths;
- `available_at` for every normalized fact/population observation used in as-of decisions;
- `timestamp_source` for semantic/query-derived facts.

Do not impose `location_reported_utc ≤ provider_state_updated_utc`. Cross-system provider-vs-local clock comparisons are QC/latency checks under a frozen `clock_skew_tolerance_seconds`, not semantic ordering truth. Same-system ordering is record-class specific (`http_received_at_utc ≤ raw_persisted_at_utc ≤ available_at` for webhook; analogous retrieval→persistence→availability ordering for FIDS).


**Eight candidate OOOI/ASPM aliases to support without guessing:**

1. `scheduled_gate_out`
2. `actual_gate_out`
3. `scheduled_wheels_off`
4. `actual_wheels_off`
5. `scheduled_wheels_on`
6. `actual_wheels_on`
7. `scheduled_gate_in`
8. `actual_gate_in`

The provider-native candidates are tested at Gate 0.5; any unverified semantic/actuality mapping remains NULL with `milestone_unverified`/`actuality_unverified`. Never copy one ambiguous scheduled/revised/runway value into two aliases merely to fill columns.

**Route/tail identity contract that uses this identity layer:**

- directed OD = `(origin ICAO, original_scheduled_destination ICAO)`;
- keep original scheduled, operational, and actual destination separately;
- diversion = actual destination differs from original + flag;
- route uses operating carrier, not marketing code;
- tail identity priority: verified `aircraft_reg` > mode-S > ICAO24 > verified provider aircraft ID, but `tail_known=true` only for verified registration;
- identifier conflicts and aircraft swaps break the chain;
- `tail_chain_links` require consistent verified registration, next origin matching prior operational destination (or frozen no-diversion rule), and `max_turnaround=6h`; cross-midnight allowed.

#### 1.5.5 Phase 0E — general semantic event identity and event-log-before-state

**Plan coverage:** §§2, 6 S3/S4, 6.1–6.5.

**Current owner:** `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts` plus route/extractor/raw provenance.

**Required repair:**

1. semantic observation identity must work for location and non-location events;
2. key is versioned over canonical flight instance + event type/phase + available provider state/location clocks + raw-item hash;
3. same timestamp may have multiple distinct updates;
4. `flight_events` append occurs before convenience/current-state mutation;

8. never treat `(flight, carrier, locReportedUtc)` as universal identity.

**Tests:** same-clock multiple updates, non-location update with no location timestamp, retry duplicate provenance, parse order, source-clock preservation.

#### 1.5.6 Phase 0F — exact T milestone and PRE snapshot builder

**Plan coverage:** §§5.2, 6.0–6.1, 6.3–6.5, 7.3–7.4, 12–13.

**Important distinction:** the **code path must be implemented now**, but the actual `selected_t_milestone` remains BLOCKED until Gate 0.5 verifies provider-native semantics. Do not hard-code a guessed FAA alias during Phase 0.

**Find owner first.** If no compliant PRE snapshot service exists, suggested owner:
`server/lib/disruption/preSnapshotBuilder_v3.ts`.

**Required builder input contract:**

- append-only `flight_population` membership observations;
- frozen/verified T construct only after Gate 0.5;
- horizon (`T-24h`, `T-6h`, `T-90m`);
- historical/weather/operational feature stores with `available_at`;
- manifest/config versions.

**PRE prediction-state contract:** the builder emits `prediction_state = PRE_DEPARTURE` only for T−24h/T−6h/T−90m snapshots built from the frozen selected-T milestone.

**Selected-T immutability:** after Gate 0.5 verifies the provider-native milestone, `T` for each physical flight uses the **first observed value of that verified selected milestone** from the first qualifying identity/schedule evidence. Later `revisedTime`/schedule revisions are append-only versions and never silently move T or earlier cutoff membership.

**Snapshot existence rule:**

```text
snapshot_exists =
  population_member_at_cutoff
  AND horizon_eligible
```

Webhook capture and optional feature completeness are **not** prerequisites.

**Feature rule:** each feature may enter only when its information availability timestamp is `≤ prediction_cutoff_utc`; otherwise NULL + missingness flag. A future-dated feature must make the test fail.

**Required snapshot provenance:**

- `flight_instance_id`;
- population observation/query ID;
- horizon;
- selected-T version;
- prediction cutoff;
- builder version/SHA;
- feature source/version/effective time/available_at;
- frame/reference/config hash;
- missingness flags;
- sampling strategy/reason/window metadata where applicable.

**Canonical `snapshot_provenance` payload must be reconstructible:**

```text
flight_population_row_id/hash
fids_response_hash
schedule_version
weather_snapshot_id
historical_feature_row_ids[]
feature_builder_version / git SHA
snapshot_builder_version
```

Store this as explicit columns and/or `provenance_json`/`provenance_hash` without losing the joinable source IDs. `materialized_at` is audit time only and never substitutes for an input's `available_at`.

**Phase-0 tests:** population member with zero webhook still gets snapshot; missing optional feature doesn't drop row; post-cutoff feature excluded; later population observation cannot backfill earlier cutoff; T unavailable → horizon BLOCKED/not fabricated.

#### 1.5.7 Phase 0G — S5 airborne preservation + AIRBORNE snapshot builder

**Plan coverage:** §§2, 6.1–6.2, 6.4–6.5, 7.5, 13 POST.

**Current owners to reuse/repair:**

- `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`
- migration `0020_collection_v39_airborne_time_series.sql`
- `tests/airborne_pipeline.test.ts`

If no compliant AIRBORNE snapshot service exists, suggested:
`server/lib/disruption/airborneSnapshotBuilder_v3.ts`.

**AIRBORNE prediction-state contract:** derived rows use `prediction_state = AIRBORNE`; keep `state_observation_time_utc` physically separate from deployable `prediction_cutoff_utc`/`available_at`.

**Required layers:**

```text
raw airborne/location observations
  → clean_airborne_points
  → flight_trajectory
  → flight_airborne_snapshots
```

**Rules:**

1. preserve every legitimate point; never latest-location-only first;
2. physical location observation clock is separate from deployable prediction cutoff;
3. `prediction_cutoff_utc` is normally durable availability time;
4. only verified provider-native airborne movement evidence enables AIRBORNE state;
5. flight-level funnel:
   `airborne_eligible → observed → usable → trajectory_complete → POST_snapshot_eligible → POST_labeled`;
6. multiple snapshots from one flight remain in the same primary evaluation partition;
7. cadence/completeness thresholds are later Gate-0.5 MEASURE→FREEZE values; code must accept frozen config rather than hard-code the answer.

8. `airborne_eligible` is defined independently of webhook airborne capture: `flight_population ∩ verified provider-native evidence that the flight actually became airborne`; a webhook point cannot define this denominator;
9. if no provider-native operational state or verified-actual runway evidence can independently establish airborne movement, set `AIRBORNE_DENOMINATOR=BLOCKED`;
10. preserve flight phase values explicitly (`pre_departure`, `taxi_out`, `airborne_climb`, `airborne_cruise`, `airborne_descent`, `approach`, `landed`, `taxi_in`, `gate_in`) rather than pooling incompatible physical states;
11. raw/clean trajectory preservation includes identity, all Plan timestamp clocks, provider-native scheduled/revised movement fields, verified OOOI aliases only when justified, lat/lon/altitude/pressure altitude/pressure/ground speed/true track/VSI/on-ground/phase, `eta_provider` where supplied, and QC fields including `trajectory_gap_seconds`, `source_latency_seconds`, and `data_quality_flag`;
12. `distance_to_destination`, `distance_flown`, and `fraction_of_route_completed` are **derived downstream features**, never raw provider facts, and carry transform/provenance/availability clocks;
13. implement a configurable `post_departure_observation_policy` and record `obs_per_flight`, `median_gap_seconds`, `P95_gap_seconds`, `max_gap_seconds`, trajectory duration, completeness %, and source latency. Do not assume provider cadence; supplementary REST-airborne monitoring remains OFF unless Gate 0.5 later measures insufficient cadence and a separately frozen need/budget authorizes it.
14. preserve the milestone-explicit POST target family rather than one ambiguous arrival-delay field:
    - **POST-A:** absolute landing target = verified `actual_wheels_on`; deployable remaining-time label = `actual_wheels_on - prediction_cutoff_utc`;
    - **POST-B1:** `label_arr_delay_wheels_on = actual_wheels_on - scheduled_wheels_on`;
    - **POST-B2:** `label_arr_delay_gate_in = actual_gate_in - scheduled_gate_in`;
    - PRE-side departure delay likewise keeps `actual_gate_out - scheduled_gate_out` and `actual_wheels_off - scheduled_wheels_off` as separate targets.


#### 1.5.8 Phase 0H — target-specific outcomes, terminalizer, censoring, and recovery

**Plan coverage:** §§7.3–7.4, 5.4 outcome REST category, 6.3 milestones.

Find current owner; if absent, suggested:
`server/lib/disruption/outcomeTerminalizer_v3.ts`.

**Required outcome model has two independent dimensions:**

```text
flight_operational_state:
  scheduled / departed / arrived / canceled /
  canceled_uncertain / diverted / unknown

label_status (per target):
  pending / observed / censored / missing / not_applicable
```

Never collapse those into one five-state status.

**Recovery contract:**

- target-specific terminalization; `gate_out`, `wheels_off`, `wheels_on`, `gate_in`, cancellation/diversion, and POST targets close independently;
- preserve the four target booleans (`gate_out_label_observed`, `wheels_off_label_observed`, `wheels_on_label_observed`, `gate_in_label_observed`) rather than one generic observed flag;
- recovery uses the **same bounded canonical query identity** from population acquisition: source airport, service interval, original local bounds/timezone, original direction/role, and flight-instance matching key; never issue an unbounded loose search;
- separately freeze `recovery_direction` and any diversion/destination query shape before Phase 6;
- reference arrival = latest revised arrival that was available when recovery was scheduled, otherwise the original verified scheduled-arrival construct;
- logical recovery opportunities at reference-arrival `+30`, `+120`, `+360` minutes; at least 90 minutes between the first two;
- compatible due identities may be coalesced into pre-frozen buckets, so one physical FIDS call may satisfy multiple flights;
- each physical request still follows the maximum-three-total-attempt FIDS transport rule;
- third logical recovery opportunity ends further normal recovery calls but **does not** terminalize an absent label;
- unresolved labels remain `pending` until the frozen recovery deadline = reference arrival `+24h`;
- at deadline: verified target evidence → `observed`; cancellation can make movement target `not_applicable`; absent unusable evidence → `missing` (or `censored` only for a separately valid survival endpoint);
- every physical call enters `OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET`;
- if category cap cannot fund a call, `DEFER/REFUSE` rather than borrow;
- every attempt records endpoint/query identity/request+response hashes/retrieval+availability times/units/evidence/decision;
- estimated timestamps cannot masquerade as verified actual terminal milestones; `CanceledUncertain` never fabricates cancellation.

**Gate 0.5 later measures/finalizes censoring grace from verified actual terminal evidence.**

#### 1.5.9 Phase 0I — historical feature store, weather as-known joins, feature discipline, and graph metadata

**Plan coverage:** §§10, 12, 12.2.1–12.3, 13.

**Current owners:**

- `historicalFeatureStore_v3.ts`
- `weatherSignal.ts`
- relevant history migration `0024`
- `tests/weather_history.test.ts`

**Historical store required fields/semantics (`historical_feature_store`):**

- append-only bitemporal/as-of versions;
- entity types: airport, route, carrier×airport, tail, OD pair, weather;
- source/effective timestamp;
- `available_at`;
- `valid_from`;
- source/version/hash;
- as-of query requires semantic validity **and** `available_at ≤ T`;
- never mutate prior row just to set a derived `valid_to`; derive with window logic where specified;
- history readiness is infrastructure readiness, not a promise every row is complete;
- row-level `history_complete_for_snapshot` is evaluated from frozen lookbacks/minimum counts; incomplete history stays NULL/flagged and does not delete snapshot;
- actual-delay history cannot be called “actual” until target semantics are verified.

**Bootstrap/readiness:**

- implement support for lawful weather archive + permitted provider history/pre-run data;
- `history_store_ready_at` records when the schema, ingestion, source versions, and as-of query are operational;
- global `history_ready_at` is the later evaluation-readiness boundary that prevents structurally immature early days from primary evaluation; do not conflate it with `history_store_ready_at`;
- after readiness, incomplete row features stay in denominator with flags.

**Weather:**

- preserve observation/forecast issue/valid/availability clocks;
- operational hierarchy follows Plan §10;
- no retrospective ERA5 (or equivalent hindsight analysis source) may fill an operationally missing as-known feature;
- source retention/licensing/expiry handled by prerequisite P/Phase 5;
- weather join uses only what was available by cutoff.
- operational precedence: `live_metar > archive_metar > frozen GFS/NAM grid source`; ERA5 is not operational fallback;
- spatial join: nearest operational station within 30 km, otherwise the appropriate frozen GFS/NAM grid cell;
- no qualifying weather observation within 6h → `weather_missing=true`, source NULL; do not impute;
- TAF uses latest amendment whose `issue_time` **and** `available_at` are ≤ cutoff;
- GFS/NAM keep model run/issue, valid time, retrieval, and availability distinct;
- preserve weather source/version/API-date/retrieval/archive-depth evidence in the manifest; specifically, the currently documented AviationWeather retrieval depth of up to the previous 30 days is only `weather_api_history_depth_verified`, **not** a retention license/right, and must be reverified at FREEZE.

**Universal feature-discipline rules:**

- every feature must satisfy `information_available_timestamp ≤ prediction_cutoff_utc`;
- schedule/status/destination values are as-known-at-cutoff versions, never final later revisions;
- previous-leg/tail features require both semantic occurrence and actual availability by cutoff; preserve the Plan knowledge flags `previous_leg_exists_known`, `previous_leg_landed_known_0/1`, `previous_leg_arrival_value_known_0/1`, and `previous_leg_arrival_available_at`;
- store explicit missingness/knowledge flags rather than dropping rows;
- preserve source/effective time separately from availability;
- for every deterministic derived feature preserve `input_knowledge_available_at = max(required_input.available_at)`, `online_feature_available_at` when an already-deployed online transform actually made it usable (nullable for offline replay), and `materialized_at` for later table construction; offline replay may use only the frozen transform over cutoff-eligible inputs and may never backdate availability;
- record `source_matrix_version` for historical/weather/ATC/storm sources;
- pre-run bootstrap must make `history_ready_at` precede the first eligible primary snapshot;
- Model −1 persistence is not well-defined without its required recent-delay/previous-leg history;
- no `1/p` auto-weighting.

**Historical-store binding constants/code paths:**

- key form: `(entity_type, entity_id, feature_name, valid_from)`;
- entity types: airport, route, carrier×airport, tail, OD-pair, weather;
- minimum lookbacks: airport/route 7 days; tail previous-leg search within 24h; weather operational lookback 6h;
- airport/route recent-delay aggregate requires at least 5 qualifying flights, otherwise `history_incomplete`;
- update on qualifying `flight_events` plus the frozen batch/daily recomputation path;
- primary history feature list must support airport delay 1h/6h/24h, route delay 1h/6h/24h, carrier×airport delay, tail previous-leg delay, OD delay, utilization 7d, congestion 1h, and weather snapshot linkage, with any unavailable/unverified item NULL/flagged;
- actual-delay features remain NULL/unverified until actual milestone semantics are proven.

**Graph/chain metadata:**

- preserve route/OD/tail identity;
- chain completeness uses the nested denominators exactly:
  `scheduled_candidate_successor → observable_candidate_successor → successor_linked`, while `known_absent`, `unknown/boundary`, aircraft swap, missing registration, and conflicts are reported separately;
- report `scheduled_chain_coverage = observable_candidate_successors / scheduled_candidate_successors`;
- report primary `observable_chain_link_rate = linked_successors / observable_candidate_successors`;
- an unobservable future leg is not a linkage failure;
- graph edge taxonomy stays explicit: **static route/schedule**, **dynamic state-at-cutoff congestion**, **resource/capacity/ATC**, and **aircraft/flight-chain**;
- `known-absent` edge and `unknown/unobserved` edge are different masks; a graph model may not equate missing observation with “no edge”;
- deferred multi-flight disruption-event taxonomy does not become a Phase-0 invented source; until a later named-source/taxonomy freeze, `disruption_event_id=NULL` and event sampling is OFF.

#### 1.5.10 Phase 0J — sampling frame code, tier/region rules, eligibility, adaptation, and anchor-score implementation

**Plan coverage:** §§4, 8.2–8.6, 9.

**Current owners:**

- `adbAirportCatalog_v3.ts`
- `adaptiveMi_v3.ts`
- `adbCollectionController_v3.ts`
- `scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts`
- `scripts/anchor_probe.ts`
- migrations `0021`, `0022`, `0023`
- `tests/adaptation.test.ts`

**Important phase distinction:** implement the algorithms now; **do not freeze real traffic/region source values until Phase 2 after Gate 1.**

**Required frame behavior:**

- measured provider-covered universe comes from the pinned provider **health/feed coverage endpoints**; a project-local `/api/v1/collection/coverage` aggregator is acceptable only when its provider inputs, contract/version, cache policy, and hashes are proved;
- every catalog rebuild comes from a versioned coverage artifact, writes a new hash, and never overwrites the prior frame artifact;
- `coverage_cache_ttl` is MEASURE→FREEZE from provider/account behavior; no hard-coded 12-hour TTL is binding;
- measured provider-covered universe;
- explicit feed eligibility;
- `UNCLASSIFIED` remains visible, not blanket REGIONAL;
- `UNMAPPED` remains visible/excluded from corresponding stratum;
- only confirmed `coverage_failed` leaves feed-eligible frame;
- primary strata = traffic tier × macro-region;
- core slots = 1 HUB + 2 MID + 1 REGIONAL;
- every core slot must be dual PRE+POST eligible;
- anchor consumes HUB slot when enabled;
- no POST-only auxiliary airport fills a core slot.

**Tier/reference code must support later frozen values:**

- one 12-month exogenous traffic reference ending no more than 30 days before frame freeze;
- exactly one deterministic threshold/rank/tie/missing rule: absolute-threshold equality enters the higher tier; rank/percentile cuts sort `(traffic_metric DESC, ICAO ASC)` and freeze exact rank counts;
- missing/nonfinite/negative traffic reference stays visible as `tier='UNCLASSIFIED'`, `tier_verified=false`, never REGIONAL;
- `traffic_prior`: HUB=3, MID=1.5, REGIONAL=1;
- `traffic_score = min(1, traffic_metric_value / hub_cut_metric)`;
- the later Phase-2 reference artifact/manifest must name exact provenance fields including `traffic_source_name`, `traffic_source_version`, `traffic_retrieval_date`, `traffic_period`, `traffic_metric_name`, metric units, `hub_cut_metric`, `tier_cut_rule`, `tier_version`, `tier_hash`, `region_mapping_source`, `region_mapping_version`, `region_mapping_retrieval_date`, and `region_mapping_hash`;
- country→macro-region mapping + explicit overrides: Turkey→EU, Greenland→NA, Australia→OC; Russia uses frozen airport longitude `<60.000000°E`→EU and `≥60.000000°E`→AP, otherwise `UNMAPPED`;
- no unstated ICAO prefix fallback; a prefix table is permitted only if separately versioned and explicitly frozen before Stage 1;
- the production core selector must enforce the semantic equivalent of `in_frame=true AND pre_eligible=true AND post_eligible=true` **plus verified tier and mapped region** for every core slot; an empty required cell is `REFUSED/UNSAT`, never substituted.

**Anchor/reference variables:**

- degree, carrier diversity, intl share, traffic score, geo score, carrier score;
- directed/undirected network degree is based on the frozen scheduled-route reference; primary = undirected degree;
- `effective_carriers = 1/HHI` using carriers with ≥5% of departures; Shannon diversity may be reported diagnostically but is not the primary metric;
- `degree_cap` = 90th percentile of non-null degree across the frozen frame;
- `degree_norm = min(1, degree/degree_cap)`;
- `carriers_cap` = 90th percentile of non-null effective-carriers across the frozen frame;
- `effective_carriers_norm = min(1, effective_carriers/carriers_cap)`;
- `intl_share` is already [0,1];
- `region_rarity = 1 - region_share`, where the mapped `in_frame` denominator excludes `UNMAPPED`; zero mapped rows yields NULL;
- `geo_score = 0.5*degree_norm + 0.3*intl_share + 0.2*region_rarity`;
- `carrier_score = 0.6*effective_carriers_norm + 0.4*intl_share`;
- normalization caps and formulas are frozen before Stage 1 and carry `anchor_norm_frozen_at`;
- no probe result may retroactively choose exogenous normalization.

**Adaptive REGIONAL behavior:**

- store `airport_layer_design_probability = p_i` as the conditional design probability at each REGIONAL draw; `sampling_weight` remains NULL by default and there is no automatic `1/p`;
- Phase-6 initial state is explicitly `m_i_initial_source='uniform'`, `ema_initial_source='none'`, and `zero_yield_initial_state='normal'`; every eligible REGIONAL airport starts `m_i=1.0`, EMA NULL, and probe results do not seed adaptation;
- deterministic EMA `α=0.5`;
- frozen zero-yield state machine;
- coverage-floor boost and realized selection reporting;
- only final weighted draw is random under frozen seed;
- Phase-6 initial state is uniform `m_i=1`, EMA NULL, zero-yield normal; probe results do not seed Phase-6 adaptation.

**Exact REGIONAL adaptation contract the code must implement:**

- `m_i ∈ [0.25,1.5]`;
- first valid nonempty Phase-6 yield sets EMA directly; thereafter `ema = 0.5*yield + 0.5*previous_ema`;
- `raw_m = ema_yield_i / median_ema_yield_frame`;
- `m_i = clamp(raw_m,0.25,1.5)`;
- score cap `≤ traffic_prior*1.5`;
- reference median uses eligible REGIONAL airports with non-NULL EMA excluding provider/coverage failures and persistent zero-yield; if pool empty or median ≤0, base `m_i=1`;
- true zero requires a successful complete provider observation with zero distinct canonical flights; provider failure is never a zero;
- `zero_yield_once`: no numeric penalty;
- `zero_yield_repeated` after ≥2 consecutive valid empties: use `clamp(base_m_i*0.75,0.25,1.5)`;
- `zero_yield_persistent` after ≥5 consecutive empties or 30 days of empties: remains in frame/selectable via coverage floor, excluded from adaptive median pending manual review;
- only proven `coverage_failed` leaves feed-eligible frame;
- `stale` >60 days does not remove the airport;
- every update appends version/history to `m_i_history`; persist the canonical state hash, probability vector, frozen `random_seed`, and selected ICAO so deterministic replay can be proved; no clock reset of EMA.

**Exact HUB/MID/region-selection contract:**

- when `ADB_ANCHOR_ENABLED=true`, the enabled anchor consumes the one HUB slot;
- new anchor draws rotate the frozen five-pool no-repeat-until-all under `anchor_pool_seed`; crossover period-2 `PAIR_REPLAY` is the only explicit repeat;
- if anchor disabled, HUB uses freshest-first among eligible candidates;
- MID uses deterministic freshest-first with `recent_exclusion=7 days` unless the frame is too small for the slots;
- logical slots are `HUB`, `MID_A`, `MID_B`, `REGIONAL`;
- region is selected **before** airport;
- each non-anchor slot uses a frozen seeded balanced permutation over its nonempty eligible mapped-region set;
- MID_A and MID_B use distinct derived seeds and cannot select the same airport in one batch;
- crossover period 2 replays the period-1 airport set and consumes no new region draw;
- after region is fixed, selection occurs only inside that tier×region cell;
- empty cell → `REFUSED_CELL_EMPTY`; never substitute another tier/region or POST-only airport;
- freeze/hash slot→region sequence and report planned/realized counts/refusals/replays.

**Recently-observed diagnostic (never a selector input):**

- `recently_observed_5d=true` only when the latest qualifying **successful direct observation** is within five days of that batch's `window_start`;
- recompute it per batch from the immutable direct-observation owner;
- never infer it from edge discovery or an empty/failed check;
- report count/share by tier×region;
- it is diagnostic only and cannot change frame, eligibility, adaptation, anchor ranking, or representativeness claims.

**Coverage-floor exact rule for REGIONAL draws:**

- if an eligible REGIONAL airport has never had a qualifying Phase-6 direct observation or its last one is ≥20 days old, `coverage_floor_eligible=true` and `coverage_boost_i=1.5`; otherwise boost=1.0;
- `adaptive_score_i = traffic_prior_i * m_i`;
- `draw_score_i = adaptive_score_i * coverage_boost_i`;
- `p_i = draw_score_i / Σ draw_score`;
- an unsuccessful draw does not reset age/boost; only a successful valid Phase-6 direct observation does;
- positive probability/boost is not a guarantee of finite-run selection; report age, boost, probability, and realized selection separately.

**Per-batch environmental context implementation:**

- persist source-grounded METAR/TAF components (visibility, ceiling, wind/gust, precipitation, convective indicators);
- persist verified ATC traffic-management flags such as `ground_stop` and `ground_delay_program` with source/effective/retrieval/availability clocks;
- storm/radar context is allowed only from a named NOAA product + frozen spatial/temporal join;
- `any_atc_program_active` may be derived as true/false/NULL;
- a composite `weather_severity_index` remains NULL/DEFERRED unless its deterministic formula/source/missing rule/version is frozen;
- retire non-standard `MCD (Major Command Decision)` terminology;
- write the complete audit payload to `environmental_context_json` on the batch/window record.

**Probe code now, live use later:**

- exact 12 dual-eligible HUB candidates across regions including WSSS + OMAA;
- Stage 1 target 2h; Stage 2 target 4h;
- cumulative `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` per immutable probe budget day;
- sequential, settlement before next budget day;
- capacity gate `rows_per_hour ≥ 60`;
- current score weights/formulas from Plan §9; observed yield only supplies the allowed observed component;
- persist the exact ambiguity-bound fields `confirmed_unique_lower` and `confirmed_plus_ambiguous_upper`; the conservative lower bound drives scoring and final-five membership must be invariant under the ambiguity bounds or Gate 2 becomes `INSUFFICIENT_IDENTITY_RESOLUTION`;
- stability uses `bucket_width=15 minutes`; only complete 15-minute buckets fully inside actual exposure count, the final partial bucket is excluded unless an alternative exposure-normalized rule was frozen before Stage 1, and fewer than pre-frozen `min_stability_buckets` yields `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`;
- cap-censoring recorded, never pretend shortened probe completed full target;
- deterministic replacement/rank-invariance logic.

**Exact anchor yield-reference/scoring implementation:**

- WSSS is primary yield reference;
- OMAA is fallback only when WSSS capacity/reference validity fails under the frozen fallback rule;
- both are still probed diagnostically and `wsss_vs_omaa_ratio` reported;
- both reference failures → abort/no scoring;
- for each `{uf_per_credit, chain_per_credit, stability}`:
  `component_std = clamp(candidate_component/reference_component,0,1)`;
- reference component must be finite and >0; no after-the-fact epsilon/drop/reweight;
- `yield_score=(uf_std+chain_std+stability_std)/3`;
- `anchor_score=0.40*traffic_score + 0.20*geo_score + 0.20*carrier_score + 0.20*yield_score`;
- station/API capacity is a feasibility gate, not a score component;
- score tie → lexical ICAO.

**Stability implementation:**

- 15-minute buckets;
- only complete buckets fully inside actual active exposure;
- bucket count = distinct canonical flight instances whose first valid probe observation falls in the bucket;
- population variance across observed buckets;
- `CV=SD/mean`; mean 0 → stability 0;
- `stability=1/(1+CV)`;
- cap-truncated unobserved time is not filled with zeros;
- fewer than pre-frozen `min_stability_buckets` → `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`.


#### 1.5.11 Phase 0K — R1/R2/R3/R4/R5 accounting, exclusivity, settlement, and watchdog

**Plan coverage:** §§3, 11, 15 R1–R5, Gates 0/3/4.

**Current owners:**

- `adbCollectionController_v3.ts`
- `budgetAccounting_v3.ts`
- `gates_v3.ts`
- `aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts`
- `scripts/credit_canary.ts`
- Gate-0 scripts
- raw/item/attempt ledgers
- migrations `0017`, `0018`

**R1 — exclusivity**

- paid experimental set has explicit owner/batch/run identity;
- isolated smoke/probe/canary uses the one intended experimental subscription/set;
- Phase-6 batch may have its intended multiple airport subscriptions linked to one batch;
- never delete foreign subscription by broad convenience flag;
- before accounting, prove experimental set attribution.

**R2 — SEND-aware admission/watchdog**

- immutable Phase-6 budget-day cap = 1,900 Alert credits;
- `protected_alert_floor` is at least 1,000 credits and is a reserve, not a spend target;
- `ending_alert_margin`, `protected_api_floor_units`, and `ending_api_margin_units` are explicit nonnegative frozen reserves; unknown reserve blocks the affected billable path;
- `pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling` is the exact sum of separately authorized ceilings for smoke, every possible Stage-1 candidate/replacement, every Stage-2 confirmation/replacement, Gate 3, Gate 0.5, and the small Gate-4 live check; no unbounded “extra/retry probe” bucket;
- budget-day identity is `run_day_index`/owning parent batch day, not UTC calendar date;
- `MAX_DESIGN_CEILING=57,900` is only the project-authorized upper bound; the actual Phase-6 run ceiling is frozen `≤57,900`, not a “31×1900 spend target”;
- admission uses settled provider delta + received cost ledger + reservations + frozen unsettled-burst reserve;
- `SOFT_STOP=1900−ADB_DAILY_SOFT_STOP_MARGIN`; the `50`/`1850` pair is only the required arithmetic fixture until the margin is measured/frozen; `HARD_CAP=1900`;
- overshoot/mismatch blocks new start.

**R3 — canary implementation**

- same production raw/accounting path as real collection;
- shared stable settlement;
- exact Gate-3 reconciliation `tol=0`;
- >0 item;
- zero failures;
- raw-before-2xx;
- owned cleanup;
- no foreign billable subscription.

**R4 — two-resource model**

Plan §17 step 1 does not require a standalone “R4 module”: R4 is a cross-cutting cost-model consistency rule that must be enforced in the controller/accounting/config/docs and tested wherever units/credits are admitted or reported.

- Alert credits and REST/API units are separate resources;
- seven REST categories remain separately capped: `FIDS_BASE_UNITS`, `FIDS_SPLIT_UNITS`, `FIDS_RETRY_UNIT_BUDGET`, `VALIDATION_UNIT_BUDGET`, `OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET`, `HISTORY_BOOTSTRAP_UNIT_BUDGET`, `DIAGNOSTIC_UNIT_BUDGET`;
- never add the two units as if one number;
- refill is a separately authorized conversion/mutation accounted in both ledgers.

**R5 — failure → pause/reconcile**

- provider SEND charged but no webhook receive must still prevent uncontrolled continuation;
- record `failure_window_start` and `failure_window_end`; the window is not assumed to end at the next batch;
- population/horizon-eligible flights remain in the snapshot denominator through a failure window with notification-derived features marked missing;
- raw persistence failure, settlement timeout, accounting mismatch, or foreign-sub state pauses/refuses;
- recovery requires reconciliation + new valid state, not “keep going because local rows are low.”

**Shared settlement service required:**

Before the first paid smoke, the shared service consumes frozen:
`settlement_initial_wait_seconds`, `settlement_poll_interval_seconds`,
`settlement_stable_read_count` (minimum 3), `settlement_timeout_seconds`, and
`pre_smoke_unsettled_burst_margin_credits`. The smoke then measures
`unsettled_burst_margin_credits`, which may stay the same/increase but never silently decrease.


After stop/deletion/inactivation, authoritative balance reads must reach **at least three consecutive equal reads spanning the frozen stability window**. Any change resets stability. Timeout → `SETTLEMENT_UNRESOLVED`. No later paid run starts until resolved. A later charge that appears after a prior closure reopens that record as `MISMATCH` and pauses paid work.

The internal accounting view preserves separately: `notification_items_received`, `delivery_attempt_cost_credits_received` (sum of applicable received `deliveryAttempt.costCredits`), `raw_items_durably_persisted`, `unique_flight_rows_created_or_updated` (plus inserted/updated/duplicate diagnostics), and authoritative `credits_actually_consumed = B_before - B_stable`. Admission/remainder arithmetic uses nonnegative **SEND-attributable** settled spend quantities; refill or unrelated balance movement is never silently counted as spend. Received rows/items alone never authorize more spend.

Gate 3 alone uses exact `tol=0`. If later production evidence justifies a nonzero operational tolerance, it must be frozen under the separate name `PRODUCTION_RECONCILE_TOLERANCE`; until then any nonzero discrepancy is `MISMATCH`.

For a settled budget-day overshoot, keep the deviation recorded and compute `next_budget_day_cap=max(0,1900-overshoot_amount)` while recomputing the run remainder. Overshoot >100 credits pauses the project for human intervention; this compensating rule never erases the original breach.

**Current code repair:** remove the legacy short delay/single-read + configurable tolerance as authoritative closure. One shared service must be reused by smoke/probe/canary/controller/gates.

#### 1.5.12 Phase 0L — R6 scheduler/crossover/calendar implementation

**Plan coverage:** §§8.1, 8.7–8.8, 15 R6, TEST-017.

**Current owners:**

- `experimentCalendar_v3.ts`
- `adbCollectionController_v3.ts`
- `scripts/calendar_solve.ts`
- `tests/calendar_solver.test.ts`

**Required scheduler contract:**

The scheduler must explicitly refuse undeclared experiment templates, template/tier/slot mismatch, and crossover period-2 without period-1. A requested 6h alternative that stops at the cap is recorded as `up-to-6h` with `stop_reason='budget_reached'`, never relabeled complete 6h. Hard constraints are never traded away for balance/soft objectives.


- final shape exactly `26×4h + 3×2×2h + 2×up-to-6h = 31 batch-days`; `run_day_index=1..31` follows actual window-start chronology, and washout may make the run span more than 31 calendar dates;
- each complete 6-day block uses the six UTC start slots `{00,04,08,12,16,20}` exactly once; among valid schedules minimize weekday×UTC imbalance, but never claim perfect weekday×UTC balance;
- **exactly five crossover pairs** in the 31-batch-day calendar:
  - 3 pairs: 4h vs 2×2h;
  - 2 pairs: 4h vs up-to-6h;
- paired periods use same frozen airport set, time class, weekday class, evaluation partition;
- ≥24h end→start washout;
- `crossover_group_id` is the unique matched pair ID; optional `crossover_block_id` is separate;
- `randomization_unit='batch-day-within-pair'`, `analysis_cluster_unit='batch-day'`, and `paired_analysis_block='crossover_group_id'`;
- seed randomizes order **within frozen pair**, not arbitrary shapes after outcomes;
- hard constraints yield `UNSAT`, never silent relaxation;
- candidate/template/treatment assignment cannot depend on post-freeze observations;
- deterministic replay from same frozen state/seed.
- six UTC slot classes remain the frozen design inputs under one frozen `time_window_schedule_seed`; constrained allocation balances them subject to hard constraints;
- equal imbalance solutions use lexicographically smallest canonical serialized schedule (ordered date, slot, shape, pair ID);
- seed is an input, not a post-hoc tie-break candidate;
- no optional region/local-time soft objective appears unless explicitly frozen.

Tag every crossover batch with `crossover_group_id`, optional `crossover_block_id`, `crossover_period (1/2)`, `pair_role=control/alternative`, `window_shape`, `actual_window_hours`, and `stop_reason`. A reserved period-2 anchor replay is explicitly `anchor_draw_type='PAIR_REPLAY'` and `anchor_replay=true`; it consumes no new anchor/region draw.

For each `2×2h` parent treatment, persist one parent `experiment_day_id`/`parent_batch_id` and two child rows carrying exact `segment_id`, `segment_start`, `segment_end`, `gap_start`, and `gap_end` fields. Delete/settle segment-1 subscriptions before the gap; no subscription remains active in the gap. Build population independently for each half-open child interval; gap-only captures are `captured_outside_population=true`. Attribute item costs to the receiving child segment and shared setup costs to the parent under the frozen allocation rule.

The current 26/3/2 SAT fixture is useful unit evidence. The **final calendar is not generated until Phase 5 with real frozen inputs.**

#### 1.5.13 Phase 0M — R7 manifest, split rule, evaluation guard, model instrumentation, and marginal-value instrumentation
