- manifest completeness is schema-validated;
- preprobe record embedded unchanged later;
- contains current code/builder SHAs, account/channel/budgets, provider pin, frame/reference hashes, Gate-measured frozen values, calendar, scheduler/adaptation, split rule, security/retention/history/weather readiness, endpoints/claim/eval settings;
- incomplete manifest refuses FREEZE/start.

**Split/evaluation guard now:**

Before Phase 6 freeze the **assignment rule**, not nonexistent row IDs:

```text
TRAIN run_day_index 1–20
VALIDATION 21–25
TEST 26–31
test_holdout_gap_batch_days = 0
```

Store group/block rules, chronological ordering, seed, and `split_rule_hash`.

After Phase 6 but before first tuning, apply the frozen rule to actual rows, materialize protected Engine-A test row IDs, compute `test_row_hash`, and make that test set read-only.

**Model ladder instrumentation that must remain possible without redefining Month 1:**

- Model −1 persistence baseline;
- Model 0 calendar/seasonal baseline;
- Model 1 tabular XGBoost;
- Model 2 = Model 1 + weather;
- Model 3 = cross-sectional network;
- Model 4 = temporal/network rollout/GNN hypothesis;
- Model 5 = rotations/chains;
- Model 6 = event/disruption signals only after the deferred event source/taxonomy is frozen;
- Model 7 = later conformal/uncertainty stage.

Month 1 does not turn Models 4–7 into mandatory deployed winners; the primary comparison remains Model 1 vs Model −1.

**Evaluation code must already exist/test before collection for:**

- Engine A chronological primary;
- Engine B — unseen airport;
- Engine C — unseen region;
- Engine D — unseen tail;
- Engine R — unseen route/OD;
- Engine P — population audit;
- POST grouped by flight + chronology;
- Engine E deferred until event taxonomy/source frozen;
- leakage refusal;
- no tuning on test;
- mechanism-ablation metadata;
- state-age/staleness curve inputs;
- info-per-credit vectors;
- actual resource vector `(Alert_credits, REST_API_units)`.

**Primary claim preregistration support:**

- Model 1 XGBoost vs Model −1 persistence;
- Engine A;
- T−6h headline;
- selected primary target only if Gate 0.5 constructible;
- MAE;
- practical delta ≥2 minutes;
- 95% calendar-day block bootstrap as pilot uncertainty;
- decision classification fixed before protected read.

**Collection-mechanism ablation metadata must support the frozen sequence:**

- A = all features;
- B = remove coverage-age / notification-count / capture / observation-density / sampling-strategy / subscription metadata;
- C = additionally remove airport identity;
- D = additionally remove graph connectivity.

The Month-1 mechanism comparison may include train-4h → test-2×2h. The event-regime arm stays deferred with Engine E until event membership is source-grounded/frozen.

**Staleness curve:** compute `state_age = prediction_cutoff - last_observation_timestamp` using the frozen buckets (10m, 30m, 1h, 3h, 6h, 12h, 24h, 48h, …). Do not treat staleness as harmless by default.

No result/winner is written into manifest before observation.

### 1.6 Phase 1 — Gate 0: account, channel, budget, and cost identity

**Plan ownership:** §3 + §16 Gate 0 + §17 steps 5–9.

**Prerequisite:** Phase-0 closure PASS and reviewed.

**Purpose:** verify the already-implemented refusal/budget code against the actual subscribed account. Gate 0 is not “check balance”; it freezes the identity and feasible budget trees needed to admit later work.

**Current repository owners to inspect/use:**

| Responsibility | Current observed owner | Phase-1 rule |
|---|---|---|
| live account/balance/subscription evidence | `scripts/gate0_evidence_gather.ts` + AeroDataBox client | current real `v39:gate0:inspect`; repair semantics if incomplete |
| budget arithmetic/report | `scripts/gate0_budget_report.ts`, `budgetAccounting_v3.ts` | must implement current two-ledger exact identities |
| REST/Alert provider client | `aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts` | all account/provider calls pass central limiter/authorization |
| gate state | `gates_v3.ts` | Gate-0 PASS derives from complete evidence, not a balance-only boolean |
| evidence/meta | manifest/config/evidence owners | Gate-0 artifact is versioned/hashable; final manifest is still later |

No new Gate-0 script should be created unless these existing owners cannot be repaired cleanly.

#### 1.6.1 Gate-0 evidence

Record:

1. active subscribed plan; user-confirmed 60,000-unit entitlement still applicable;
2. exact `subscription_channel` (RapidAPI/API.Market/direct/custom);
3. account/plan identifier;
4. billing-cycle start/end;
5. REST/API units already used and remaining;
6. current settled non-expiring Alert-credit balance;
7. refill history;
8. current refill conversion and per-refill/balance caps;
9. current FIDS endpoint tier/unit cost for actual channel/account;
10. current rate limit/account mechanics;
11. current active subscription inventory;
12. protected Alert floor ≥1,000;
13. proposed ending Alert margin;
14. pre-smoke unsettled-burst admission reserve;
15. protected API floor and ending API margin;
16. all seven REST categories;
17. exact `pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling`, generated **before the first paid action** as the sum of separately authorized ceilings for safety smoke, all frozen Stage-1 candidates/replacements, all possible Stage-2 confirmations/replacements, Gate 3, Gate 0.5, and the small Gate-4 live check;
18. actual feasible `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling = min(57,900, verified balance-tree remainder)`;
19. `unallocated_alert_credits` explicitly non-spendable unless reauthorized/frozen;
20. Gate-0 artifact hash.

#### 1.6.2 Budget identities to prove

**Never combine the resource types.**

```text
ALERT CREDITS:
opening_nonexpiring_alert_balance + authorized_alert_refill_credits
  = pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling
  + phase6_alert_spend_ceiling
  + protected_alert_floor
  + ending_alert_margin
  + unallocated_alert_credits

API UNITS, per billing cycle:
cycle_entitlement_units
  = api_units_consumed_before_freeze
  + authorized_alert_refill_units
  + FIDS_BASE_UNITS
  + FIDS_SPLIT_UNITS
  + FIDS_RETRY_UNIT_BUDGET
  + VALIDATION_UNIT_BUDGET
  + OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET
  + HISTORY_BOOTSTRAP_UNIT_BUDGET
  + DIAGNOSTIC_UNIT_BUDGET
  + protected_api_floor_units
  + ending_api_margin_units
  + unallocated_api_units
```

`protected_rest_budget` is the exact sum of the seven named REST category caps. For Phase 6, `budget_day_id=run_day_index` for the owning parent batch-day; it is not a provider UTC-day or billing-cycle-reset concept.

The 57,900 number is a **maximum design ceiling**, not a required spend and not `60,000−something` shorthand.

#### 1.6.3 Gate-0 mutation rule

If authoritative evidence cannot establish refill conversion/caps without a refill mutation, **STOP** and request a separately scoped human authorization for the minimum permitted test mutation. The Plan is not that authorization.

**PASS:** complete current account/channel/budget artifact reconciles and all admission margins/reserves are known.

**FAIL/BLOCKED:** unknown channel, cycle, unit balance, cost, cap, floor, reserve, or unexplained balance difference.

**Next:** Phase 2 prerequisite P.

#### 1.7.3 Phase 2C — freeze traffic/region reference rules

After Gate 1 PASS:

Use the frozen rules + current Gate-1 coverage:

Before safety smoke/Stage 1 freeze:

This record is hash-locked before paid Stage 1 and later embedded unchanged into the Phase-5 manifest.

#### 1.7.6 Phase 2F — mandatory pre-probe safety smoke

This is **not Gate 3**.

Exact run-specific AUTH names airport/filter/window, max Alert credits, allowed REST category units, start/expiry, stop owner, artifact hash.

The production path must prove:

- intended owned subscription only;
- `maxDeliveryRetries=0`;
- >0 item;
- raw-before-2xx;
- current canonical identity/codeshare bounds;
- internal cost ledger;
- ≥3-read stable settlement;
- exact external/internal reconciliation;
- zero delivery failures;
- owned subscription deletion/inactivity;
- no foreign billable subscription;
- provisional measured unsettled-burst reserve.

`FAIL`, `BLOCKED`, or materially inadequate evidence → no Stage 1.

**Mandatory handoff:** STOP after smoke settlement/report and return before Stage 1.

#### 1.7.7 Phase 2G — Gate 2 Stage 1

Run the frozen 12-candidate/replacement protocol sequentially:

- candidate/window comes from hash, not manual choice;
- target 2h;
- cap-censored;
- cumulative 500-credit probe budget day;
- each probe settles/closes before next;
- canonical unique flights/items/chain metrics;
- capacity separately checked;
- no update/retry counted as a new flight;
- score uses frozen exogenous terms + permitted observed-yield term;
- incomplete/censored exposure explicitly reported with `probe_start_utc`, `probe_end_utc`, `probe_duration_minutes`, and `duration_censored=true` where applicable.

Stop on cap mismatch, settlement unresolved, foreign sub, AUTH expiry, provider change, or ranking-relevant insufficient sample.

After valid Stage-1 set, compute deterministic ranking/promotion.

#### 1.7.8 Phase 2H — Gate 2 Stage 2

- only valid Stage-1 promoted airports can enter;
- target 4h;
- same frozen rules;
- sequential confirmations/replacements;
- each fully settles;
- lock final five only after all valid evidence;
- rank-invariance/replacement evidence retained.

**Gate-2 PASS:** final-five pool + scores + replacements + exposure + settlement all valid.

**Mandatory handoff:** return before Phase 3 / Gate 3.

#### 1.8.1 Gate 3 — official isolated credit canary

**Purpose:** prove provider SEND-side money accounting matches internal owned item accounting on the real production path.

Before start:

- Gate 2 PASS;
- freeze and record a small `canary_alert_credit_ceiling` and fixed `canary_duration_seconds`; do not assume a one-credit canary;
- prior paid work settled/closed;
- R1 subscription inventory clean;
- exact Gate-3 AUTH;
- current hashes unchanged.

PASS requires:

```text
>0 items
raw-before-2xx PASS
delivery_failures = 0
shared ≥3 equal stable balance reads PASS
C_external = balance_before - balance_stable
C_internal = owned immutable item/attempt ledger
C_external == C_internal
tolerance = 0
owned cleanup/inactivity PASS
no foreign billable subscription
```

Return before Gate 0.5.

#### 1.8.2 Gate 0.5 — dedicated provider/content/clock/T/target/cadence pilot

This is a separate adequately sampled pilot, not “inspect the canary payload.”

Before seeing results freeze minimums:

- `min_notifications`;
- `min_unique_flights`;
- `min_completed_flights`;
- `min_airborne_flights`;
- `min_airborne_points`;
- `min_pilot_duration_minutes`.

One airport, one frozen short pilot window, same production path.

Measure/verify:

1. complete canonical provider clock taxonomy;
2. movement semantics and actual-vs-estimated guard;
3. requested-airport primary vs opposite-leg context;
4. FIDS boundary inclusivity/truncation/range behavior;
5. codeshare behavior, including a matched `withCodeshared=true` versus `withCodeshared=false` comparison for the same airport/window to quantify ambiguity and prove canonicalization does not over-merge distinct physical flights;
6. selected T constructibility;
7. selected primary target constructibility;
8. terminal actual-milestone evidence;
9. censoring-grace distribution;
10. population acquisition guard: measure FIDS response latency plus scheduler/queue/rate-limit allowance and an explicit safety margin; replay at least one response whose durable availability crosses a cutoff;
11. population refresh cadence: characterize schedule appearance/revision timing on a predeclared sample and choose the cadence subject to REST budget/provider limits, never outcomes/yield;
12. population staleness ceiling: predefine the maximum age of the latest qualifying FIDS population observation and test an older-only observation case;
13. airborne cadence/gaps/completeness;
14. minimum usable points, frozen as `airborne_usable_min_points`;
15. trajectory duration/completeness threshold;
16. recovery latency;
17. webhook 10-second/contract behavior for actual channel;
18. `target_cadence_seconds`, `min_acceptable_cadence_seconds`, and `max_gap_seconds`;
19. `min_trajectory_duration_seconds`, `completeness_threshold_%`, and the exact completeness formula;
20. `cadence_warning_rule` and `cadence_fail_rule`;
21. `censoring_grace_minutes` from verified-actual terminal latency + margin;
22. `population_acquisition_guard_minutes`, `population_refresh_cadence_minutes`, and `population_max_staleness_minutes`.

Outcomes:

- `PASS`: all checks + minimums pass; freeze measured values.
- `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`: no failure but minimum unmet; **do not freeze measured values**.
- `FAIL/BLOCKED`: semantics/durability/leakage/target constructibility failure.

No forced gate-out/wheels-off fallback. If no accepted T/primary target construct exists, Phase 6 remains blocked.

Return before Gate 4.

#### 1.8.3 Gate 4 — cap/failure/reliability

Offline Phase-0 tests already must prove:

- injectable cap 100;
- margin 10;
- expected soft stop 90;
- hard cap 100;
- production arithmetic `1900−50=1850`;
- SEND charged but webhook unreachable;
- unsettled burst;
- raw-persistence failure;
- second-start refusal.

Gate 4 then performs only a **small authorized live reconciliation/second-start check**. Never spend toward 1,900 merely to prove the cap.

PASS requires no reconciliation/failure anomaly and correct pause/refusal.

**If a settled Phase-6-style budget-day overshoot is observed during validated operation:** record `hard_cap_overshoot`; the deviation is never erased. The compensating rule reduces the next budget-day authorization to `max(0,1900-overshoot_amount)` and recomputes run remainder. Overshoot >100 credits requires project PAUSE + human intervention. This is a compensating control, not proof the cap cannot be breached.

Return before Gate 5.

### 1.9 Phase 4 — Gate 5 population-observability validation


**Repository owners:** reuse `fidsCensus_v3.ts`, PRE snapshot builder, outcome terminalizer, population tables, and gate/evidence services. The canonical `v39:gate5:population` is currently a stub; if no existing population-validation orchestrator exists after Phase-0 discovery, create a thin `scripts/gate5_population.ts` that computes the independent funnel from production data definitions rather than inventing a new denominator.

**Plan ownership:** §5 population/funnels + §16/§17 steps 17–19.

For a frozen sample of airport service intervals:

1. fetch role-aware FIDS/schedule under protected API budget;
2. build append-only `flight_population`;
3. independently compute:
   - population;
   - horizon eligibility;
   - expected snapshots;
   - created snapshots;
   - webhook captured;
   - feature completeness/missingness;
   - captured inside population;
   - captured outside population;
   - target-applicable rows;
   - observed outcomes;
4. verify `captured_in_population ≤ population_total`;
5. investigate outside-population captures rather than deleting them;
6. where available in US, spot-check against FAA/BTS-derived operational/schedule reference;
7. report AIRBORNE nested funnel separately;
8. report results as “population relative to validated AeroDataBox-supported operational frame” unless independently validated;
9. report every PRE numerator/denominator by horizon/airport/tier/region: population, horizon eligible, snapshot expected/created, webhook captured, required-feature complete, optional-feature missing, target applicable/observed;
10. zero denominator → rate NULL + reason, never zero;
11. report coverage-age distribution by tier/region;
12. report the AIRBORNE flight-level funnel as `eligible → observed → usable → complete → snapshot_eligible → labeled`, each transition both against the prior stage and against `airborne_eligible`.

**Critical principle:** webhook capture is parallel; it never controls whether a PRE snapshot should exist.

**PASS:** denominator construction and independent funnel evidence are coherent.

Return before Phase 5.

**Repository owners:** reuse `experimentCalendar_v3.ts` + `scripts/calendar_solve.ts`, repaired `manifest_v3.ts`, registry/traceability/dictionary/lineage owners, budget ledger, security/retention service, and snapshot/evaluation split owner. The current `v39:freeze` is a stub; if no freeze orchestrator exists, create one thin `scripts/freeze_v39.ts` that validates/writes the repaired manifest service and refuses incomplete evidence.

Phase 5 does not redesign code. It verifies measured inputs and freezes the exact executable contract.

#### 1.10.1 Reverify readiness

Reverify current:

- provider Terms/Plan/channel;
- retention/security lifecycle;
- traffic/region reference hashes;
- preprobe record hash unchanged;
- final five anchor pool;
- Gate-0.5 selected T/target/cadence/grace/acquisition values;
- history infrastructure ready;
- weather operational sources/retention ready;
- outcomes/recovery budgets;
- all seven REST category budgets;
- protected Alert/API floors/margins;
- code/schema/config hashes;
- zero open P0/P1 implementation blockers.

#### 1.10.2 Materialize exact REST/API-unit calendar budget

From the **final** 31-batch-day calendar + frozen max FIDS range + acquisition grid:

- enumerate every base FIDS request;
- enumerate split requests;
- reserve retry budget;
- validation budget;
- outcome budget;
- history bootstrap budget;
- diagnostics budget;
- include every physical attempt;
- require total ≤ protected API allocation.

No hand estimate is budget proof.

#### 1.10.3 Solve and hash final calendar

Use the solver with real frozen inputs:

- exact 26/3/2 shape;
- five matched pairs;
- same-airport/time/weekday/split hard constraints;
- ≥24h washout;
- split containment;
- no relaxed UNSAT;
- deterministic seed/replay;
- run dates may span >31 wall-clock days.

Earlier SAT fixture is not this final artifact.

#### 1.10.4 Freeze split-assignment rule

Freeze:

- TRAIN day-index 1–20;
- VALIDATION 21–25;
- TEST 26–31;
- chronological/blocking/group rules by engine;
- seed;
- `split_rule_hash`.

Do **not** materialize test row IDs before rows exist.

#### 1.10.5 Write final manifest

Manifest embeds:

- unchanged preprobe reference record;
- account/channel/budget trees;
- provider pin, including `fids_api_version`, `fids_docs_date`, `openapi_sha256`, subscribed channel identity, and live-frozen `fids_max_range_for_actual_plan`;
- frame/reference;
- five anchors;
- scheduler/adaptation/crossover;
- history/weather;
- T/target/OOOI mapping;
- cadence/grace/acquisition;
- endpoint hierarchy;
- primary claim;
- split rule;
- code/builder/schema/config hashes;
- gate evidence IDs;
- security/retention;
- all MEASURE→FREEZE values, including every frozen field from Plan §§4.1/4.2/4.5/5.1–5.4/6.0/6.3–6.6/7.1–7.5/8.2–8.8/9.1–9.2/10.1–10.2/12.2.1–12.2.2/13.2–13.6;
- `history_infrastructure_ready`, `historical_feature_sources_verified`, `weather_operational_sources_verified`, `weather_as_known_at_cutoff_test_pass`, and `retention_terms_verified` with evidence hash/time;
- `phase6_start_date`; changing it after FREEZE requires re-FREEZE because it affects billing-cycle alignment, weather/archive coverage, history bootstrap, and evaluation dates;
- actual allowed Phase-6 Alert ceiling;
- REST budgets.

Every field evidence-derived. Missing required value → FREEZE refuses.

**FREEZE PASS does not start Phase 6.**

**Mandatory handoff:** return with exact manifest hash before Phase 6 authorization.

---

### 1.11 Phase 6 — separately authorized frozen 31-batch-day collection


**Repository owners:** the current `adbCollectionController_v3.ts` should remain the production controller if it can be repaired to consume the frozen calendar/manifest and shared accounting services. Do not create a second collection controller casually. The current `v39:phase6:start`, `status`, and `pause` entries are stubs; map them to thin wrappers around the repaired controller/state/evidence services. `pause` must be independently callable even when start prerequisites fail.

Phase 6 starts only when:

- Gates 0–5 PASS;
- Phase-0 closure remains valid;
- P/security/retention current;
- final calendar VALID;
- final manifest/FREEZE PASS;
- `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT` currently OFF;
- separate human AUTH names exact manifest hash, run, start, ceilings, owner.

**Start transition:** only the authorized production start command may transition OFF → ON. Any pause/failure makes it OFF again.

#### 1.11.1 Per batch-day pre-start checks

Before every parent budget day:

- previous day fully settled/closed;
- owned subscriptions inactive/deleted;
- current manifest/code/config/provider identity still matches;
- budget-day `run_day_index` correct;
- Alert remaining + reservations + frozen unsettled reserve fit 1,900 and run total;
- REST category calls fit category/total budgets;
- exact planned airport set/window/treatment from calendar;
- no foreign billable sub;
- cleanup/pause path available.

#### 1.11.2 Runtime

- execute hash-frozen shape/calendar;
- all alerts `maxDeliveryRetries=0`;
- SEND-aware watchdog;
- raw-before-2xx;
- append-only population/events/snapshots/outcomes;
- non-adaptive population acquisition grid;
- frozen adaptation logic;
- no model tuning/protected-test inspection;
- daily evidence;
- weekly data-quality/info-per-credit diagnostics;
- monthly airborne-cadence remeasurement as evidence, not adaptive redesign;
- persist per-batch `environmental_context_json` and the underlying weather/ATC/storm source fields; crossover matching never implies identical environment;
- adaptation updates only from completed valid Phase-6 observations; probe yields never seed `m_i`/EMA/zero-yield state;
- no next budget day starts while the prior budget day is unsettled.

#### 1.11.3 Required Phase-6 diagnostics without model tuning

Weekly diagnostics and the Month-1 report must retain at minimum:

```text
Alert credits consumed
REST/API units by category
unique flights / airports
new airports / routes / tails
new routes / credit
tier and region shares
PRE snapshots / credit
POST airborne observations / credit
delay events and severe delays ≥60 / ≥120 min
tail-missing rate
route-direction coverage
coverage-age distribution by tier/region
tail observations
consecutive tail pairs
3-leg chains
4+-leg chains
scheduled_chain_coverage
observable_chain_link_rate
tail-chain links / credit
hour spread / time-class coverage
window shape / sampling strategy / reason
```

For window-regime comparisons report 4h, 2×2h, and **up-to-6h** separately: unique flights/credit, tails/credit, chain-links/credit, 3-leg/4+-leg chains, routes/credit, PRE snapshots/credit, POST observations/outcomes/credit, hour uniformity, coverage-age, and environmental context.

These are data-quality/collection diagnostics. They may not perform feature selection, hyperparameter selection, protected-test inspection, or model-policy adaptation during Phase 6.

#### 1.11.4 Batch close

- stop/delete/inactivate owned subscriptions;
- shared ≥3-read settlement;
- external/internal reconciliation;
- final actual cost;
- all artifacts/hashes;
- no next budget day until closed.

Any `FAIL`, `MISMATCH`, provider/config drift, settlement unresolved, unsafe budget state → pause, set auto collection OFF, write report, return.

---

### 1.12 Phase 7 — post-collection protected materialization and Month-1 evaluation


**Repository context:** current `scripts/` contains descriptive/data-analysis utilities such as `analyze_flight_data_pre_post.py`; these are inspection/reuse candidates, not automatically the canonical protected evaluation pipeline. Discover current model/dataset code first. If no single compliant orchestrator exists, create one thin evaluation CLI only after mapping the already-tested snapshot, split, model, and metric services.

**Phase 7 is not when ETL is invented.** Snapshot/evaluation builders had to be production-wired/tested in Phase 0.

Order:

1. close Phase-6 collection and final accounting;
2. apply frozen split-assignment rule to actual rows;
3. materialize Engine-A protected TEST rows (days 26–31);
4. hash test row IDs and make read-only;
5. TRAIN/VALIDATION only for feature/model/hyperparameter decisions;
6. model selection before protected TEST read;
7. one protected primary pilot read;
8. report all preregistered secondary/exploratory outputs.

Required Month-1 families:

- Model −1 persistence;
- Model 1 XGBoost;
- Engine A primary;
- B unseen airport;
- C unseen region;
- D unseen tail;
- R unseen route;
- P population audit;
- POST airborne;
- Engine E deferred.

**Primary protected pilot rule:**

- selected primary target is frozen only if Gate 0.5 proves it constructible;
- headline horizon = T−6h;
- primary metric = MAE;
- `delta_MAE = MAE_Model−1 − MAE_Model1`;
- `positive` requires point `delta_MAE ≥ 2 min` and 95% day-block-bootstrap lower bound >0;
- `negative` requires point `delta_MAE ≤ −2 min` and upper bound <0;
- all other outcomes = `neutral/inconclusive`;
- the six protected test batch-days are pilot evidence, not confirmatory/deployment validation.

**Rolling-origin/test chronology:**

- no model tuning during Phase 6;
- after Phase 6 and before tuning, materialize/hash the protected TEST rows from `run_day_index=26..31`;
- rolling-origin development uses only non-test days 1–25 and is frozen before first fit/outcome-driven tuning;
- final protected TEST is read once after model selection;
- `test_holdout_gap_batch_days=0` means no omitted batch-day index, but report actual wall-clock gap caused by washout/calendar spacing.

Required reporting:

- MAE/RMSE;
- ECE;
- Brier selected delay >15/>60;
- interval coverage/width;
- tail performance;
- POST ETA error vs remaining time;
- 95% block-bootstrap pilot uncertainty;
- mechanism ablation;
- staleness curve;
- population/capture/snapshot/outcome funnels;
- cost/sample learning curves: cost-indexed Alert checkpoints may use 2k/5k/10k/20k/30k/40k/50k and the actual frozen Phase-6 ceiling when reached; sample-indexed checkpoints are frozen before first fit from the realized domain;
- every learning curve reports `N_unique_flights`, `N_PRE_snapshots`, `N_POST_snapshots`, `N_airborne_points`, `Alert_credits`, and `REST_API_units`; never equate rows/flights/credits;
- marginal value per **actual credit/resource vector**, not per flight; preserve `MV_data = ΔM / Δcredits` separately from feature-ablation contribution;
- negative MV allowed;
- randomized vs observational comparisons labeled honestly.


**Analysis settings/deadlines:**

- 1000 block-bootstrap replicates and 95% interval;
- ECE = 15 equal-width bins;
- staleness buckets = 10m/30m/1h/3h/6h/12h/24h/48h;
- learning-curve fit form `a·n⁻ᵇ + c` only over the actually observed domain; extrapolation is labeled predicted/unmeasured with uncertainty;
- XGBoost missing strategy `missing_indicator + median` unless a pre-fit frozen model-specific rule supersedes it;
- Engine-B/C/D/R holdout details and rolling-origin intervals are frozen before first model fit without reading protected outcomes;
- conformal is deferred to Model 7+; Month 1 uses 90% quantile baseline coverage/width;
- classify every analysis setting as `frozen` or `DEFERRED_BUT_FREEZE_BEFORE_ANALYSIS`; deferred settings must be fixed before any validation/test outcome is read.

All Month-1 results are labeled **early operational pilot**, not deployment validation.

### 1.13 Mandatory handoff schedule

1. **CURRENT:** finish Phase 0 → aggregate preflight → write report → STOP before Gate 0.
2. After Gate 0: return if any identity/budget/channel item is partial/changed; no paid action until P.
3. **After safety smoke:** mandatory return before Stage 1.
4. During Stage 1: immediate return on FAIL, ranking-relevant insufficient sample, cap mismatch, provider change, cleanup failure, settlement unresolved, or AUTH expiry.
5. After Stage-1 ranking: return before Stage 2 when promotion/replacement/rank decision needs review.
6. After Gate 2: return before Gate 3.
7. After each Gate 3, Gate 0.5, Gate 4, Gate 5: return before next gate.
8. **After FREEZE:** mandatory return before Phase-6 authorization.
9. During Phase 6: immediate return on pause/failure/mismatch/drift.
10. After Phase 6: return before protected-test/model evaluation if any closure/split integrity issue exists.

There is no arbitrary “come back within N minutes” rule. State, settlement, frozen time class, authorization expiry, and hash continuity control validity.

### 1.15 Universal prohibited actions — active implementation guardrail

Do **not**:

- treat a document/file/test name as proof of production wiring;
- run a paid/live command because a script exists;
- use `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` as the current unpatched controller's shell-safe value; use `0` until fixed/tested;
- allow missing env to imply auto collection ON;
- allow experimental retries >0;
- acknowledge webhook success before required durable raw commit;
- use local stored-row count as authoritative provider spend;
- use UTC calendar day as the Phase-6 budget-day identity;
- use two balance reads as settlement;
- use Gate-3 reconciliation tolerance 3;
- treat missing traffic as REGIONAL;
- use unstated ICAO-region fallback;
- derive T from an unverified FAA alias;
- create a new flight identity merely because a retime is ≥2h/date-shifted;
- use webhook capture to decide snapshot existence;
- backfill earlier population/snapshot state from future retrieval;
- use retrospective weather to repair operational missingness or silently backfill missing weather from a later/revised source;
- auto-stamp `sampling_weight=1/p`;
- equate rows/flights/credits;
- combine Alert credits and REST units;
- let probe results seed Phase-6 adaptive initial state;
- choose probe airports manually outside frozen shortlist/replacements;
- auto-extend an inadequate/failed paid run;
- deliberately spend to the 1,900 cap to test it;
- treat FREEZE as Phase-6 authorization;
- tune models or inspect protected outcomes during Phase 6;
- create parallel wrappers/services that duplicate a current production owner merely because the old owner is messy.
- start another V3.10/V3.11 theoretical redesign while f.8 execution closure remains the task;
- claim a six-day/slot pattern is automatically unbiased or population-representative;
- claim pilot 4h statistically “beats” 2×2h as a universal result;
- claim 31 batch-days establishes seasonality;
- claim an observational +1 MID/REGIONAL/day caused a universal model improvement;
- run REST-based airborne monitoring before Gate-0.5 cadence/semantics approval;
- merge PRE and AIRBORNE rows into one modeling population;
- treat window end as proof of “no outcome” without censoring/grace;
- run manual Rescore/Simulate/Rescore-all billable UI actions during Phase 6;
- treat one empty/stale observation as proof an airport is useless;
- claim crossover matching makes environmental context disappear;
- **No GNN-first** Month-1 redesign; do not enable large event-sampling, IPW, advanced intervention optimization, or long-season claims outside the frozen Month-1 scope.
- **No REST-airborne monitoring** before Gate 0.5 has measured webhook cadence/coverage and a separately frozen need/budget authorizes it.

## 2. Replit / shell-command manual — exact operator timeline matched to §1

**Purpose:** §1 tells the AI/human **what must be built and why**. §2 tells you **what to type in Replit and when**. This is the only active shell-command authority.

**Critical current-repository warning:** at inspected commit `5bffdafce6ef152a665eb3b22f735d6cf394491f`, the controller considers `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT` enabled unless its string value is exactly `"0"`. Therefore, until §1.5.1 is fixed and tested, use:

```bash
export ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=0
```

Do **not** rely on `export ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` with that unpatched code.

### 2.1 Current canonical command trust table at inspected GitHub commit

#### 2.1.1 Real mapping exists — still must be semantically verified

```text
v39:test:offline       → vitest run tests/
v39:test:full          → vitest run
v39:typecheck          → tsc --noEmit
v39:scanner            → scripts/v39_preflight_consistency.ts
v39:gate0:inspect      → scripts/gate0_evidence_gather.ts
v39:gate1:coverage     → scripts/measure_coverage.ts
v39:frame:build        → scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts
v39:probe:stage1       → scripts/anchor_probe.ts --stage 1
v39:probe:stage2       → scripts/anchor_probe.ts --stage 2
v39:gate3:canary       → scripts/credit_canary.ts
v39:calendar:solve     → scripts/calendar_solve.ts
```

A real mapping is not a PASS claim; some mapped scripts still encode legacy assumptions and must be repaired under §1.

#### 2.1.2 Current broken mapping — repair before use

```text
build / v39:build → package.json currently points to nonexistent `script/build.ts`
actual file       → `scripts/build.ts`
current builder   → currently targets nonexistent `server2/index.ts`
```

Therefore `npm run build` / `npm run v39:build` is **not a trusted Phase-0 PASS check until Phase 0A repairs the package path and production server entrypoint and the repaired build exits 0**. The existing builder is intended to emit `dist/index.mjs` plus an `index.cjs` wrapper; the extension pair itself is not the defect.

#### 2.1.3 Current `echo` stubs — may not close anything

v39:repo-intake
v39:migrate:check
v39:lint

`v39:preflight` currently duplicates the scanner and is **not** aggregate preflight.

The coding agent fixes these during Phase 0. Until then, do not type a stub and interpret exit 0 as success.

### 2.2 Before touching code in every Replit session

Run from repository root:

```bash
set -euo pipefail

# SAFETY for the CURRENT UNPATCHED controller:
export ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=0

printf 'repo=%s\n' "$(pwd)"
printf 'utc=%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
printf 'ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=%s\n' "${ADB_AUTO_COLLECT:-<unset>}"

git branch --show-current
git rev-parse HEAD
git status --short
git remote -v | sed -E 's#(https?://)[^/@]+@#\1<redacted>@#g'

node --version
npm --version

# See every current V3.9 mapping.
npm run 2>&1 | grep -E 'v39:' || true

# Confirm current GitHub-inspected commit relationship.
git log -1 --oneline
git diff --check

# Runtime/build entrypoint consistency:
printf '\n--- Replit/package runtime entries ---\n'
grep -nE 'deployment|run =|build =' .replit || true
node -e 'const p=require("./package.json"); console.log({build:p.scripts?.build,start:p.scripts?.start,v39build:p.scripts?.["v39:build"]})'
test -f script/build.ts && echo 'script/build.ts PRESENT' || echo 'script/build.ts MISSING (current package path)'
test -f scripts/build.ts && echo 'scripts/build.ts PRESENT (actual observed builder)' || echo 'scripts/build.ts MISSING'
grep -nE 'entryPoints|outfile' scripts/build.ts 2>/dev/null || true
```

**STOP before `npm run dev` if:**

- `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT` is not exactly `0` while §1.5.1 is unpatched;
- worktree contains changes you do not understand;
- branch/HEAD differs from the evidence report and the AI has not reconciled it;
- an automatic-collection startup path cannot be proven OFF.

### 2.3 Phase 0 — repository intake and blocker inventory

Until `v39:repo-intake` becomes real, use this manual read-only intake:

```bash
set -euo pipefail
export ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=0

git branch --show-current
git rev-parse HEAD
git status --short
git log -5 --oneline

printf '\n--- V3.9 package scripts ---\n'
node -e '
const p=require("./package.json");
for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(p.scripts||{})) {
  if (k.startsWith("v39:")) console.log(`${k} = ${v}`);
}
'

printf '\n--- critical production files ---\n'
find server/lib/disruption scripts tests migrations -maxdepth 2 -type f \
  | sort \
  | grep -Ei 'v3|fids|raw|snapshot|outcome|terminal|history|weather|calendar|budget|gate|manifest|probe|canary|trace|registry|lineage|migration' \
  || true

printf '\n--- unsafe/stale patterns ---\n'
grep -RIn --exclude-dir=node_modules --exclude-dir=.git \
  -E 'ADB_AUTO_COLLECT|maxDeliveryRetries|reconcileTolerance|ADB_RECONCILE_TOLERANCE|tol.?=.?3|UTC day|tierForIcao|REGIONAL|availableAt: null|payloadSha256: null|AugMDnotes|f\.9' \
  server scripts tests package.json .replit 2>/dev/null || true
```

Save output to the new Phase-0 evidence/run report.

### 2.4 Phase 0 work-package loop — use this after every code change

Do not wait until the end to discover breakage.

```bash

git diff --check
git diff --stat

# Run the specific test file(s) for the owner changed:
npx vitest run <target-test-file-1> [<target-test-file-2> ...]

# Then current regression:
npm run v39:test:full

# Typecheck every work package; nonzero remains a blocker:
npm run v39:typecheck
```

If typecheck is currently broadly broken, the coding agent may use targeted diagnostics while repairing it, but **Phase 0 does not exit until the full command returns 0**.

### 2.18 What to do when any Phase-0 command fails

| Failure | Immediate action | Next allowed action |
|---|---|---|
| targeted test FAIL | fix mapped production owner or corrected fixture | rerun same targeted test |
| stale test expects superseded Plan behavior | update production behavior + test to binding Plan | rerun targeted + full suite |
| missing TEST ID | implement offline fixture/failure case | targeted → offline suite |
| no disposable DB | build test DB harness; do not use production DB casually | migration check only |
| typecheck nonzero | fix errors; remain Phase-0 blocked | rerun typecheck |
| lint/build nonzero | fix | rerun failed command |
| registry/traceability missing | map owner/test/evidence | rerun check |
| scanner conflict | create `DOC_CONFLICT`; reconcile to Plan | scanner |
| preflight failure | read exact aggregated failed prerequisite | that prerequisite only |
| unexpected provider call | STOP; record incident/cost; disable V3.9 provider path | no external command until reviewed |
| auto-collection starts unexpectedly | immediately pause/disable, set env `0`, capture subscriptions/balance/logs, write incident | no dev/live action until root cause fixed |

<a id="sec-3"></a>
## 3. Code and scripts — required production responsibilities + last-known inspection targets

### 3.1 Required production component and script map

Repository discovery decides the exact paths, but the following responsibilities are mandatory first-class production components. Reuse a proven existing module when it truly owns the responsibility; otherwise create a clearly named component. The requirement matrix records the final path/function.

| Responsibility | Required production owner | Required script/CLI owner | Minimum proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| Raw webhook ingress/auth/persist-before-2xx | webhook route + raw ingress service | fixture/replay command | TEST-006/007 + integration artifact |
| FIDS population acquisition/range/DST/retry | provider client + FIDS census service | `v39:gate5:population`, frame inputs | TEST-001–004 |
| Canonical flight identity/codeshare/retime | identity service | identity fixture runner | TEST-008 |
| OOOI/milestone/T mapping | milestone normalizer | Gate-0.5 pilot analyzer | TEST-009 |
| PRE snapshots | snapshot builder | offline materializer/replay | TEST-011/013/014 |
| AIRBORNE points/trajectory/snapshots | airborne event + trajectory pipeline | cadence/trajectory diagnostics | TEST-012 + cadence cases |
| Outcome terminalization/recovery | outcome service + recovery scheduler | recovery fixture/runner | TEST-015 |
| `m_i`, zero-yield, coverage floor | sampling/adaptation controller | deterministic replay | TEST-016 |
| Calendar/crossover solver | scheduler/solver | `v39:calendar:solve` | TEST-017 |
| Anchor probe scoring/censoring/caps | probe controller | `v39:probe:stage1/2` | TEST-018 |
| Credit/accounting/settlement/watchdog | ledger + watchdog | Gate-3/4/status commands | TEST-019–021 |
| Security/retention/expiry | retention/security service | `v39:security:verify` | TEST-023 |
| History/weather as-known joins | feature store + weather service | offline replay | TEST-013/014 |
| Protected split/evaluation freeze | split/manifest service | `v39:freeze` | TEST-025/026 plus TEST-022/023/027 for budget/security/manifest completeness |
| Traceability/registry/dictionary/lineage | audit metadata | preflight checks | TEST-027 |
| Contradiction/regression scanner | scanner module | `v39:scanner` | TEST-027 seeded bad fixtures + TEST-028 full-suite/build/replay evidence |

Scripts are not secondary utilities. Each script must call the same production services used by the real runtime, or explicitly state that it is a read-only verifier. A script that reimplements business rules separately is a P1 divergence risk and cannot close a gate.

### 3.2 Last-known repository code walkthrough — active inspection aid, not current status proof

> **Repository-verification rule:** the concrete paths/function names below came from the previously observed repository and are useful inspection targets. They become current only if §1 intake verifies them on the present commit. Paid CLI examples in this walkthrough are explanatory legacy syntax; use §2 for current authorized commands.
>
> The full plain-English walkthrough of every file is in
> **`AugMDnotes/CODE_WALKTHROUGH.md`** — read that for the complete tour. This
> section summarizes the pieces that matter for what we're doing right now (the
> probe), so you can follow a run.

#### 3.2.1 `scripts/anchor_probe.ts` — the probe runner (`npm run anchor-probe`)

**Historical command plumbing (verification target, not current repository fact).** In the last-known repository snapshot, `package.json` mapped the legacy `anchor-probe` alias to `tsx scripts/anchor_probe.ts`, the legacy `canary` alias to `tsx scripts/credit_canary.ts`, and `build-catalog` to `tsx scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts`. The `--` convention passes later arguments through npm to the underlying script. **Do not assume any of those aliases, files, or mappings still exist.** Current repository intake (§1) must inspect `package.json`/the actual launcher and then map or implement the canonical §2 `v39:`* command surface. Operators use only the verified canonical interfaces after that mapping is evidenced.

**Last-known legacy intent versus current requirement.** This script was built around an older probe design. It is an inspection target, **not** current executable authority. The current binding owner is Plan §§4.5 and 9 plus the pre-probe freeze record: Stage 1 is target-2h and Stage 2 target-4h, capacity is `rows_per_hour≥60`, `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` is cumulative per immutable `probe_budget_day_id`, weights are 0.4/0.2/0.2/0.2, and the shortlist is exactly **12 dual-eligible HUB-tier candidates across regions** including WSSS and OMAA. The Plan does **not** impose a fixed per-region quota on the 12-candidate shortlist. Exogenous components use the frozen §4.5 traffic/geo/carrier formulas and source hashes; probe outcomes supply only the observed-yield 20%. The repository agent must correct/verify the script against those rules before mapping it to `v39:probe:stage1/2`.

Its modes:

- `--stage 1 [--icao X] [--hours N]` — **historical CLI syntax only** for a target-2h cap-censored Stage-1 probe. Current operator interface is `v39:probe:stage1` under an exact authorization; the runner may block for the bounded run, but actual exposure/stop/censoring/settlement must be recorded.
- `--stage 2` — **historical CLI syntax only** for target-4h cap-censored confirmation; current interface is `v39:probe:stage2` and must refuse any airport without a valid completed Stage-1 result
  (**required refusal behavior, not a present-code claim**): the current implementation must make the rl8 out-of-order Stage-2 start impossible, and TEST-015/related probe-state tests must prove that refusal before paid use.
- `--score` — **historical CLI mode only**. A current scorer must implement the frozen Plan §§4.5/9 formulas, the `rows_per_hour≥60` capacity gate, the frozen yield-reference rules, deterministic tie handling, and proposed exactly-five lock only after valid Stage-2 confirmation. The legacy scorer is an inspection target until repository evidence proves equivalence.
- `--status` — list recorded probes from `clean.adb_anchor_probe`.
- `--cleanup [--force]` — **historical CLI description only**. Current cleanup must be scoped to experiment-owned subscriptions and must preserve an audit record. A generic `--force` safety bypass is forbidden. A foreign/untracked active billable subscription is a refusal condition. Binding R1 requires it be disabled/deleted before the experiment can proceed, but that cleanup must be ownership-aware, explicitly authorized/audited, and never implemented as a generic force bypass; `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false` does **not** make broad deletion safe.
- `--check-webhook` — prints the public webhook URL, whether
  `REPLIT_DOMAINS`/`WEBHOOK_BASE_URL` are set, and does a GET reachability probe.

**Current mechanics required before this script may be mapped to production:** resolve and validate the exact AUTH record → reserve against the immutable `probe_budget_day_id` cumulative `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` **and** pre-run/floor ledgers → R1 exclusivity/owned-subscription check → verify the current feed/cost/tier under the frozen provider contract → create only the authorized owned subscription with `maxDeliveryRetries=0` → persist the run/probe ownership record → execute the target window until normal end or cap censoring → delete/inactivate and verify owned cleanup → apply the **shared settlement rule** (frozen initial wait/poll/stability window; ≥3 consecutive equal authoritative balance reads; change resets; timeout blocks) → compute settled external Alert spend from `B_before−B_stable` and reconcile to immutable SEND/item accounting → derive canonical confirmed/ambiguous flight-instance and chain metrics plus complete-bucket stability → write the external report/evidence record and only then mark the probe terminal. Any legacy 1,900-only probe guard, fixed 10-second settle, single post-read, free-feed assumption, or legacy dedup/chain SQL is insufficient and must be replaced or proven equivalent.

#### 3.2.2 `scripts/credit_canary.ts` — Gate 3 (`npm run canary` **(legacy alias; current mapped interface: `v39:gate3:canary`)**)

A **canary** = a tiny controlled live test meant to die loudly before the real work. Its cost is **not guaranteed to be 1 credit**: billing is per flight item per provider SEND. Freeze a very small authorized Alert-credit reservation/ceiling, then measure actual settled spend. The historical rl9 attempt happened to cost 1 credit. The official canary:

1. Reads the live balance (`balance_before`).
2. **Asserts R1 exclusivity** — lists subscriptions, refuses to continue if any
   foreign ACTIVE billable subscription exists (the rl8 orphans would have failed
   this).
3. Creates exactly one **authorized** owned subscription for the resolved Gate-3 airport/filter/window with `maxDeliveryRetries: 0`; no hard-coded KLAX requirement comes from this walkthrough.
4. Runs only for the frozen authorized duration, deletes/inactivates the owned subscription, then applies the shared settlement procedure; fixed 120 s / 5 s waits are historical implementation details, not current settlement proof.
5. Applies the shared settlement rule: after the configured initial wait, poll the authoritative balance until at least **three consecutive equal reads** (or a larger frozen count) span the required stability window; any change resets the count; timeout = `SETTLEMENT_UNRESOLVED`. Then `C_external = balance_before − balance_stable`.
6. Reconciles against the current immutable delivery/item/attempt ledger mapped during repository intake: `C_internal = Σ notification_items` for the owned subscription/SEND scope, plus raw persistence, processing, rows, and failures. Historical `adb_ingest_events` is only a candidate audit source and cannot substitute for the normalized immutable raw/item ownership proof.
7. **Official Gate-3 PASS iff `C_external == C_internal` (`CANARY_TOLERANCE=0`), the shared ≥3-read settlement rule passes, raw-before-2xx evidence passes, `delivery_failures=0`, >0 items arrived, owned cleanup is confirmed, and no foreign billable subscription exists.** Otherwise FAIL/STOP.

This is the command that FAILED on 08-19: `C_external=1`, `C_internal=0`,
`delivery_failures=1` — the webhook was reachable (charged 1 credit) but the
handler threw before storing. A later `is_randomized` code change was reported as the historical bug fix, but that does **not** prove a current canary will pass; current presence/wiring/tests and live reconciliation must be re-established.

#### 3.2.3 `scripts/build_stratified_catalog.ts` — step 11 (`npm run build-catalog`)

Builds the sampling frame from the measured universe (current Plan §4 + §17). The last-known script called provider coverage inputs that were documented as non-paid/free-tier at the time; **current tier/cost/version must be re-verified under Gate 1**. Historically it then
historically classified the 276 curated airports and coerced every other coverage airport into REGIONAL with `traffic_prior=1.0`. **That legacy behavior is noncompliant with binding Plan §4.1**: airports missing the frozen traffic reference must remain `tier='UNCLASSIFIED'`, `tier_verified=false`, `tier_source='missing_reference'` and stay out of primary tier-stratified sampling until deterministically classified. Region assignment must use the frozen country→macro-region mapping plus the explicit country/airport/longitude overrides; an ICAO-prefix fallback is forbidden unless a separately versioned prefix table is explicitly frozen before Stage 1. The current Plan does not silently grant such a fallback. Per-feed eligibility is still computed independently. The historical "267 curated + 4,053 unclassified→REGIONAL" and "18/18 strata" outputs are **provisional evidence only**. Under the current §§16–17 sequence, Gate 1 first remeasures coverage; only then are the traffic/region rules frozen and the final frame rebuilt/hashed before the pre-probe freeze, safety smoke, and Gate 2.

#### 3.2.4 `server/lib/disruption/adbCollectionController_v3.ts` — the brain

This legacy controller is **not yet evidence of binding Phase-6 behavior**. Binding configuration must distinguish the `HARD_CAP=1,900` Alert-credit ceiling per immutable Phase-6 `budget_day_id`, the frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling≤57,900`, the protected Alert floor, and the separate seven-category REST/API-unit ledger; it must not treat a generic 1,900 "budget" or an old fixed retry allowance as interchangeable resources. The tier mix remains `{HUB:1, MID:2, REGIONAL:1}`, with the enabled anchor consuming the HUB slot, and the six UTC slots are `{0,4,8,12,16,20}`. the logical safe state `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT=false/OFF` is mandatory until the authorized Phase-6 start transition; at inspected commit `5bffdaf...`, the literal shell-safe value is `0` until the parser is fixed/tested. HUB/MID selection follows Plan §8.5. REGIONAL selection must be wired to the seeded adaptive rule **within the already selected tier×region cell**: `adaptive_score_i=traffic_prior_i·m_i`, `draw_score_i=adaptive_score_i·coverage_boost_i`, and `p_i=draw_score_i/Σdraw_score` over the current eligible set, with Phase-6 initialization `m_i=1`, EMA=NULL, the frozen recurrence/zero-yield state machine, persistent coverage-floor eligibility, and replayable seed. Any legacy uniform `1/n_eligible` production draw is compliant **only on an initialization step where all eligible scores are equal**, not as the general algorithm. Batch start/stop must use the frozen template plus SEND-aware accounting/reconciliation; current repository wiring remains to be proven by implementation tests.

#### 3.2.5 The webhook path (`routes_v3.ts` + `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts` + `flightDataPrePostStore_v3.ts`)

**The data flow, answered in plain words** (the "what comes from us vs AeroDataBox"
question):

1. AeroDataBox sees a flight change for a subscribed airport and **POSTs a payload**
   to `POST /api/v1/webhooks/aerodatabox[/secret]`. The payload contains the
   `subscription` block, the `balance` block, and a `flights[]` array.
2. Our route performs authentication/minimal envelope validation and looks up sampling metadata by `subscription_id`. It **must not promise to always 2xx**: failure to durably commit the normalized raw envelope/item layer before acknowledgement returns non-2xx so silent loss cannot be disguised as success. After durable raw commit, semantic parse/normalization failures are recorded as processing attempts and may be acknowledged according to the frozen retry policy. `maxDeliveryRetries=0` is the intended subscription setting, but protocol correctness is not weakened merely to avoid a possible paid retry.
3. `flightNotificationExtractor_v3.ts` may flatten provider items into convenience/current-state columns, but the binding implementation must first preserve raw delivery/item identities and all canonical provider/source clocks from Plan §6; derived `data_stage`/sampling metadata are OUR annotations, not provider facts.
4. Any legacy mutable `flight_data_pre_post` upsert by `dedup_key` is a **current-state convenience path only**. It must not replace or deduplicate immutable `raw_delivery`/`raw_delivery_item` or the canonical semantic-observation identity; `(flight, carrier, locReportedUtc)` is invalid for non-location updates and is not a universal event key.
5. Durable raw persistence occurs **before successful 2xx** according to the Plan's receive→commit raw→2xx→semantic-processing contract. `adb_ingest_events`/processing attempts are audit/accounting records; implementation evidence must prove the actual route follows this order.

**The rl9 bug, and the fix (in this same file):** migration 0022 created
`is_randomized` as `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`. But for any delivery with no managed
batch (probe/canary subscription), the extractor set `isRandomized: null` — and
inserting NULL into a NOT NULL column makes Postgres throw, which made the whole
handler throw, store 0 rows, and record `delivery_failure=1` (your canary FAIL). A later code change reportedly defaulted unmanaged rows to `isRandomized: false`, consistent with the NOT NULL constraint for non-randomized unmanaged probe/canary rows. Treat that as **historical fix provenance only**: current code existence, production wiring, raw-before-2xx ordering, TEST-006/007/019, and any new canary/probe behavior remain NOT_VERIFIED.

#### 3.2.6 `aerodataboxLimiter_v3.ts` — the AeroDataBox client

Throttled/rate-limited REST calls: `getBalance`, `refillBalance`,
`createSubscription`/`listSubscriptions`/`deleteSubscription`/`getSubscription`,
`checkAirportFeeds`, `listFeedAirports`, `defaultWebhookUrl` were last-known client methods. The current production client/caller must be verified to create every experiment-owned subscription with `maxDeliveryRetries: 0`; **billing remains one Alert credit per flight item on provider SEND, not one credit per HTTP delivery**. Webhook URL/port requirements must be taken from the pinned provider contract/live Gate-0.5 evidence; a historical `:443` workaround is not treated as universal provider-contract authority.

#### 3.2.7 Historical typecheck baseline — not grandfathered

A historical repository snapshot reported **57 pre-existing type errors** (mostly in `server/routes.ts`). That number is provenance, **not a passing criterion**. Phase-6 technical readiness requires the repository's binding typecheck command to exit 0 (or an explicitly adjudicated toolchain exception recorded as a blocker, not silently grandfathered). The next repository-capable pass must run the command and record its exact exit code/output; "no new errors relative to 57" is insufficient for GO.

#### 3.2.8 Current required formulas versus last-known legacy code

The binding formulas come from the **current Plan**, not from whatever arithmetic happens to exist in a legacy script. Repository intake must map each row below to production code and tests; any different legacy formula is a correction blocker.

| Requirement | Current binding formula/rule | Legacy inspection status |
|---|---|---|
| Unique flights per credit | `uf_per_credit = confirmed_unique_flight_instances / settled_alert_credits`; also report the ambiguity-sensitive upper bound when identity is not fully resolved | Legacy row/dedup counting must be audited against canonical `flight_instance_id` and settled SEND-side credit scope |
| Chain links per credit | confirmed compatible chain links under canonical tail/flight identity divided by settled Alert credits | Legacy `count(*)-1 GROUP BY aircraft_reg` is only a candidate and cannot be accepted until identity/continuity rules are proven |
| Stability | `1/(1+CV)` on **complete observed 15-minute buckets only**, with frozen minimum bucket rule; incomplete/censored buckets do not silently enter | Legacy bucket code must be checked for completeness/censoring handling |
| Component standardization | per component, `clamp(probe/reference,0,1)` against WSSS primary; OMAA only under the pre-frozen component fallback rule; neither valid ⇒ `NOT_SCORABLE` | Legacy blanket fallback logic must be compared to the frozen per-component rule |
| Yield score | arithmetic mean of the three valid standardized yield components required by Plan §9.2 | No imputation from invalid references |
| Capacity gate | `rows_per_hour ≥ 60`, separate feasibility gate and never part of anchor score | Must remain separate from scoring |
| Traffic score | `traffic_score = min(1, traffic_metric_value / hub_cut_metric)` using the frozen §4.1 metric/boundary | Any legacy “flights/year ÷ shortlist maximum” formula is **noncompliant** |
| Geo score | `0.5*degree_norm + 0.3*intl_share + 0.2*region_rarity`, with `region_rarity=1-region_share` and frozen normalization inputs | Legacy monolithic `geo index` must be replaced or proven algebraically identical to the frozen inputs |
| Carrier score | `0.6*effective_carriers_norm + 0.4*intl_share` with frozen normalization inputs | Legacy generic `carrier index` must be replaced or proven identical |
| Anchor score | `0.40*traffic_score + 0.20*geo_score + 0.20*carrier_score + 0.20*yield_score` | Weights are binding; inputs must be the current formulas above |
| External Alert spend | `C_external = B_before - B_stable` **after** the shared stable-read settlement procedure | A single `balance_after` read is insufficient |
| Internal Alert spend | `C_internal = Σ notification_items` over the immutable owned delivery/item SEND scope | Mixed legacy audit tables alone are insufficient without raw/item ownership/provenance |

**Status:** these formulas are document-closed but **repository implementation is NOT_VERIFIED**. Do not describe the legacy `computeScores` implementation as “the frozen code” until repository mapping, TEST-008/018/019, and the pre-probe freeze prove it.

#### 3.2.9 How to read a probe run — required evidence shape versus historical output

A historical Stage-1 runner printed a simple sequence such as balance → subscription → waiting → delete → one post-balance → row/flight/chain metrics. That is useful for understanding the old script, but it is **not sufficient current gate evidence**.

A current mapped `v39:probe:stage1/2` run must additionally emit/reference: exact AUTH ID and resolved candidate/window; `probe_budget_day_id`; before-run reservations against the 500-credit probe cap and pre-run/floor ledgers; provider contract/tier/version; owned subscription identity; start/end/censoring reason; cleanup confirmation; the complete authoritative settlement-read series and `B_stable`; immutable internal SEND/item accounting; `C_external/C_internal` reconciliation; canonical confirmed/ambiguous flight-instance bounds; chain denominator/identity QC; complete-bucket stability and minimum-bucket status; capacity result; source/reference/preprobe hashes; exit status; report/evidence ID and artifact hash. `SKIPPED`, `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`, `MISMATCH`, cleanup failure, unresolved settlement, or foreign subscription all stop advancement according to §1/§6.

Do not “walk away” based only on a historical console line: the runner itself must own timeout/cleanup/settlement/fail-closed behavior and leave a recoverable evidence record even if the shell disconnects.

<a id="sec-4"></a>
## 4. Phase/gate lookup index — **reference only, not a second runbook**

### 4.1 Why §4 exists

This section exists for one reason: **when you see a gate name, it tells you which phase owns it and where to go.**

It is intentionally short.

- To decide **what to implement**, use **§1**.
- To decide **what to type in Replit**, use **§2**.
- To inspect current code owners, use **§3 and §§8–26**.
- To see current PASS/BLOCKED status, use **§6**.
- To know whether to continue/return, use **§7**.
- §4 must never introduce a new implementation instruction, formula, threshold, or command.

### 4.2 Phase → gate → detailed-section lookup

| Phase | What lives inside it | Detailed implementation | Replit commands | Current role |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **0** | code/schema/offline closure; R1–R7; S1–S5; all production builders/guards/tests | §1.5 | §§2.2–2.18 | **CURRENT** |
| **1** | **Gate 0** account/channel/budget identity | §1.6 | §2.19 | blocked until Phase-0 review |
| **2** | **P → Gate 1 → reference/frame/preprobe freeze → safety smoke → Gate 2** | §1.7 | §2.20 | blocked |
| **3** | **Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4** | §1.8 | §2.21 | blocked |
| **4** | **Gate 5** population-observability validation | §1.9 | §2.22 | blocked |
| **5** | readiness, final calendar/split/manifest, **FREEZE** | §1.10 | §2.23 | blocked |
| **6** | separately authorized 31-batch-day collection | §1.11 | §2.24 | NO-GO |
| **7** | protected materialization + Month-1 evaluation | §1.12 | §2.25 | blocked by Phase 6 |

### 4.3 Binding state transition only

```text
Phase 0
  → Phase 1 / Gate 0
  → Phase 2 / P
  → Gate 1
  → reference + final frame + preprobe freeze
  → safety smoke
  → Gate 2
  → Phase 3 / Gate 3
  → Gate 0.5
  → Gate 4
  → Phase 4 / Gate 5
  → Phase 5 / FREEZE
  → separate Phase-6 authorization
  → Phase 6
  → Phase 7
```

A later state cannot PASS before its predecessor.

### 4.4 Universal state labels

Every stage/gate terminates as one of the applicable canonical states:

- `PASS`
- `FAIL`
- `BLOCKED`
- `BLOCKED_NOT_AUTHORIZED`
- `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE` where an adequacy threshold applies
- `MISMATCH` for identity/accounting/manifest disagreement
- `SETTLEMENT_UNRESOLVED` for a paid run that has not reached stable closure

A command exiting 0 does not automatically mean the scientific/operational stage is PASS.

### 4.5 Authorization/evidence rule

Before any external action that can mutate state or spend units/credits, the resolved run must bind:

- current phase/gate;
- exact current commit/config/schema/reference hashes;
- predecessor evidence;
- exact AUTH ID + expiry;
- airport/query/filter/window;
- Alert ceiling;
- REST category ceilings;
- owned subscription/run identity;
- cleanup/stop owner;
- manifest/preprobe hash where applicable.

Mismatch → refuse before SEND/request.

**Current project pointer:** go to **§1.5 Phase 0** and **§2.2**. Do not use §4 as a checklist.

```text

CURRENT_COMMAND_ZONE = LOG_§§2.2–2.18
MANDATORY_RETURN = AFTER_REAL_AGGREGATE_PREFLIGHT_PASS_BEFORE_GATE0
```

### 7.1 What you should do next

The next coding session does **not** begin with Gate 0. It begins with the two highest-risk Phase-0 work packages:

1. **§1.5.1 runtime fail-closed safety** — fix `ADB_AUTO_COLLECT` default/parser, startup/watchdog authorization, and experimental retry defaults.
2. **§1.5.2 raw-before-2xx production wiring** — make the real webhook route durably persist S2 raw envelope/items before successful acknowledgment.

Then continue §1.5.3 through §1.5.17 in order.

### 7.2 First mandatory return point

Return with a new Run Report only when one current Replit evidence scope proves:

- current branch/HEAD/worktree;
- safe auto-collection default/refusal;
- S1–S5 production wiring;
- PRE/AIRBORNE/outcome/history/weather builders;
- R1–R7 behavior;
- corrected identity-v2 tests;
- `tests/fidsTimezone.test.ts`;
- migrations fresh/upgrade/repeat/constraints/recovery;
- TEST-001–028 required offline portions PASS;
- full test suite PASS;
- full-repo typecheck exit 0;
- lint PASS;
- production build + production-start/deployment-entrypoint consistency PASS;
- registry/traceability/dictionary/lineage PASS;
- scanner PASS;
- aggregate `v39:preflight` PASS;
- no required `v39:*` command remains an echo stub;
- exact artifact hashes and unresolved warning list.

Then **STOP before another Gate-0/provider/account action**.

### 7.3 Later mandatory return points

Use §1.13 as the definitive schedule: after safety smoke; after material Stage-1 ranking decisions; after Gate 2; after each Gate 3/0.5/4/5; after FREEZE; and immediately on any Phase-6 pause/failure/mismatch.

### 7.4 Run Reports remain evidence, not the implementation manual

It is okay for the coding AI to end a Run Report with a proposed next step, but the report must point to §1 and §2. If the report says “next = Gate 0” while §6 still says Phase 0 is blocked, the report is preserved as historical evidence and **the Log controls the next action**.

## 8. Decision record and implementation priority queue

**DEC-001 — No V3.10:** selected patch f.7+f.8; rejected V3.10 (violates absolute versioning).

### 8.1 Dependency-aware implementation priority queue (derived from binding Plan; not proof of code status)

Severity and dependency are both considered. A lower-numbered item may block many later items even when another item is equally P0.

**P0 — resolve/code/test before any paid collection:**
1. retention-rights gate + configurable compliant raw lifecycle; normalized raw ingress before 2xx;
2. FIDS population query roles/timezone/range/scope/codeshare provenance, **record-class timestamp applicability, internal half-open boundary filtering, and frozen `population_acquisition_guard_minutes` + `population_refresh_cadence_minutes` + `population_max_staleness_minutes`**;
3. stable flight-instance/retime/codeshare identities;
4. canonical clocks + `available_at`/`materialized_at` semantics;
5. provider milestone/T/actual-vs-estimated constructibility guards;
6. historical/weather as-known-at-cutoff stores and leakage tests;
7. PRE and AIRBORNE snapshot builders with population-defined row existence;
8. target-specific terminalizer/outcome-recovery state machine with generated/coalesced `OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET`;
9. SEND-aware Alert accounting, exact canary, hard-cap/soft-stop safety;
10. 31-day SAT calendar with exact five crossover pairs/split containment + separate Alert-credit/REST-unit budget proof.

**P1 — resolve before final frame/FREEZE/Phase 6:** traffic/region reference classification and final frame; anchor probe/reference exactness; production `m_i`/zero-yield integration; split/test protection and Engine-E deferral; complete registry/requirement matrix/schema dictionary/lineage/contradiction scanner; migration/typecheck/integration closure.

**P2 — complete before publication/strong inference:** diagnostic/report reproducibility, staleness/ablation/MV presentation, analysis metadata completeness, archive cleanup where it cannot affect executable authority.

**P3 — cosmetic/history:** wording or organization that cannot change denominator, billing, leakage, sampling, labels, identity, or executable code behavior.

A live/provider/human blocker is tracked separately from priority; it must not be relabeled as an offline implementation PASS.

**Registry completeness status:** the 22-row table above is transitional and incomplete. Before FREEZE it must also represent account plan and billing dates; monthly entitlement/opening balance/pre-run and Phase-6 ceilings; every FIDS/validation/outcome/history/diagnostic budget; provider contract URL/version/hash/rate/range; `query_direction`/`recovery_direction` and FIDS `population_role`; selected T and primary target plus milestone actuality-verification rule/version; traffic/tier/region references and hashes; scope/codeshare ambiguity policy; anchor yield-reference/probe cumulative cap-censoring protocol; `m_i` function/initial state and coverage floor; calendar hash/randomization unit/washout; canary vs production tolerances, balance-poll cadence, clock-skew tolerance and unsettled-burst margin; Gate-0.5 minimum evidence; raw-retention period/legal basis/compliance-deletion behavior; weather/history readiness; and split hash. Each needs type/default/safe-default/required/secret/producer/consumer/phase/gate/failure behavior. `CONFIG_REGISTRY_COMPLETE=BLOCKED` until no required setting is unmapped.


### 9.1 Canonical rule/config registry requirements

For each constant/rule record:

```text
registry_id | name | value/state | units | Plan owner | code owner
source (project/provider/measured) | freeze stage | mutation rule
validation | tests | manifest field | evidence hash | status
```

At minimum include both budgets; 60,000 (only when confirmed for the actual
channel); 57,900; 1,000; 1,900; soft margin; `ending_alert_margin`;
`protected_api_floor_units`; `ending_api_margin_units`; `protected_rest_budget`;
`pre_smoke_unsettled_burst_margin_credits`; `unsettled_burst_margin_credits`;
all per-run ceilings/reservations; settlement settings; FIDS contract/attempt
policy; T/target; timestamps; identity; frame/regions; `m_i`; probe protocol;
calendar; weather/history; outcome recovery; evaluation/split; retention;
security; and auto-collect. Offline closure requires zero unmapped binding
rules.

## 10. Database data dictionary — required current contract

**Every table:** `table name / purpose / producer / consumer / PK / unique keys / FKs / indexes / row unit / cardinality / append-only vs mutable / source of truth / retention / provenance`

**Every column:** `name / SQL type / nullable / unit / semantics / source: provider/derived/our server/reference / timestamp type / availability rule / producer / consumers / missing-state meaning / QC`

The table below mixes existing legacy objects, migration-created-but-unapplied objects, and intended objects. It is not proof that every row/column exists or is production-wired. A complete schema-introspection-backed column dictionary is `NOT_VERIFIED` in this document pass and remains mandatory repository work; any intended object is DOCUMENTED unless separately evidenced as migration-created, tested, applied, and wired. **Retention is a data-class decision, not a table-name shortcut. Classify every important table/column as `raw_api_content`, `derived_work_candidate/derived_work`, `non_aerodatabox_project_metadata`, or another sourced class with legal basis. AeroDataBox Article 5.5 governs retained raw API Contents unless Plan Terms grant another period; Article 5.6 states that §5.5 does not apply to Derived Works, but other Terms restrictions still do. Therefore no table below receives an unconditional “permanent” claim merely because it is normalized or useful.**

**Key column examples (every important column `name / type / nullable / unit / semantics / source / timestamp type / availability / producer / consumers / missing / QC`):**

- `flight_events.event_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`: optional semantic event time; location events may source `location_reported_utc`, but non-location updates must not fabricate it. Snapshot eligibility is controlled by `available_at`, not this nullable field alone.
- `flight_events.provider_state_updated_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`: provider-native state-update clock sourced from `lastUpdatedUtc`; it is NOT notification generation/publication time.
- `provider_notification_generated_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`: notification-envelope generation clock when present; distinct from state update.
- `delivery_attempt_seq_no INTEGER`, `delivery_attempt_utc TIMESTAMPTZ`, and `delivery_attempt_cost_credits NUMERIC`: preserve provider attempt sequence, attempt time, and cost. Received-attempt cost is diagnostic; settled balance GET remains authoritative.
- `location_reported_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`: live-location observation clock; nullable for non-location updates.
- `http_received_at_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL` and `raw_persisted_at_utc TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`: server receipt and durable persistence clocks. `available_at` cannot precede durable persistence.
- `flight_events.available_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`: earliest UTC instant the normalized fact was durably usable by the feature-building system. It must satisfy `http_received_at_utc ≤ raw_persisted_at_utc ≤ available_at`; for deterministic derived features use `max(required_input.available_at, transform-completion availability)` and store later offline `materialized_at` separately. Snapshot use requires `available_at≤prediction_cutoff`; no backfilling from final-state timestamps.
- Legacy `received_timestamp_utc` is superseded by canonical `http_received_at_utc`; do not maintain two receipt clocks with identical semantics.
- Preserve provider-native `departure.scheduledTime` without renaming it to `scheduled_gate_out` or `scheduled_wheels_off`. FAA aliases are nullable and require Gate-0.5 semantic proof; otherwise `milestone_unverified=true`.

**Identity separation:** `raw_delivery.delivery_id`, `(delivery_id,item_index,raw_item_sha256)`, versioned semantic observation identity, and identity-v2 `flight_instance_id` are four different keys. The current migration/helper shapes do not fully prove this contract; production correction and collision/retry/non-location tests remain CURRENT STATUS NOT_VERIFIED.
- `adb_collection_subs.airport_layer_design_probability DOUBLE NULL`: conditional REGIONAL airport-layer design probability **within the selected tier×region cell**, `p_i=draw_score_i/Σdraw_score`, where `draw_score_i=traffic_prior_i·m_i·coverage_boost_i`; source is the seeded controller draw. For deterministic HUB/MID paths this probability is NULL and planned allocation metadata is separate. Exact schema/check/producer ownership must be reverified in the current repository.
- `adb_sampling_frame.m_i DOUBLE`: Phase-6 adaptive multiplier in `[0.25,1.5]`, initialized deterministically to **1.0 for every eligible REGIONAL airport at Phase-6 start**; probe data must not seed it. After each valid Phase-6 observation, update only via the frozen recurrence/state machine. A missing/NULL `m_i` in an eligible production draw is a **REFUSE/BLOCK** condition, not an implicit uniform fallback.

*Note retained:* `clean.flight_data_pre_post` kept for compat but **not** only true table — S-layers above first-class per Plan §2/§6, A30 item 72 / former Log §6 wording fixed (current table/schema teaching is §28).


### 10.1 Required schema-dictionary and lineage completeness rules

The dictionary records table/column/type/nullability/key/index/constraint,
source class, semantic/effective/available/materialized clocks, content
classification, retention/expiry, producer, consumer, and tests.

Required lineage:

```text
FIDS request/query provenance → raw response (lawful retention)
  → append-only population observation → horizon membership → PRE snapshot

webhook HTTP/auth → raw delivery → raw item → processing attempt
  → semantic event/current state → airborne point/trajectory → AIRBORNE snapshot

source facts → bitemporal history/weather join → cutoff-safe feature
  → target-specific outcome → protected evaluation split

account/balance/API-unit evidence → reservation/attempt/settlement ledgers
  → gate artifact → manifest/FREEZE
```

No arrow may skip raw-before-2xx, availability, identity, role, or retention
controls.

### 11.1 Current lineage completeness rule

Every Phase-6-critical arrow must record source table/system, destination table, exact production function/job, filters, dedup/identity rule, timestamp/availability rule, version/hash, failure behavior, and at least one test/evidence ID. Missing current-repository proof keeps `LINEAGE_COMPLETE=BLOCKED`.

## 12. Requirement → Plan → code/test/evidence traceability (104 requirements)

**Audit-count clarification.** The preserved older material explicitly contains the A30 77-item adjudication and an 88-component walkthrough, while this final ledger carries 104 normalized implementation requirements plus TEST-001–028. The supplied files do not contain an authoritative literal 106-item master checklist, so this Log does not pretend that a separate “106” list was independently passed. Instead, closure is by requirement identity and rule coverage: every current binding Plan rule must map into the 104-row matrix, reverse map, tests, registry/schema/lineage, and scanner with zero unmapped items. Old row numbers such as 56 or 84 are provenance labels, not extra executable requirements.


This table intentionally preserves all 104 requirement IDs. `DOC_ALIGNED`

`REPO_REQUIRED` means no current repository evidence was supplied.

### 12.1 Required repository traceability-row schema

Required columns:

```text
requirement_id | Plan section | exact rule | priority | owner module/function
production caller(s) | schema table/column/constraint | migration
config key/default/freeze stage | unit test | integration test | failure test
artifact/evidence ID | commit | status | live dependency | unresolved issue
```

Offline closure requires `REQUIREMENT_MATRIX_UNMAPPED=0`.

## 13. Code → Requirement reverse map

Every production route/controller/scheduler/worker/provider client/DB writer
must map back to one or more Plan requirements and tests. Orphan critical code
is investigated; stale production behavior is removed or made unreachable.
Offline closure requires `PRODUCTION_PATH_MODULES_NOT_WIRED=0` and no
unexplained critical path.

The requirement IDs below are current §19 IDs, but the named symbols are last-known repository examples only; repository intake must confirm actual owners/callers before any row is upgraded from `REPO_REQUIRED`.

## 16. 88-component implementation walkthrough / verification map

> **Current interpretation:** this is an active **verification map**, not evidence that the listed paths still exist or are production-wired. §1 repository intake must confirm/update every path and §19/§20 must map it to current requirements/tests/evidence.
>
> **Structural gap (F5):** This table is a location map (file:function:status).
> A30 §18 requested 20-field per-function detail (inputs, outputs, error handling,
> dependencies, etc.). For every Phase-6-critical owner, repository closure must verify the location map and populate the required 20-field function evidence before technical readiness. Noncritical explanatory rows may be completed later, but no owner needed by a paid gate may wait until after that gate.
> The `CODE_WALKTHROUGH.md` file provides plain-English prose for the full tour.
