<a id="sec-17"></a>
## 17. Migration policy — fresh/upgrade/repeat/constraint verification

The **last-documented Sep-1 snapshot** reported migration filenames 0017–0025 and last live application evidence through 0023; the current repository has not been inspected here. After §1 intake, distinguish for every required current migration (historically 0024/0025 or mapped successors): `MIGRATION_FILE_CREATED` = actual observed YES/NO; `MIGRATION_TESTED_OFFLINE` = evidence-backed status; `MIGRATION_APPLIED_LIVE` = evidence-backed status. This document does **not** pre-fill `MIGRATION_FILE_CREATED=YES`. Required fresh-DB, upgrade-from-0023, repeat-boot/idempotency, indexes/constraints, append-only behavior, and rollback/recovery tests remain **NOT_VERIFIED**. If a required test truly needs a database environment that is unavailable, record `BLOCKED_ENV` with the exact missing dependency; otherwise it is mandatory offline work.


**Current minimum migration proof:** fresh install, upgrade from the last evidenced prior level, repeat/idempotent boot, required indexes/constraints, append-only/raw-mutation refusal, and actual target-environment application evidence where live application is required. File existence alone is not `MIGRATION_APPLIED`.

---
<a id="sec-18"></a>
## 18. Typecheck / lint / build policy — zero-error readiness

Historical baseline reported 57 type errors; that is not a pass. The Sep1 correction requires actual typecheck, lint, build, unit, and integration commands with exit codes and failure attribution. This document-edit pass does not provide repository execution evidence. Those commands/fixes remain **NOT_VERIFIED / REQUIRED** and must be run by a repository-capable implementation pass; a legacy docs-only scope label is not a current exemption. Do not claim CODE GO.


**Binding policy:** readiness requires the current project typecheck/lint/build commands to succeed under the recorded toolchain, or an explicit P0/P1 blocker remains. A historical baseline error count is never grandfathered into readiness.

---
<a id="sec-19"></a>
## 19. Runtime / dependency reproducibility

**Historical last-observed repository snapshot only:** a prior Sep-1 audit recorded Darwin, branch `main`, HEAD `9fa04fea6c1b1de0a3182fa3b0ee439f72a0224a`, and migration filenames through 0025. **None of those values is current repository evidence in this document pass.** §1 `v39:repo-intake` must record the actual branch/HEAD/worktree/migration inventory/toolchain/lock hash before any repository status is claimed.


Current repository intake (§1) must additionally record exact toolchain/runtime versions, lockfile/dependency hash, build target, database engine/version, migration runner, scheduler/worker topology, deployment environment, and provider-client versions before technical readiness can be claimed.

---
<a id="sec-20"></a>
## 20. Run and gate evidence policy — reports stay external

New run/gate outputs live in `V3.9_RUN_REPORTS_AND_EVIDENCE.md`; this Log contains only durable rules/status/evidence pointers.

### 20.1 Gate evidence record

Every `GATE-<id>-YYYYMMDD-###` contains:

| Field | Required value |
|---|---|
| Identity | gate/run ID, Plan version/hash, Log version, commit, schema/config/provider pins |
| Authorization | approver, exact action, account/channel, ceiling(s), start/expiry, stop owner |
| Preconditions | evidence IDs for every prior gate/offline/security/retention item |
| Inputs | frozen airport/window/query/filter/duration/sample minima/settlement settings |
| Budget | Alert reservation/ceiling, REST category/units, pre-run/day/run remainder |
| Subscriptions | before/during/after inventory; owned versus foreign; cleanup evidence |
| Execution | actual start/end, commands/interface, exit codes, provider request IDs |
| Data | notifications/items/raw persistence/unique bounds/population/snapshots/failures |
| Settlement | timestamped balance reads, stable-read reset history, external/internal reconciliation |
| Result | exactly `PASS`, `FAIL`, `INSUFFICIENT_SAMPLE`, or `BLOCKED` |
| Artifacts | immutable locations and SHA-256 hashes |
| Consequence | next permitted action or mandatory pause/recovery |

### 20.2 Minimum run-report fields

Every `RUN-YYYYMMDD-###` records the actual mapped command/interface, sanitized args, authorization ID, commit/schema/config/manifest hashes, UTC start/end, balances/API units before and after, subscriptions before/during/after, request/item/raw-row counts, settlement-read series, failures, stop reason, exit code, result, artifact paths/hashes, and the exact next permitted action. Never paste secrets or unrestricted raw provider payloads into this Log.

---
<a id="sec-21"></a>
## 21. Phase-6 daily evidence policy

The detailed daily record belongs in `V3.9_RUN_REPORTS_AND_EVIDENCE.md`, not here. For each frozen Phase-6 `budget_day_id`, record at minimum: manifest/calendar hash, run-day index, UTC window(s), airports/tiers/regions and crossover identifiers, authorization ID, provider/account state, Alert reservations/settled spend, REST units by category, subscriptions lifecycle, raw deliveries/items/failures, population/snapshot/outcome counts, watchdog/pause events, settlement proof, stop reason, status, and artifact hashes.

A day may not be marked complete until its subscriptions are closed/inactive, the shared settlement rule is satisfied, external/internal accounting reconciles, and the evidence record is hash-addressed. A later day cannot begin while the prior `budget_day_id` is unsettled.

---
<a id="sec-22"></a>
## 22. Money, dates, credits, and accounting ledger

### 22.1 The budget numbers (from the plan §3)

| Number | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| **60,000** | **USER-CONFIRMED monthly plan entitlement** (`MONTHLY_PLAN_ENTITLEMENT_UNITS`); Gate 0 verifies the same plan is still active plus the current billing-cycle dates, remaining units, and already-consumed units |
| **57,900** | `MAX_DESIGN_CEILING` for Phase-6 Alert credits, **not** an automatically available/spendable balance |
| **`phase6_alert_spend_ceiling`** | exact frozen Alert-credit ceiling `≤57,900`, computed from opening Alert balance + new refill − pre-run ceiling − protected floor − ending margin |
| **1,900 per Phase-6 `budget_day_id`** | project Alert-credit hard ceiling for one immutable parent batch-day; not a UTC/provider-calendar reset, not `60,000/31` scientific arithmetic, and not necessarily fully spent |
| **1,000 floor** | project-chosen protected Alert-balance minimum (`protected_alert_floor`); Gate 0 must prove feasibility; may increase before FREEZE but not drop below 1,000 without design amendment + complete re-FREEZE |
| **REST category caps** | FIDS/splits/retries/validation/outcome/history/diagnostic API-unit budgets generated separately from the calendar; no fixed `1,000` is accepted as proof until the exact total fits |

There is **no valid arithmetic that simply adds Alert credits and REST API units as if they were the same post-allocation resource**. Gate 0 balances the Alert and API trees separately.

### 22.2 Historical balance observations (dated provenance; never current Gate-0 truth)

| Date (UTC) | Event | Balance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-08-16 | Historical balance read before the refill experiment | 862 |
| 2026-08-16 | 1-credit refill — **proved 1 unit = 1 credit** | 863 |
| 2026-08-16 | full refill | **2,901** |
| 2026-08-18 | **first probe attempt (rl8)** — first moment spending *could* have begun | 2,901 (still — 0 delivered, 0 spent) |
| 2026-08-19 | **canary FAIL** 1 delivery charged (SEND→throw) `C_external=1` | **2,900** (`2901−1`) |
| 2026-08-19 last evidenced | real probe spend had NOT begun; failed canary accounted for the 1 evidenced credit | **2,900 at that historical read** |

**Historical evidenced Alert spend through rl9: 1 credit** (the failed 2026-08-19 canary delivery). Current account balance/spend must be reread before any new live action. `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` is cumulative across all probe attempts charged to one immutable `probe_budget_day_id`, not 500 per candidate and not a UTC-calendar reset. Settle one probe budget day before starting another; default-refuse a probe that would cross UTC midnight unless the authorization/ledger explicitly split it into two budget IDs.

### 22.3 Reserve and invariant rules

- Gate 0 must print the **current exact Alert balance tree** and separate REST/API-unit category tree. A legacy `spend ≤57,900` check is insufficient; the controller must enforce the frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling≤57,900` plus protected floor/pre-run/ending allocations.
- `HARD_CAP=1,900` is the project budget-day ceiling. `SOFT_STOP=1,900−ADB_DAILY_SOFT_STOP_MARGIN`; 50/1,850 is the pre-measure/prod-arithmetic fixture and must not be treated as an automatically frozen runtime margin. The current smoke/canary evidence freezes or conservatively increases the applicable unsettled-burst/margin controls. Any settled overshoot is `MISMATCH` and blocks further starts.

<a id="sec-23"></a>
## 23. Implementation-log entry specification (59 fields)

> This section expands the current 59-field record schema. Any old counts, SHAs, test totals, provider values, or example statuses appearing in historical/example text are **not current evidence** unless a new `LOG-YYYYMMDD-###` record in §11 re-establishes them.


Every task/change after `2026-08-30` **must** use `LOG-YYYYMMDD-###` (e.g. `LOG-20260831-001`) and record all 59 fields below. Do not reduce to vague prose. This is the `Definition of Done` per `A30 §8`.

| # | Field | What to write | Example (LOG-20260831-001) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Entry ID | `LOG-YYYYMMDD-###` sequential | `LOG-20260831-001` |
| 2 | UTC date/time | `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM UTC` | `2026-08-31 06:00 UTC` |
| 3 | Local date/time | if useful `America/Los_Angeles` etc. | `2026-08-30 23:00 PDT` |
| 4 | Git SHA before | `git rev-parse HEAD` before change | `<before_sha>` |
| 5 | Git SHA after | after commit (or `uncommitted`) | `<after_sha_or_uncommitted>` |
| 6 | Real V3.9 phase | Phase 0-7 per `Plan §17` | `<phase>` |
| 7 | Gate | Gate 0/1/2/3/0.5/4/5/FREEZE | `<gate_or_none>` |
| 8 | Workstream | A-I historical workstream label if useful | `<workstream_or_na>` |
| 9 | Historical audit item(s) | optional 1–77 provenance from `A30_77_ADJUDICATION.md` | `<historical_item_or_na>` |
| 10 | Plan section | current binding owner in Plan §§0–21 | `§4.1` |
| 11 | Requirement ID | `REQ-###` per current §19 | `<REQ-###>` |
| 12 | Human-readable title | one line | `Fix traffic-tier candidate wording` |
| 13 | Problem | what was wrong | `4053 REGIONAL blanket not stratification` |
| 14 | Why scientifically | impact on validity | `tier×region not real stratification` |
| 15 | Why operationally | impact on collection | `frame not final, Gate5 blocked` |
| 16 | Why reproducibility | impact on replay | `tier hash would change` |
| 17 | Previous documented behavior | only if reconstructing a dated change; otherwise `N/A` | `<historical_rule_or_na>` |
| 18 | Previous actual code behavior | evidence-backed before behavior only | `<before_behavior_or_unknown>` |
| 19 | Intended final behavior | binding Plan behavior | `frozen country→region mapping + explicit overrides; unresolved→UNMAPPED` |
| 20 | Files inspected | exact paths/commit/line ranges inspected | `<paths_and_ranges>` |
| 21 | Files modified | exact changed paths | `<paths_or_none>` |
| 22 | Functions/classes/modules modified | exact symbols | `<symbols_or_none>` |
| 23 | Tables/columns/indexes/constraints modified | exact DB objects | `<db_objects_or_none>` |
| 24 | Migration IDs | exact migration IDs, discovered from current repo | `<migration_ids_or_none>` |
| 25 | Config/env variables involved | exact registry keys/env vars | `<config_keys>` |
| 26 | External APIs involved | exact provider/source and contract version | `<provider_or_none>` |
| 27 | Implementation approach | how requirement was implemented without inventing policy | `<approach>` |
| 28 | Step-by-step code logic | pseudocode matching current Plan | `country→frozen region map; apply explicit overrides/longitude rule; unresolved→UNMAPPED; no unstated ICAO fallback` |
| 29 | Representative before-code excerpt | short evidence excerpt if needed | `<before_excerpt_or_na>` |
| 30 | Representative after-code excerpt | short evidence excerpt if needed | `<after_excerpt_or_na>` |
| 31 | Inputs | exact runtime inputs/artifact IDs | `<inputs>` |
| 32 | Outputs | exact outputs/artifact IDs | `<outputs>` |
| 33 | Side effects | DB/files/provider/account effects | `<side_effects>` |
| 34 | Data flow | producer→transform→consumer | `<data_flow>` |
| 35 | Timestamp semantics | event/provider/available/received | `available_at≤cutoff` |
| 36 | Units | Alert credits vs REST/API units; never conflate | `<units>` |
| 37 | Provenance implications | raw hash etc. | `tier_hash` |
| 38 | Sampling implications | eligibility/strata/probability effects | `<sampling_effects>` |
| 39 | Population/denominator implications | FIDS population vs webhook capture | `<denominator_effects>` |
| 40 | Label implications | target-specific | `label_observed` |
| 41 | Leakage implications | `available_at` | `available_at` |
| 42 | Evaluation implications | Engine A etc. | `Engine A` |
| 43 | Credit/API-unit implications | identify Alert-credit vs REST/API-unit effects without conflation | `0 spend` |
| 44 | Failure modes considered | exact requirement-specific refusal/failure states | `<failure_cases>` |
| 45 | Recovery behavior | fail-closed recovery/retry/rollback behavior | `<recovery_behavior>` |
| 46 | Tests added/changed | exact discovered/created test path(s) | `<test_paths_and_TEST_ids>` |
| 47 | Commands executed | exact verified canonical command(s) | `<verified_command>` |
| 48 | Exit codes where available | `0` `1` | `0` |
| 49 | Expected result | requirement-specific expected result | `<expected_result>` |
| 50 | Observed result | evidence-backed observed result | `<observed_result>` |
| 51 | Raw artifact location | external report/artifact path + evidence ID | `<artifact_path>` |
| 52 | Artifact hash | SHA-256 of exact artifact | `<sha256>` |
| 53 | Seed/version/hash affected | `tier_version` | `tier_version` |
| 54 | A/B/C/D classification | historical audit classification if applicable; otherwise `N/A` | `<classification_or_na>` |
| 55 | Reversibility | can you revert? | yes, re-freeze |
| 56 | Manifest fields affected | `adb_collection_meta` keys | `tier_version` |
| 57 | Unresolved issues | exact remaining issue IDs or `none` | `<ISS-ids-or-none>` |
| 58 | Next required action | exact dependency-aware next action; never skip the §1 order | `<next_action>` |
| 59 | Final status | canonical §0 status vocabulary with evidence basis | `<status>` |

Example: `LOG-20260831-001 V3.9-f.8 10 fixes` covers A30_3 #1-10 with 59 fields above, see `§34.8 Implementation/change history — dated provenance`.

## 26. Document + repository regression scanner

The document scanner must parse **all active §§0–35 of this unified Log** plus **Plan §§0–21**. It must exclude **only §36 Archive** and the Plan preserved-reference appendix from current-rule contradiction counts. Active teaching/reference sections may mention stale phrases only in explicit negation, historical comparison, or scanner fixtures; they may not state them as current executable rules. It must fail on:

- unclosed fences, malformed tables, duplicate anchors, or invalid section
  references;
- **FORBIDDEN/SCANNER-FIXTURE rules (never executable):** `tol=3`, `one row = one credit`, fixed spend 57,900, automatic `1/p`,
  UNCLASSIFIED→REGIONAL, UNMAPPED→a region, capture-gated PRE rows, forced T
  fallback, estimate→actual aliases, `lastUpdatedUtc` dominating Location,
  ERA5 operational fallback, generic five-state outcomes, Month-1 deployment
  claims, Engine-E activation, two-read settlement, “3 retries” ambiguity,
  ≥2h retime→new physical flight, POST-only core-slot substitution, or
  auto-collect enabled before authorization;
- Plan/Log disagreement in constants, gate order, status, or filenames.

The repository scanner must additionally compare registry constants, schema,
production call graph, commands, tests, and manifest fields. Seed at least one
known-bad fixture per forbidden rule and prove the scanner fails. A scanner
that only searches old phrases without parsing current contexts is diagnostic,
not closure evidence. The document scanner must exempt this section's quoted
forbidden-rule lexicon and explicit negations/prohibitions; it fails only when
a stale rule appears as an executable/current rule, not when the guard itself
mentions the stale wording.

Current scanner evidence is now two-layered:

```text
DOCUMENT_PLAN_LOG_SCANNER = PASS_FROM_DOCUMENT_REAUDIT
SUPPLIED_V39_PREFLIGHT_CONSISTENCY_SCRIPT = PASS_REPORTED_CURRENT_CONTRADICTIONS_0
SEEDED_REPOSITORY_FAILURE_FIXTURES = NOT_CLOSED
AGGREGATE_PHASE0_PREFLIGHT = BLOCKED
```

The supplied Run Report says `scripts/v39_preflight_consistency.ts` was repaired (current SEPmd paths + regex correction) and reported `CURRENT_CONTRADICTIONS=0`. Preserve that as real scanner evidence. It still does **not** make aggregate Phase-0 preflight PASS: the scanner must be rerun on the final Phase-0 commit, seeded known-bad fixtures must prove fail-closed behavior, and §5/§18 migration/test/typecheck/lint/build/registry/traceability requirements must close independently. A scanner PASS never substitutes for those other prerequisites.

<a id="sec-27"></a>
## 27. Binding Plan §§0–21 explanatory mirror (reference; not daily runbook)

> The uploaded Plan `V3.9_DataCollectPlan_f.8(4).md` current core §§0–21 is the only binding executable spec for this audit; its preserved historical appendix/adjudication material is non-executable and cannot override §§0–21.
> This section explains what each part says and why it exists — in order. We do not
> edit the plan; we execute it.

### 27.1 Plan §0 — Document authority, risk priority, and closure rule

Plan §0 establishes the interpretation hierarchy before any implementation detail: Plan §§0–21 are normative; this Log cannot override them; historical documents do not create current PASS states; `DOC_STATUS`, `CODE_STATUS`, and `LIVE_STATUS` are independent; P0/P1 defects must be resolved at their required stages; and the pre-probe reference freeze is distinct from the later final manifest. A Plan↔Log disagreement creates `DOC_CONFLICT` and stops the affected path.

### 27.2 Plan §1 — How to read the plan

`R#` = code delta, `S#` = schema/pipeline delta, `G#` = GO gate. All must finish
before Phase 6 starts. The current Plan core is the V3.9-f.8 end-state; every other
`AugMDnotes` file is history. Times are UTC.

### 27.3 Plan §2 — Locked architecture (end-to-end)

The pipeline:

```text
AeroDataBox webhook ─► raw_delivery/raw_item (immutable while lawfully retained) ─► semantic flight_events ─► current flight_state

AeroDataBox FIDS REST ─► raw FIDS response/provenance ─► provider-observable flight_population

flight_population + as-known history/weather + eligible webhook/event facts
    ├─► PRE snapshots at T-24/T-6/T-90
    └─► AIRBORNE snapshots at deployable decision-time `prediction_cutoff_utc`
          (physical `state_observation_time_utc` retained separately)
         ─► target-specific outcomes ─► ML datasets/evaluation
```

Webhook capture never creates the FIDS denominator. Two **prediction states** are never merged: PRE and AIRBORNE/POST. Three promises: (1) no destructive overwrite while data are lawfully retained, with compliance expiry/deletion governed by the verified retention policy; (2) no future information in features/snapshots (`available_at ≤ prediction_cutoff_utc`); (3) no foreign/non-experimental billable subscription may contaminate the authorized experimental set (R1).

### 27.4 Plan §3 — Budget and accounting (two budgets — Gate 0)

AeroDataBox has two resource ledgers: **REST/API units** and **Flight-Alert credits**. Alert credits are funded by API-unit refill but are not interchangeable with REST calls after allocation, so the accounting tree is explicit rather than a fixed `57,900 + 1,000 + ...` assumption.

```text
opening_nonexpiring_alert_balance + authorized_alert_refill_credits
  = pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling + phase6_alert_spend_ceiling
  + protected_alert_floor + ending_alert_margin + unallocated_alert_credits

cycle_entitlement_units (=60,000 while the USER-CONFIRMED plan remains active)
  = api_units_consumed_before_freeze + authorized_alert_refill_units
  + FIDS_BASE_UNITS + FIDS_SPLIT_UNITS + FIDS_RETRY_UNIT_BUDGET
  + VALIDATION_UNIT_BUDGET + OUTCOME_REST_UNIT_BUDGET
  + HISTORY_BOOTSTRAP_UNIT_BUDGET + DIAGNOSTIC_UNIT_BUDGET
  + protected_api_floor_units + ending_api_margin_units
  + unallocated_api_units
```

`MAX_DESIGN_CEILING=57,900 Alert credits`, but the **actual** Phase-6 ceiling is computed only after Gate 0 verifies the account/billing-cycle values: `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling = min(57,900, opening_nonexpiring_alert_balance + authorized_alert_refill_credits - pre_run_alert_spend_ceiling - protected_alert_floor - ending_alert_margin)`. Inputs must be nonnegative and the full identity must reconcile; leftover balance is `unallocated_alert_credits`, not automatic Phase-6 authorization. It is a ceiling, never a spending target. REST categories (FIDS base/splits/retries, validation, outcomes, history, diagnostics) are API units and never silently added to Alert credits.

Three Alert-cap concepts remain separate: (1) reservation before a batch against settled run/day spend and the frozen unsettled-burst margin; (2) SEND-aware live safety (`SOFT_STOP`, `HARD_CAP`, balance/attempt-cost checks); (3) settled post-batch reconciliation. The isolated Gate-3 canary requires exact `C_external == C_internal` after balance stability; a later production tolerance is separate and only exists if measured/frozen. R1 means the active billable set contains only authorized experiment subscriptions, not that a Phase-6 batch is limited to one airport subscription.

Gate 0 requires verifying, live: that the USER-CONFIRMED 60,000-unit subscribed plan remains active, `subscription_channel`, the current billing-cycle dates/used/remaining units, refill conversion (1 unit = 1 credit), per-refill and balance caps, endpoint costs/range/rate limits, and that every REST category fits its frozen ledger.

### 27.5 Plan §4 — Sampling frame (measured, stratified)

- **Universe** = airports AeroDataBox covers, measured from the provider health/coverage inputs documented as non-paid/FREE-TIER in the Sep-2 source audit; execution must reverify their current tier/cost/version before use (`/health/services/feeds/{service}/airports` and/or `/health/services/airports/{icao}/feeds`). A repository `GET /api/v1/collection/coverage` route is a **project-local aggregator**, not a provider endpoint, and is authoritative only after its provider inputs/version/cache/hashes are evidenced.
- **Frame** = the measured provider-covered universe with explicit feed-eligibility and classification dimensions. Keep zero-yield airports. `UNCLASSIFIED`, `UNMAPPED`, and feed-ineligible rows remain visible/reportable but cannot enter the corresponding primary stratum; only a **confirmed** `coverage_failed` condition removes feed eligibility. Core experimental slots additionally require `in_frame && pre_eligible && post_eligible` plus verified tier/region.
- **Primary strata** = **traffic tier × macro-region** only (3 × 6 = 18 cells).
  Crossing more variables would explode cell count.
- **Balancing variables** (reported within strata, not crossed): network degree,
  intl/domestic, carrier diversity, time zone — from a **fixed reference snapshot
  at frame-build time**, never from the recursive current sample.
- **Tier mix per batch**: `{HUB:1, MID:2, REGIONAL:1}`.
- **Unit of prediction**: a flight-leg outcome ("departure delay of leg L at cutoff C").

### 27.6 Plan §5 — The provider-observable prediction population (S1 — the census layer)

The webhook is an **event stream, not a census**. "Flights that emitted an update
we captured" ≠ "all flights that existed". Observability selects on size, activity,
severity, tracking quality, etc. So `flight_population` records which flights
existed for each queried service interval (last-documented public FIDS tier/cost evidence from the Sep-2 audit; the current account/endpoint cost and range must be re-verified and frozen at Gate 0 before execution), snapshots
are built for **every population flight** (population ∧ horizon-eligible), and a
post-cutoff event is used for the **label only** — it never decides whether a
snapshot exists. Each feature value enters the snapshot only if
`information_available_timestamp ≤ prediction_cutoff`; unavailable optional features
are `NULL` with `feature_missing=true`. Coverage is **not one mutually-exclusive arrow state**: preserve independent dimensions such as `in_frame`, `pre_eligible`, `post_eligible`, `subscription_started`, `delivery_received`, `provider_failure`, `zero_yield_state`, `captured_in_population`, `captured_outside_population`, `snapshot_created`, and `outcome_observed`. `recently_observed_5d` and edge-discovery are separate diagnostics; `edge-discovered` is not the same as a qualifying successful direct observation.

### 27.7 Plan §6 — Data pipeline and provenance (S2–S5)

S2 normalized ingress = immutable `raw_delivery` envelope + `raw_delivery_item` flight items while lawfully retainable, with `processing_attempt` separated from raw content; legacy `adb_ingest_events` is a mixed audit/processing ledger, not the canonical raw envelope. S3 event log precedes current state (`flight_events` append-only feeds mutable `flight_state`). S4 can rebuild from raw content **only within its lawful retention horizon**; after compliant raw deletion, reproducibility must rely on permitted derived records, hashes/tombstones, manifests, parser/transform versions, and other lawfully retainable provenance. §6.1 defines the dual
PRE/AIRBORNE data contract with the canonical timestamp taxonomy:
`notification_id`, `provider_notification_generated_utc`, `delivery_attempt_seq_no`,
`delivery_attempt_utc`, `provider_state_updated_utc`, nullable
`location_reported_utc`, `http_received_at_utc`, `raw_persisted_at_utc`,
`available_at`, and `timestamp_source`. `lastUpdatedUtc` is a state-update clock,
not automatically a notification publication clock.
§6.2 is the airborne foundation: preserve the time series
(`raw_airborne_events → clean_airborne_points → flight_trajectory → flight_airborne_snapshots`). The append identity must bind delivery/item provenance + canonical `flight_instance_id` + provider location clock/observation identity; `(flight, carrier, locReportedUtc)` is a **legacy noncanonical key** and cannot identify non-location observations or safely serve as a universal event identity.

### 27.8 Plan §7 — Flight operational state and target label status

Two independent dimensions replace the invalid five-state mixture:
`flight_operational_state = scheduled/departed/arrived/canceled/canceled_uncertain/diverted/unknown`
and per-target `label_status = pending/observed/censored/missing/not_applicable`.
Before grace expiry an unavailable target is `pending`; diversion may coexist with an
observed landing; wheels-on may be observed while gate-in remains missing.

### 27.9 Plan §8 — Sampling design (LOCKED)

Default window **1 × 4 h per day** (preserves aircraft-chain continuity). UTC slots
rotate through `{00,04,08,12,16,20}` — a seeded balanced permutation, every 6-day
block uses each slot exactly once (HARD), minimize weekday×UTC imbalance (SOFT).
Calendar: 26 × 4h + 3 × 2×2h + 2 × up-to-6h = 31 ordered batch-days. Washout may make these span more than 31 calendar dates. Crossover template frozen before
treatment. Anchor pool = **5 airports, provisional `KLAX·EGLL·WSSS·SBGR·OMDB`,
finalized only after probing**. Anchor score = 40% exogenous traffic + 20% geo
diversity + 20% carrier/international diversity + 20% standardized observed yield;
capacity is a separate feasibility gate, not a score component; the formula, normalization inputs, shortlist, replacements, and source hashes must be frozen in the `preprobe_reference_freeze_record` before Stage 1. Repository implementation/freeze status is not established by this document. REGIONAL selection = normalized yield-aware draw (`m_i ∈ [0.25,1.5]`,
cap ×1.5, Σp = 1). Phase 6 starts uniformly with EMA NULL; probe data does not seed
adaptive state. Valid Phase-6 nonempty observations update EMA and deterministically
recompute `m_i`; provider failures and true-zero observations do not update EMA.
`sampling_weight` stays NULL — no auto `1/p`.

### 27.10 Plan §9 — Two-stage anchor probe (budget-capped)

1. **Stage 1:** exactly 12 shortlisted candidates across regions, including WSSS and OMAA; remaining membership and ordered replacements are BLOCKED until the final reference frame is frozen. Target 2 h
   cap-censored probes at matched time-class/weekday-class, never crossing in real time.
   Record distinct canonical flight-instances/credit, chain-links/credit, and stability based on first valid observation per flight-instance across 15-minute buckets. WSSS and OMAA use the same yield-reference protocol, not model calibration.
2. **Stage 2:** top 5 capacity-pass candidates receive cap-censored confirmation.
   If fewer than 5 pass, use the frozen replacement list; each replacement must pass
   Stage 1 and Stage 2 before entering the final five.
3. Rank capacity-pass candidates by descending score, lexical ICAO on exact ties. A failed Stage-2 candidate is replaced by the next ranked candidate only after valid Stage-2 confirmation. Final pool is **5** only after confirmation; probe spend cap is `PROBE_CAP_DAILY=500` credits per immutable `probe_budget_day_id` (not a UTC-calendar reset); default-refuse any probe that would cross UTC midnight unless its authorization and ledger explicitly split the exposure into two budget IDs.

### 27.11 Plan §10 — Weather (LOCKED)

METAR/TAF forecast-as-known-at-cutoff (a TAF issued at T−2 is never used for a
T−24 prediction); operational sources are aviationweather.gov and verified NOAA
forecast products. ERA5 is retrospective truth or an explicitly lagged historical
feature, never a current-weather fallback. The `weather_observation` and
`weather_forecast` tables/joins remain CURRENT STATUS NOT_VERIFIED.

### 27.12 Plan §11 — Credit accounting and the canary

Three quantities per batch: `notification_items_received` (webhook),
`credits_actually_consumed` (balance delta — the **authoritative denominator**),
`unique_flight_rows_created_or_updated`. Gate 3 uses R1 exclusivity: **refuse** while any foreign ACTIVE billable subscription can contaminate accounting. Binding run policy requires such subscriptions be deleted/disabled before proceeding, but an operator must not use a generic convenience/force deletion: identify ownership, record the conflict, and perform the explicitly authorized cleanup/recovery (or remain BLOCKED). Run one exactly authorized owned subscription with `maxDeliveryRetries=0`, then delete/inactivate it and apply the shared settlement rule requiring at least three consecutive equal authoritative balance reads across the frozen stability window. `C_external = B_before − B_stable` must equal `C_internal = Σ notification_items` exactly (`CANARY_TOLERANCE=0`), with failures=0, raw-before-2xx proof, >0 items, and confirmed cleanup. R2/R5: delivery failure → PAUSE + flag; the soft stop is `1900−frozen_margin`, not a permanently fixed 1,850; second-start protection remains mandatory.

### 27.13 Plan §12 — Model ladder, features, graph

Rungs −1 (naive persistence — the Month-1 primary comparison baseline) → 7 (conformal uncertainty). Month 1 yields a protected pilot endpoint, not a deployment claim; later deployment/confirmatory claims require separately preregistered evidence. Features must be as-known-at-cutoff. Graph edge taxonomy for the GNN
rung (4 is a hypothesis, not the default).

### 27.14 Plan §13 — Evaluation suite

Month 1 uses Engines A/B/C/D + R/P plus POST; Engine E is deferred until a named multi-flight event source/taxonomy is frozen. Calibration metrics, staleness, collection-regime robustness, chain-depth, and POST partition rules remain.

### 27.15 Plan §14 — Marginal value per credit

The final objective — measured per credit (using `C_actual`, never row counts).

### 27.16 Plan §15 — Code to-do — final list (R1–R7, S1–S5)

R1 exclusivity, R2 SOFT_STOP margin, R3 canary, **R4 cost-model/resource wording (API units vs Alert credits; SEND billing)**, R5 delivery-failure flag,
R6 crossover template freeze, R7 versioned manifest; S1 population layer, S2 raw
envelope, S3 event log first, S4 provenance invariant, S5 airborne time series.
**Do not infer implementation from this explanatory mirror.** Legacy pieces were previously observed, but every current code/status claim must be re-established by §1 repository intake, §12 traceability, §5 tests, and §7 readiness evidence.

### 27.17 Plan §16 — The GO gates (ALL must pass; Gate 5 alone does not authorize Phase 6)

| Gate/prerequisite | Action | Pass criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Budget partition | active plan/channel/cycle/units/refill conversion/caps and exact Alert/API identities verified; no unapproved mutation |
| P | Prepaid security/retention | content-class retention matrix + licensed references + credential/webhook/raw/expiry/backup/redaction/incident controls PASS before Gate 1 stores affected content |
| 1 | Coverage | complete contract-pinned coverage artifact with sane counts/hashes and reference readiness |
| 2 | Anchor probe | only after P, Gate 1, preprobe freeze, and safety-smoke PASS; frozen-formula Stage1/Stage2 + capacity gate + complete final-five/replacement/settlement evidence |
| 3 | Credit canary | stable settled `C_external = C_internal` exactly (`tol=0`), failures=0, raw-before-2xx, exclusive set, cleanup |
| 0.5 | Provider/content constructibility | adequately sampled dedicated pilot verifies clocks, movement semantics/actuality, T/primary-target constructibility, FIDS roles/boundaries, cadence/recovery; insufficient sample does not freeze values |
| 4 | Webhook + cap/reliability | scaled offline cap/failure tests pass; small live reconciliation/second-start check passes; runtime soft stop uses the frozen margin |
| 5 | Population-observability validation | independent population/horizon/snapshot/capture/feature/outcome counters and AIRBORNE funnel are coherent; captured-in ≤ population |

### 27.18 Plan §17 — Step-by-step runbook (what to do)

The Plan's phases 0–7 are the operational skeleton implemented in **§1** of this Log; §2 supplies the matched Replit commands and §3 is only a code-owner reference. Current order is: Phase 0 offline closure; Phase 1 Gate 0; Phase 2 `PREPAID_SECURITY_RETENTION` then Gate 1, reference/frame/pre-probe freeze, safety smoke, and Gate 2; Phase 3 Gate 3 → dedicated Gate 0.5 pilot → Gate 4; Phase 4 Gate 5 plus remaining readiness; Phase 5 FREEZE (manifest + hashed split-assignment rule); Phase 6 the solver-generated **31-batch-day** run; Phase 7 Month-1 deliverables + evaluation. §17.1 lists month-1 deliverables (validated
pipeline, snapshot pipeline, Model1-vs-persistence comparison with unknown result, info-per-credit curves, A/B/C/D/R/P results, population coverage, window-experiment pilot, POST pilot; Engine E deferred).

### 27.19 Plan §18 — Regression guards

Defines the stale rules that the scanner and human review must reject (capture-gated snapshots, one five-state outcome, true-census wording, conflated budgets, old calendar shares, auto `1/p`, row≈credit, retime→new-flight, POST-only core substitution, two-read settlement, ambiguous retry wording, and other superseded rules). It is an executable guardrail, not merely revision history.

### 27.20 Plan §19 — Sources and research foundation

The scientific bets are grounded in peer-reviewed work plus clearly labeled preprint/provider evidence. Core SDSU/SJSU research citations and current provider-contract claims were rechecked during the Sep-1 document-closure audit:
previous-leg delay propagation (Chen & Li, AIAA SciTech 2019, SDSU), delay
propagation by utilization (Zheng et al., SJSU 2021), aircraft-chain continuity (Chen & Li 2019 + Zheng/Wei/Hu 2021; **not** SJSU #4774), AIRBORNE trajectory ETA (SJSU #4774), GNN as hypothesis not default (SJSU #4935 GCN-GRU),
persistence as first gate (Chen & Li; Sternberg et al., 2017), network/propagation
taxonomy (Transportation Research Part E 2024), two-budget accounting + credit
rules (AeroDataBox "Flight Alert API Guide" 2026-01-31), weather availability
(AviationWeather.gov), in-flight ETA as a first-class state (Springer 2024; SJSU #4774).

### 27.21 Plan §20 — Explicit NOT-do list

Highlights: no V3.10+ reviews, no GNN-first, no `1/p` before measuring the
denominator, no over-claiming ("6-day slot-once ⇒ unbiased" etc.), no foreign active
subscription, no raw-event overwrites, no REST-airborne monitoring before cadence
measurement (Gate 0.5), no merging PRE and AIRBORNE sets, no post-cutoff features,
no manual Rescore/Simulate during the run, no silent weather backfill, no "one
empty observation ⇒ airport is useless".

### 27.22 Plan §21 — Final status

Architecture LOCKED; sampling architecture documented but final frame/reference freeze is pending; credit logic requires exact offline/code closure plus authorized gates; AIRBORNE preservation/decision-time semantics require implementation and Gate0.5 evidence. The run waits on offline P0/P1 closure, Gates 0–5, retention approval and FREEZE. No blanket “R1–R7/S1–S5 code complete” claim is permitted.

### 27.23 Non-binding adjudication provenance (outside executable Plan §§0–21)

The Plan file may preserve adjudication/revision history outside its executable §§0–21. That material explains how V3.9-f.8 was reached but **cannot override or add an executable requirement**. Useful provenance includes the earlier airborne/S5, dual-state, no-REST-before-measurement, coverage/zero-yield/staleness/robustness/dashboard/crossover/event-state/POST-partition adjudications.

## 28. Tables and columns — schema/reference teaching

> Active schema explanation, **not schema-introspection evidence**. §10 is the required dictionary contract; the repository audit must confirm actual tables/columns/migrations before any IMPLEMENTED/PASS claim.

The table guide below is a **contract map, not proof of live schema**. Some objects are legacy/live, some have migration files only, and some are intended. Each object must be tagged independently as `INTENDED / MIGRATION_FILE_CREATED / MIGRATION_TESTED / MIGRATION_APPLIED_LIVE / PRODUCTION_WIRED`; §10 is the current evidence-oriented dictionary.

### 28.1 Last-known `clean.adb_sampling_frame` (historical migration 0021) — current frame contract

| Column | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `icao` | airport code (primary key) |
| `tier` | `HUB` / `MID` / `REGIONAL` / `UNCLASSIFIED`; `UNCLASSIFIED` is never silently coerced into a primary sampling tier |
| `tier_source` | versioned frozen traffic/reference source or explicit `missing_reference`; historical `curated/unclassified` labels are provenance only and are not the binding Phase-6 classification rule |
| `traffic_prior` | strictly `>0` for **eligible REGIONAL** airports under the frozen traffic reference; `NULL`/not sampling-usable for `UNCLASSIFIED` until deterministically classified |
| `region` | one of the 6 frozen macro-regions or explicit `UNMAPPED`; `UNMAPPED` remains outside primary tier×region sampling |
| `feed_schedule` / `feed_live` / `feed_adsb` | measured provider coverage/feed flags with retrieval timestamp/source/hash; these flags do not by themselves supply a traffic tier or region |
| `pre_eligible` | PRE provider/feed eligibility under the frozen provider rule; primary sampling additionally requires verified tier/region and all other binding eligibility checks |
| `post_eligible` | AIRBORNE provider/feed eligibility under the frozen provider-native denominator rule; primary sampling additionally requires verified tier/region and all other binding eligibility checks |
| `in_frame` | membership in the frozen measured provider-covered frame under the verified coverage/feed rule. `UNCLASSIFIED`/`UNMAPPED` may remain visible/in-frame but cannot enter the corresponding primary tier×region stratum; only confirmed `coverage_failed` removes feed eligibility. Core sampling additionally requires verified tier+region and `pre_eligible && post_eligible` |
| `built_at` | when the frame artifact was written; the final frame must also carry/source a reproducible rule/reference hash |

**Historical provenance only:** the 2026-08-18 builder wrote **4,320 rows = 267 curated + 4,053 legacy-unclassified-coerced-to-REGIONAL**, with pre 3,337 / post 2,264 / both 1,281 and reported 18/18 cells. Those counts/classifications are **not binding current frame facts** and must be rebuilt under Plan §§4.1–4.2 before FREEZE.

### 28.2 Last-known `clean.adb_anchor_probe` (historical migration 0023) — current probe-evidence contract

One row per probe observation: one airport, one stage, one window. `probe_id` is
just the row's serial number (the database's auto-increment counter, like a receipt
number). `stage` is which probe round it was — **1** = the shortlist measurement
(2 h), **2** = the confirmation measurement (4 h) for the top picks. `status` historically used values such as `probing`, `completed`, `failed`, and `abandoned`; the current probe implementation must verify/normalize its state machine and owned-subscription cleanup semantics. The scoring inputs are `rows_per_hour` (capacity gate), confirmed unique-flight-instances/credit, verified tail-chain-links/credit, and exposure-correct stability; current ranking is produced only by the repository-mapped `v39:probe` services under §9.

| Column | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `probe_id` | primary key |
| `stage` | 1 (shortlist, 2 h) or 2 (confirmation, 4 h) |
| `icao`, `region` | airport and macro-region |
| `window_start` / `window_end` / `window_hours` | the live window (UTC) |
| `subscription_id` | the AeroDataBox subscription used |
| `balance_before` / `balance_after` | credit balance at window edges |
| `credits_spent` | final `C_external` from authoritative **settled** provider balance delta under the shared ≥3-equal-read stability rule; a single immediate after-read is not final spend |
| `rows_delivered` | rows attributed to this subscription in the window |
| `unique_flights` | confirmed distinct physical `flight_instance_id` values observed during actual exposure; retries/updates/codeshare labels do not create extra flights; preserve identity ambiguity bounds |
| `tail_chain_links` | aircraft-rotation chain links |
| `rows_per_hour` | station capacity — the feasibility GATE, not a score component |
| `unique_flights_per_credit` | yield component 1, standardized against valid WSSS primary reference or permitted OMAA fallback |
| `tail_chain_links_per_credit` | yield component 2 |
| `stability` | 1/(1+CV) of 15-min bucket counts |
| `recorded_at` | insert time |

The historical schema used a uniqueness rule around `(icao, stage, window_start)`; repository intake/tests must verify the current attempt identity is sufficient to preserve retries/replacements without collision. File/schema existence is not inferred here.

### 28.3 Last-known `clean.flight_data_pre_post` (historical migration 0010) — legacy flattened compatibility table

Last-known legacy code stored one flattened row per flight/delivery here. **It is not the binding raw source of truth.** The f.8 contract requires immutable `raw_delivery` + `raw_delivery_item` persistence before successful 2xx, with semantic/event/trajectory layers derived from that raw provenance. Keep this table only for compatibility/diagnostics if repository intake proves it still exists; never reconstruct authoritative raw history from it. The row is a mix of **what AeroDataBox sends** and **what we
stamp ourselves**:

**Columns that come FROM AeroDataBox (the webhook payload):**

| Column group | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `flight_number`, `carrier_icao/iata/name`, `call_sign` | identity of the flight |
| `status`, `status_code`, `codeshare_status` | current status (numeric enum → name) |
| `last_updated_utc` | when the provider last updated this flight |
| `dep_*` / `arr_*` (airport, scheduled/revised/runway UTC, terminal, gate) | departure / arrival plan |
| `aircraft_reg`, `aircraft_mode_s`, `aircraft_model` | the physical aircraft |
| `loc_*` (lat/lon/altitude/speed/heading/live location) | live ADS-B position (POST) |
| `subscription_*`, `subject_*`, `subscriber_*`, `credits_remaining` | the subscription + balance blocks of the payload |
| `payload_json` | **the whole raw flight item**, kept verbatim only while lawfully retained under Plan §10.2; raw hash/non-content audit metadata may survive only as permitted |
| `gcd_km`, `great_circle_distance` | route distance |

**Columns WE stamp (our server, at receive time):**

| Column group | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `received_at` | our clock when the POST landed |
| `dedup_key` | **LEGACY current-state key only:** historical SHA-256 `(flight, carrier,lastUpdatedUtc)` behavior may exist in a mutable convenience table. It is **NOT** the canonical identity for `raw_delivery`, `raw_delivery_item`, semantic observations, or trajectory points; Plan §6 four-identity contract governs those append-only layers |
| `data_stage`, `has_live_location` | PRE vs POST decided by us from the payload's location/status |
| `sampling_batch_id`, `airport_tier` | which batch/tier this delivery belonged to |
| `is_randomized` | binding semantic is `true` for randomized REGIONAL selections and `false` for deterministic HUB/MID fills; current schema nullability/producer wiring must be reverified rather than inferred from the historical 08-19 fix |
| `airport_layer_design_probability` | the realized conditional design probability (REGIONAL draws only) |
| `planned_share` | the HUB/MID allocation share (deterministic fills only) |
| `collection_window_start/end`, `random_seed` | which window/seed produced this row |
| `sampling_weight` | stays NULL — the Plan forbids auto `1/p` (Plan §8; Log §27.9) |

**Which other tables are derived from this one?** Precisely:

- **`clean.adb_ingest_events`** — historically a mixed per-delivery audit/processing log written alongside the legacy path. It is **not automatically the current `C_internal` authority**; current reconciliation must map immutable raw-delivery/item/attempt ownership and sum notification items over the owned SEND scope.
- **`clean.flight_events` / `raw_airborne_events` / `clean_airborne_points` /
  `flight_trajectory` / `flight_airborne_snapshots`** — derived *from the same
  payload* at receive time (appended from the extracted rows), never re-derived
  from `flight_data_pre_post` later. They keep every airborne observation so
  trajectories are reconstructable (Plan §6.2).
- **`clean.adb_anchor_probe`** — NOT derived from the table's rows as a transform;
  it is a *measurement log* that the historical probe script filled by **counting** legacy rows for a subscription inside a window. That is provenance only; the current probe-evidence contract is Plan §9 plus Log §§3.2.1 and 20.
- **`clean.adb_sampling_frame`** — NOT derived from collected webhook outcomes; it is built from the current provider-coverage artifact plus frozen reference inputs. Provider endpoint tier/cost/version must be reverified; do not assume “free measurement.”

Note: `flight_data_pre_post` is kept for compatibility but is **not** the only
true data table. The S-layers above (`flight_population`, `flight_events`,
`flight_snapshots`, `flight_outcomes`, `raw_airborne_events`,
`clean_airborne_points`, `flight_trajectory`, `flight_airborne_snapshots`,
`historical_feature_store`, `weather_observation`, `weather_forecast`) are all
first-class research tables per Plan §2/§6 — see §10 data dictionary.

### 28.4 Last-known `adb_collection_batches` / `adb_collection_subs` / `adb_collection_meta` (historical migration 0012)

- `adb_collection_batches` — one row per collection window: batch id, seed, window
  start/end, credit budget, tier mix, `balance_before/after`,
  `credits_consumed_actual` vs `credits_consumed_internal`,
  `notification_items_received`, rows stored/inserted/updated,
  `delivery_failures`, `reconciliation_status`.
- `adb_collection_subs` — the airport subscriptions that make up a batch, each
  stamped with `batch_id`, `icao`, `tier`, sampling metadata (design probability /
  planned share), and `is_randomized`.
- `adb_collection_meta` — key/value store for rotation state (`batch_seq`,
  `last_anchor`, `run_template`, `manifest`, anchor-pool lock state).

### 28.5 Legacy `clean.adb_ingest_events` (historical migration 0017) — inspection/audit ledger

Historically this mixed ledger recorded one row per webhook delivery with `subscription_id`, `batch_id`, `received_at`, `notification_items`, row counts, `credits_remaining`, `delivery_failure`, and `error`. For current V3.9-f.8, treat it as a **legacy audit source** unless repository intake proves it is correctly backed by/linked to immutable `raw_delivery`/`raw_delivery_item` and the owned SEND scope. Gate-3 `C_internal` is the sum of notification items from the verified immutable owned delivery/item ledger, not a table name by fiat.

### 28.6 S-layer contract with last-known historical migrations 0019–0020

- `flight_population` — S1 **append-only population-observation layer**: provider-observable FIDS appearances/query provenance retained by retrieval; horizon membership is derived from the latest qualifying observation with `available_at≤prediction_cutoff_utc`. Slow/later responses never backfill an earlier cutoff.
- `flight_events` — append-only semantic-observation log using the binding versioned identity over canonical `flight_instance_id`, event type/phase, available provider state/location clock(s), and raw-item provenance. A location-style tuple alone is legacy/noncanonical and cannot key non-location observations (S3/S4).
- `raw_airborne_events` — airborne (POST + live location) points with all fields
  preserved incl. `loc_reported_utc`.
- `clean_airborne_points` / `flight_trajectory` / `flight_airborne_snapshots` —
  the S5 trajectory pipeline feeding the POST model.

<a id="sec-29"></a>
## 29. Glossary of technical terms

> Active glossary. If an old example embeds a dated balance, filename, or prior-run value, treat that example as illustrative/provenance. **Current execution authority is §§0–7; engineering details are §§8–26.** This glossary cannot override either.

Alphabetical, one plain paragraph each. Terms cite the **binding Plan section** and/or the **current Log section** explicitly. Bare legacy section numbers from earlier Log layouts are not used as navigation.

- **Anchor** — one of the 5 airports that drive the HUB share of every batch. The
  pool `KLAX·EGLL·WSSS·SBGR·OMDB` is **provisional until the probe proves it**.
- **Anchor score** — the frozen 0.4/0.2/0.2/0.2 weighted score used to lock the 5
  (Plan §§4.5 and 9.2; Log §3.2.8). Capacity is a gate, not a component.
- **Yield-reference** — WSSS and OMAA are the required reference airports under the binding protocol; any old rows/hour values are historical observations only. Current reference components are measured with the identical frozen cap-censored protocol before ranking (Plan §9.2; Log §3.2.1). They are marked `isYieldReference` in the shortlist.
  "Baseline" = the yardstick; "calibration" = the reference is measured by us with
  the identical protocol, not assumed.
- **Canary** — a tiny, controlled live test (a "canary in a coal mine") using a **small pre-authorized Alert-credit ceiling**. Cost is not guaranteed to be one credit because billing is per flight item per provider SEND. The historical failed run happened to cost 1 credit. It subscribes to one airport for a short frozen window, then reconciles the required ledgers and must PASS
  (Log §§1.8.1 and 2.11). The official credit canary uses the repository-mapped production service; historical `scripts/credit_canary.ts` / `npm run canary` are path/alias provenance. The current operator interface is `v39:gate3:canary` after repository mapping and authorization.
- **Capacity / feasibility gate** — PASS/FAIL: does the airport physically deliver
  enough data (our code: rows/h ≥ 60)? A gate is a yes/no that disqualifies an
  airport outright; it is **never** added to the score (Plan §9.2; Log §3.2.8).
- **Legacy curated catalog** — the historical 276-airport list (30 HUB + 89 MID + 157 REGIONAL) used in earlier development. It is provenance/reference material, **not the binding primary frame and not a rule for classifying all other airports**. Historical intersections such as 267/276 and the legacy 4,320-row frame must not be reused as current Gate-1 targets; see Plan §§4.1–4.4 and Log §27.5.
- **Chain links / tail chain** — for each aircraft tail, N−1 rotation links over N
  observed legs; a measure of delay-propagation material (Plan §§7.5 and 9.2; Log §3.2.8).
- **Conditional probability** — probability given a condition. For the binding REGIONAL draw, the condition includes the selected tier×region cell, current eligible set, and deterministic pre-draw adaptive/coverage state; `p_i=draw_score_i/Σdraw_score` and only reduces to `1/n` when all surviving scores are equal. At the REGIONAL
  draw, `p_i` is conditional on the pool that *survived filtering that day*: the
  denominator is the airports still standing at the moment of the draw, not the
  whole frame (Plan §8.2).
- **Coverage** — which airports the pinned AeroDataBox coverage/feed interfaces report as supported at a dated measurement. The relevant provider health/coverage inputs are currently documented as FREE TIER, but execution re-verifies provider contract/cost and records the retrieval/source hash.
- **Credit** — Flight-Alert balance unit; 1 credit per flight item per delivery
  attempt, deducted on SEND; refill 1 unit → 1 credit.
- **Crossover** — the window-shape experiment (4h vs 2×2h vs up-to-6h); template frozen
  before treatment so treatment never depends on post-freeze observations.
- **Cutoff** — the time boundary a prediction uses; features must be ≤ cutoff; the
  POST event supplies only the label.
- **data_stage PRE|POST** — which prediction state a payload serves; raw events
  carry `data_stage`, never `prediction_state` (that is derived on snapshots).
- **Dedup (legacy current-state behavior, NON-CANONICAL for research provenance)** — a legacy `dedup_key` may upsert a mutable convenience/current-state table, but raw deliveries/items and semantic observations are append-only identities under Plan §6. A `(flight, carrier, lastUpdatedUtc)` key must never deduplicate raw/event/trajectory provenance.
- **Design probability** — the realized probability of a REGIONAL pick
  (`airport_layer_design_probability`), stamped per row (Plan §8.2).
- **Design ceiling** — 57,900 credits is the maximum possible Phase-6 design ceiling; the actual frozen `phase6_alert_spend_ceiling` may be lower after pre-run/floor/margin accounting.
- **Engine (A–E + R + P)** — the evaluation-suite baseline engines for the later ML
  phase; each answers one generalization question (unseen airport / region / tail /
  route, disruption stress, population audit). NOT the probe (Plan §13; Log §27.14).
- **Evaluation suite** — the plan's ML testing design (plan §13): a family of
  engines A–E + R + P + Model POST, each with its own leakage-blocking split, run
  on the data we collect now (Plan §13; Log §27.14).
- **Exclusivity (R1)** — no foreign/non-experimental **billable** subscription may coexist with an authorized paid run/accounting scope. Before a run, ownership-aware authorized cleanup must delete/disable any such ACTIVE subscription; the current run may contain its intended experiment-owned batch-linked subscriptions.
- **FIDS** — AeroDataBox airport flight-information/schedule data used for the provider-observable population layer. **Do not hard-code a current unit cost or range here**: Gate 0 verifies the live account/endpoint cost/range and the materialized seven-category REST ledger proves the frozen budget.
- **Frame** — the measured provider-covered airport set with explicit feed-eligibility and classification dimensions. Zero-yield airports remain; `UNCLASSIFIED`/`UNMAPPED` remain visible but cannot enter the corresponding primary stratum, and only confirmed `coverage_failed` removes feed eligibility. Core experimental selection then requires the Plan §4.4 dual-eligibility/tier/region rules.
- **Frozen** — fixed at the Plan-defined freeze point and then hash-recorded; some values are pre-probe exogenous freezes, while designated MEASURE→FREEZE values are chosen only from the allowed gate evidence. Nothing is tuned from protected Phase-6 outcomes.
- **Gate** — a fail-closed checkpoint. The current sequence is offline closure → Gate 0 → security/retention prerequisite → Gate 1 → pre-probe freeze → authorized safety smoke → Gate 2 → Gate 3 → Gate 0.5 → Gate 4 → Gate 5 → remaining history/weather/calendar/manifest readiness → FREEZE → separate Phase-6 authorization. Also the PASS/FAIL *feasibility* kind (capacity gate, Plan §9.2):
  a yes/no that never enters a score.
- **HUB / MID / REGIONAL** — the three binding traffic-tier labels. The current tier assignment comes from the frozen 12-month external traffic metric/cut rule; the old curated 30/89/157 counts are historical only. Missing traffic reference is `UNCLASSIFIED`. The binding batch slot mix is `{HUB:1,MID:2,REGIONAL:1}`.
- **Inclusion probability (and the `1/p` weight)** — the chance that an item lands
  in the sample. Weighting rows by `1/p` is only valid when `p` is a
  *flight-inclusion* probability; our draw is over airports, so we do NOT stamp
  `1/p` — the current Plan §8 rule (Log §27.9).
- **isYieldReference** — the flag on WSSS/OMAA marking them as yield-references.
- **Macro-region** — one of our 6 documented geographic regions (North America,
  Europe, Asia-Pacific, Gulf/Africa, South America, Oceania). The 6 in "3 tiers ×
  6 regions" (Plan §4.2). Note: PART 1 defines
  primary strata as "traffic tier × macro-region" and Plan §4.2 explicitly enumerates the six regions plus the country/override mapping rules; the 6-region set is our documented choice, drawn from the plan's
  "Priority anchor regions" list.
- **Manifest** — the versioned, auditable snapshot (frame version, config, seeds,
  account plan) written at batch start (R7).
- **Population** — flights that existed at a cutoff per the provider-observable
  S1 layer (a census-like denominator, honestly labeled; Plan §§2 and 5).
- **post_eligible / pre_eligible** — an airport can serve the POST (live/ADS-B)
  layer, the PRE (schedule/FIDS) layer, or both.
- **Probe** — a standardized, budget-capped live measurement of an airport
  (subscribe → collect a 2 h/4 h window → delete → count) that produces the yield
  components; the basis for locking the anchor pool (Plan §§2 and 9). The current operator interfaces are in Log §2.20; historical `scripts/anchor_probe.ts` is only an inspection target in Log §3.2.1. "Probing" is the act of running these
  measurements.
- **Prediction state** — PRE (before departure) or AIRBORNE/POST (in flight);
  never merged into one modeling set.
- **R1–R7 requirement codes** — the plan's numbered requirements that came from the
  9 review rounds: R1 exclusivity, R2 SOFT_STOP margin, R3 credit canary, R5
  delivery-failure flag, R7 versioned manifest (Plan §15).
- **Reconciliation** — compare authoritative settled external Alert balance delta with the corresponding internal notification-item accounting. The isolated Gate-3 canary requires **exact equality (`tol=0`)**. Any later production reconciliation tolerance is a separate MEASURE→FREEZE setting and may not weaken Gate 3.
- **Reserve** — the protected 1,000-credit **budget floor** the controller refuses to spend below; “permanent” here is not a data-retention term.
- **REST/API-unit ledger** — the separately frozen seven-category API-unit budget for FIDS/validation/outcome/history/diagnostic work; it is separate from Alert credits. Do not use the old “~1,000 REST line” shorthand as a binding cap.
- **S1–S5 layer codes** — the plan's data-pipeline layer numbers: S1 population
  census, S2/S3/S4 per-flight event/observation logs, S5 airborne trajectory
  points (Plan §§2 and 6). The current table/data-dictionary references live in Log §§10 and 28.
- **Sampling frame** — see Frame.
- **Seed** — an integer that initializes a pseudo-random generator so the
  "random" sequence is reproducible: same seed → same sequence → replayable,
  auditable draws and UTC-slot rotation (Plan §8.1).
- **Settlement** — apply the shared settlement procedure: after the frozen initial wait, require at least three consecutive equal authoritative balance reads (or a larger frozen count) spanning the stability window; any change resets the count; timeout blocks further paid work.
- **Shortlist** — exactly 12 dual-eligible HUB-tier candidates **across regions**, including WSSS and OMAA, frozen with ordered replacements in the `preprobe_reference_freeze_record` before Stage 1. The binding Plan imposes **no fixed per-region quota** on those 12.
- **SOFT_STOP / HARD_CAP** — 1,900 is the hard budget-day ceiling; the soft stop is `1,900−frozen_margin`. The historical 50/1,850 arithmetic is a proposal/test fixture until valid evidence freezes the applicable margin; settled overshoot → MISMATCH.
- **Stability** — 1/(1+CV) of per-15-min bucket counts inside a probe window
  (Plan §9.2).
- **Standardized measurement** — raw anchor-yield components scaled to [0,1] against WSSS as primary reference, with OMAA used only under the pre-frozen fallback rule when the corresponding WSSS reference is invalid; if neither is valid, the component is `NOT_SCORABLE` (Plan §9.2).
- **Strata / stratified** — non-overlapping groups (tier × region = 18 cells)
  within which we sample (Plan §§4.2 and 8). “18/18 non-empty” is only a diagnostic description if observed; it is **not** a binding PASS target and empty/UNCLASSIFIED/UNMAPPED cells must be reported honestly rather than forced full.
- **Subscription** — an AeroDataBox webhook subscription (subject type +
  subject id, e.g. `FlightByAirportIcao` + KLAX) that pushes flight alerts to our webhook; billing is per **flight item on provider SEND**, not successful delivery.
- **Tier × macro-region** — the primary strata: 3 tiers crossed with 6 regions =
  18 cells (Plan §4.2).
- **Universe** — everything AeroDataBox covers (measured) — Plan §4.1.
- **UTC** — Coordinated Universal Time (≈ GMT): the global time standard used for
  every timestamp, so comparisons across time zones are apples-to-apples (Plan §8.1).
- **UTC slot** — the 4 h block of a collection window chosen from
  {00,04,08,12,16,20} with a seeded balanced rotation (Plan §§2 and 8.1).
- **Webhook** — the push mechanism: AeroDataBox POSTs notifications to
  `/api/v1/webhooks/aerodatabox[/secret]`; the current ingress contract is authenticate/minimally validate → durably persist the normalized raw envelope/items → successful 2xx → semantic processing. `maxDeliveryRetries=0` is the project setting; correctness/durability is never weakened merely to avoid a possible retry charge.
- **WSSS / OMAA** — Singapore Changi and Abu Dhabi Zayed; the required yield-reference airports. Any old rows/hour numbers are dated provenance only. Current reference components are re-measured by us under the same frozen target-2h cap-censored protocol as every Stage-1 candidate (Plan §9.2; Log §3.2.1).
- **Yield score** — the average of three standardized components
  (uf/credit, chain/credit, stability) vs the valid WSSS primary reference or permitted OMAA fallback (Plan §9.2).
- **Zero-yield** — an airport that produced no observations; one empty is never
  evidence of uselessness (once/repeated/persistent triage); only coverage-failed
  airports leave the frame.

---

### 29.1 A30 glossary gap-fill — plain / formal / V3.9 § / code / aviation example

| Term | Plain meaning | Formal meaning | V3.9 § | Code | Aviation example |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| population | provider-observable flight membership supported by an append-only pre-cutoff FIDS observation | latest qualifying observation with `available_at≤prediction_cutoff`; no future backfill | §5 | `flight_population` | LAX service interval as known by T−6 |
| sampling frame | measured provider-covered airports retained under the frozen coverage/feed taxonomy; classification dimensions remain explicit | measured provider-covered universe with explicit feed eligibility; `UNCLASSIFIED`/`UNMAPPED` remain visible but cannot enter the corresponding primary stratum; only confirmed `coverage_failed` removes feed eligibility | §4 | `clean.adb_sampling_frame` (schema/name must be reverified) | historical provisional frame had 4,320 rows; current frame must be rebuilt from current coverage/reference evidence |
| stratum | non-overlapping verified group | `tier∈{HUB,MID,REGIONAL} × region∈6` gives 18 **possible** primary cells; `UNCLASSIFIED/UNMAPPED` stay outside primary strata and are reported | §4 | `strata` | e.g. HUB×NA; no fixed cell count is assumed |
| conditional probability | probability given state before draw | `p_i = draw_score_i / Σ_{j∈E_t} draw_score_j`, conditional on the eligible tier×region pool and pre-draw adaptive/coverage state | §8 | `airport_layer_design_probability` owner must be mapped | equal eligible draw scores reduce to `1/n`; unequal scores do not |
| inclusion probability | probability that a flight-level analytic row enters the realized dataset under the complete design | **not identified by the airport draw probability alone in current V3.9**; no exact product formula is claimed until a valid full flight-inclusion design is defined | §8 | `sampling_weight=NULL` | `1/p_airport` is not a valid flight weight |
| censoring | window ended but outcome not yet known, wait grace | `grace_minutes` P95+margin | §6.2 §7.4 | `flight_outcomes` | no runway yet |
| missingness | independent population/snapshot/capture/label dimensions | snapshot existence is population-defined; capture is parallel | §5/§7 | `flight_population` + snapshots/events | PRE population row can have no webhook and still get a snapshot |
| leakage | future info in feature | `available_at > cutoff` but `event ≤ cutoff` | §6.1 | `historicalFeatureStore` | 14:07 leak into 14:05 |
| randomization | frozen seed/state → replayable assignment/draw | deterministic function of frozen seed plus the eligible pre-draw state; exact production owner must be mapped | §8 | mapped scheduler/sampler; legacy `drawWithoutReplacement` is only an inspection target | same frozen inputs must replay the same assignment |
| blocking | define comparable groups before the randomized/seeded assignment | primary strata are verified traffic tier × macro-region; empty/unverified cells remain explicit and are never silently substituted | §8 | mapped frame builder + scheduler; legacy `buildStratifiedFrame` is only an inspection target | HUB×Europe and REGIONAL×Oceania are different possible cells |
| crossover | five matched pairs with frozen contrast, then randomized order within pair | unique `crossover_group_id`; 3×(4h vs 2×2h), 2×(4h vs up-to-6h) | §8.7 | scheduler | same airport set, ≥24h washout |
| bootstrapping | resample calendar-day blocks for Month-1 **pilot uncertainty** | 95% block-bootstrap interval; six test days are not treated as confirmatory validation | §13 | `block_bootstrap` | protected days 26–31 |
| rolling-origin | chronological development folds | exact intervals frozen before first model fit on days 1–25 only; protected test 26–31 excluded | §13.4 | evaluation | early pilot |
| calibration (prob) | reliability `ECE` | `Σ abs(acc-conf)·p` | §13.1 | `ECE 15bins` | 0 perfect |
| Brier | mean squared prob error | `1/N Σ(p-y)^2` | §13.1 | `Brier >15` | 0 perfect |
| prediction interval | range covering 90% truth | `coverage + width` | §13.5 | quantile 90% | 90% coverage |
| causal vs associational | intervention vs correlation | `MV under randomized intervention` | §14 | `MV_data` | not universal causal |
| marginal value | ΔM per credit | `ΔM/Δcredits`, signed | §14 | `info-per-credit` | no assumed direction; nonrandom additions observational |
